With approximately 3k synthetic Text-to-SQL question-database pairs for RL training, ACE-SQL achieves 65.3% greedy execution accuracy on BIRD Dev while using 0.93k output tokens per query. The repository is available at https://github.com/xbchen1/ACE-SQL.

</details>


### [64] [Better Literary Translation: A Multi-Aspect Data Generation and LLM Training Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05924)
*Zhihao Lin,Ziqi Zhu,Hao Huang,Guanghui Wang,Peiyang He*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对文学翻译提出了一种多维度迭代优化框架LitMT，通过高质量参考数据生成与GRPO强化学习训练，实现了媲美顶尖商业模型（Claude Sonnet 4.5）的翻译性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决文学翻译中高质量标注数据稀缺的问题，以及如何在翻译中平衡语言流畅性与文学表现力。

Method: 提出了一种多维度迭代优化框架，利用针对不同质量维度的专业LLM翻译器生成参考数据，并结合监督微调（SFT）和强化学习（GRPO算法，配合显式奖励模型）进行训练。

Result: 生成的高质量参考数据使SFT性能提升8.65 CEA100点；GRPO训练进一步带来1.51点提升；LitMT-8B/14B在MetaphorTrans基准测试上达到67.25/69.07 CEA100，并展现了良好的跨领域泛化能力。

Conclusion: LitMT模型在文学翻译任务上表现优异，其性能可媲美Claude Sonnet 4.5；且证明了分阶段训练和GRPO在文学翻译任务中优于DPO。

Abstract: Literary translation poses unique challenges due to the scarcity of high-quality annotated data and the need to balance expression fluency with literary effect. We present a multi-aspect iterative refinement framework that generates high-quality translation references and preference data through specialized LLM translators, each targeting a distinct quality dimension. We leverage the generated data for supervised fine-tuning and reinforcement learning. Experiments show that our generated references outperform the original ground truth for SFT by 8.65 CEA100 points. For reinforcement learning, we find that DPO leads to performance degradation in this setting, while leveraging an explicit reward model for GRPO yields an additional 1.51 point improvement. We attribute this to the stability of two-stage training and GRPO's online exploration capability. Our resulting models, LitMT-8B and LitMT-14B, achieve 67.25 and 69.07 CEA100 respectively on the MetaphorTrans English-to-Chinese literary translation benchmark, competitive with Claude Sonnet 4.5 at 68.43, and demonstrate strong generalization to out-of-domain literary work (i.e., O. Henry).

</details>


### [65] [Epistemic Injustice in Language Models: An Audit of Pretraining Filters and Guardrails](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05936)
*Marco Antonio Stranisci,A Pranav,Rossana Damiano,Christian Hardmeier,Anne Lauscher*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究发现大模型的内容审核系统存在深层缺陷：盲目依赖关键词导致边缘群体内容被过度过滤（认识论抹除），而真实的有害信息却被漏过，且系统逻辑与人类的价值观严重不一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大语言模型的过滤与审核机制不仅未能有效清理有害内容，反而对边缘群体造成了过度审查，导致知识体系中的某些声音被系统性地“抹除”。

Method: 对4种预训练过滤器和3种推理阶段护栏进行审计。使用包含性别和地区来源标注的Common Crawl句子，以及500条人工标注的子集进行对照分析，评估系统决策与词表匹配度及人群属性之间的关联。

Result: 现有过滤系统高度依赖词表（blocklist），往往漏掉真实的隐私或仇恨言论；同时，跨性别者、女性及中美洲移民等边缘群体的内容被显著过度标记。人工评估显示，人类认为被系统拦截的内容中有88.5%至91.3%的内容应当被保留。

Conclusion: 当前的预训练过滤与推理阶段护栏机制存在“认识论抹除”（epistemic erasure）问题，盲目依赖词表过滤导致边缘群体内容被过度审查，同时却未能有效识别有害信息，系统判定与人类判断存在显著脱节。

Abstract: Modern language models rely on pretraining filters to remove undesirable content from training corpora and inference-time guardrails to suppress undesirable outputs during deployment. In this paper, we examine how these filtering and moderation decisions produce forms of epistemic erasure and reveal tensions both across automated systems and between these systems and human judgment. We audit four pretraining filters and three inference-time guardrails on Common Crawl sentences containing gender and regional-origin mentions, together with a manually annotated subset of 500 sentences. Our analysis shows that filtering and guardrail decisions are strongly associated with blocklist-based lexical cues, while frequently failing to flag content containing private information or explicit hate speech. At the same time, marginalized groups, particularly transgender people, women, and Central Americans, are significantly over-flagged across systems. Human annotators, by contrast, would retain 88.5\% of filter-flagged and 91.3\% of guardrail-flagged content, often recognizing representational harms arising from tensions of content removal that current systems fail to capture. Taken together, our findings document a form of epistemic erasure in which mentions of marginalized groups are disproportionately removed before pretraining and additionally suppressed again at inference time.

</details>


### [66] [Large Language Models are Perplexed by some Political Parties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05937)
*Paul Lerner,François Yvon*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究通过困惑度指标揭示了LLMs在处理不同政治倾向文本时存在显著的不均衡性，并指出这种偏见根植于预训练阶段，难以通过指令微调修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管大型语言模型（LLMs）应用广泛，但其政治公平性问题尚未得到充分研究，且目前缺乏量化评估政治偏见的方法。

Method: 使用“困惑度（Perplexity）”作为评估指标，假设一个公平的模型应平等地对待不同政治团体的文本概率。实验覆盖了10个LLM模型、3个数据集以及37种语言。

Result: 研究发现，LLMs对极右翼和民族主义政党文本的困惑度普遍高于社会民主党派，且这种困惑度与下游翻译任务的质量指标高度相关。无论模型是基础版还是经过指令微调的版本，这种政治偏向性特征高度一致。

Conclusion: LLMs的政治公平性主要源于预训练阶段，且受指令微调的影响极小。目前的LLMs在处理极右翼和民族主义政党文本时表现出更高的困惑度（Perplexity），显示出对不同政治立场的处理存在不均衡性。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly used, including in political applications, but their political fairness has been little studied. We assess it using perplexity, posing that a fair model should give equal probability to all political groups. However, we find, across ten LLMs and three datasets covering 37 languages, that LLMs are more perplexed by the texts of far right and nationalist parties than of social-democratic parties. We find this to be consistent with previous work on translation fairness, to the point that perplexity correlates with downstream translation metrics. Our method is applicable to both base LLMs as well as their instruction-tuned counterpart, and we find that both are highly correlated, suggesting that the political fairness of LLMs stems from their pretraining, and is hardly affected by instruction-tuning.

</details>


### [67] [Measuring the sensitivity of LLM-based structured extraction to prompt, model, and schema choices in clinical discharge summaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05970)
*Martin Murin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文评估了大型语言模型在临床文本提取任务中的稳定性，提出了一种无需人工标注的审计方法，发现提取结果对提示词变体和模型选择极其敏感，尤其是提示词模式导致的“缺失 vs 未记录”歧义及模型本身对分类决策的主导作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管大型语言模型在大规模临床文本结构化提取中应用广泛，但目前缺乏对其配置细节（如提示词模式、模型选择）敏感性的深入了解，且现有评估往往依赖昂贵的人工标注，亟需一种无标注的可扩展审计方法。

Method: 该研究在固定提取任务下，采用变量控制法（控制变量法），通过改变提示词变体（3种）和模型规模（2种）进行对比实验。在MIMIC-IV v3.1出院小结数据集上，利用Cohen's kappa衡量跨提示词一致性，并通过成对比较法验证模型选择的影响，以及通过将三分类标签合并为二分类来分析模式的影响。

Result: 1. 在三分类临床标志提取中，模型的一致性（Kappa约为0.68-0.69）受提示词变体高度影响，这种冲突主要源于模型对“不存在”与“未提及”的判定差异。2. 将标签简化为二进制可消除大部分分歧。3. 在入院原因多分类任务中，更改模型会导致近半数记录的标签发生改变，而更改提示词措辞的影响较小（约1/8）；大模型能显著减少对“其他”类别的依赖。

Conclusion: 该研究提出了一种无需人工标注的评估方法，揭示了临床医疗文书结构化提取中，提取结果对提示词（Prompt）和模型选择的高度敏感性。研究发现，“缺失”与“未记录”之间的歧义是导致不一致的主要原因。在多分类任务中，模型选择对结果的影响远大于提示词措辞的影响。该方法为大规模临床部署中的提取可重复性审计提供了参考。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used for structured extraction from clinical free-text notes, but the sensitivity of their output to upstream configuration choices is less understood than their accuracy on fixed benchmarks. This work measures that sensitivity without human-annotated ground truth, by holding the extraction task fixed and varying one choice at a time. The fixed schema comprises 17 clinical documentation flags on a three-way yes/no/not_documented value set and a 47-tag vocabulary for the primary admission reason. Three prompt variants expressing this schema were each run at two model sizes on MIMIC-IV v3.1 discharge summaries. Cross-prompt agreement was measured by Cohen's kappa on ICD-stratified subsets. A paired same-note comparison isolated the effect of model choice, and a post-hoc collapse of the three-way flags to binary tested the schema's contribution to disagreement. On the three-way flags, the two models reach the same pooled cross-prompt agreement (median kappa 0.69 and 0.68); the larger model raises agreement on some fields and lowers it on others, a redistribution rather than the absence of an effect. Collapsing the schema to binary dissolves most of the cross-prompt disagreement, locating it on the absence-versus-silence distinction rather than on whether the finding is present. On the multi-class admission categorization, changing the model reassigns the dominant tag on close to half of all notes while changing the prompt phrasing reassigns it on roughly one in eight, and the larger model places far less mass on residual catch-all categories (44% to 26%). These patterns indicate a schema-imposed source of disagreement concentrated on the absence-versus-silence axis and a dominance of model over prompt phrasing on multi-class categorization, identified by a reusable methodology for auditing extraction reproducibility on a population-scale deployment.

</details>


### [68] [Beyond Alignment: Value Diversity as a Collective Property in Multicultural Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05985)
*Shaoyang Xu,Jingshen Zhang,Long P. Hoang,Jinyuan Li,Wenxuan Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对多文化多智能体系统，研究者提出“价值多样性”评估框架，发现当前LLM智能体社会存在共识驱动导致的同质化倾向，多样性水平显著低于人类社会。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究仅关注“价值对齐”（Value Alignment），即单个智能体与特定文化的匹配度，忽略了多智能体系统作为一个整体是否能有效保持其所代表的文化多元性。

Method: 定义“价值多样性”为跨文化智能体在特定价值问卷中响应的不相似度；使用《世界价值观调查》（World Values Survey）作为基准，对比了19种文化和18种LLM架构；通过设置多种系统配置及参与式预算（participatory budgeting）案例研究进行实验。

Result: 1. 价值多样性与价值对齐不相关，两者互为补充；2. 当前多智能体系统的价值多样性远低于人类社会；3. 混合使用不同架构（Mixed-backbone）虽能改善但无法完全弥合多样性差距；4. 社交互动会导致智能体趋向共识，从而产生群体同质化。

Conclusion: 价值多样性（Value Diversity）是评估多文化多智能体系统至关重要的维度。目前基于LLM的智能体社会存在明显的同质化趋势，且社交互动会进一步削弱这种多样性，建议将多样性评估纳入未来系统设计。

Abstract: Multicultural multi-agent systems are increasingly deployed in globally diverse settings, where different agents are grounded in different cultural backgrounds. Existing cultural evaluation focuses on value alignment: how closely a single agent matches a target culture. Yet alignment is a per-agent property and cannot reveal whether a system, taken as a whole, preserves the cultural plurality it is meant to represent. We propose value diversity as a system-level evaluation axis for multicultural agent systems, defined through the dissimilarity between culturally conditioned agents' responses on a shared value survey. Using the World Values Survey, we evaluate 19 cultures and 18 backbone models across a wide range of system configurations. We find that diversity is largely uncorrelated with alignment, indicating that the two capture complementary system properties, and that current multicultural agent systems fall substantially below human societies in value diversity. Mixed-backbone systems narrow this gap but do not close it, and the gap persists across culture compositions and agent scales. Social interaction further erodes diversity by driving agents toward consensus, and a participatory budgeting case study shows that this homogenization narrows the breadth of collective decision-making. Together, our results establish value diversity as a distinct evaluation axis for multicultural multi-agent systems and reveal a persistent homogenization tendency in current LLM-based societies. Our code and data are publicly available at https://github.com/iNLP-Lab/MultiAgent-Diversity.

</details>


### [69] [The Generator-Eraser Paradox: Community Guidelines for Responsible LLM-Assisted Dialect Resource Creation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06004)
*Wajdi Zaghouani*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文探讨了利用LLM开发方言资源时的“生成者-抹除者悖论”，提出了旨在保护语言多样性和社区主权的12项设计指南，并通过阿拉伯语案例展示了如何在保持真实性的前提下利用LLM技术。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型（LLM）既能加速方言资源的开发，又可能因偏向主流语言、同质化拼写及反馈循环效应，导致方言多样性的抹除，这对双语制、缺乏书面标准化或边缘化的方言群体构成了严峻挑战。

Method: 1. 基于社会语言学和语料库语言学，形式化“生成者-抹除者悖论”（generator-eraser paradox）；2. 导出12项可操作的社区指南，将其转化为方言资源创建与文档记录的设计需求；3. 通过阿拉伯方言的深度案例研究，验证上述设计框架的可行性。

Result: 本项目不仅提出了一套理论框架，还制定了12条针对性的操作准则，并证实了这些指南能够有效应对阿拉伯语在双语、拼写变异及社区治理方面的问题。

Conclusion: LLM在方言资源开发中具有双重作用（生成与抹除），必须通过建立基于社会语言学和语料库语言学的框架，并遵循社区治理原则，才能在利用其技术优势的同时，保障方言的正统性、多样性和主权。

Abstract: Dialect resources occupy a unique position at the intersection of scientific description, cultural preservation, and computational infrastructure. Large language models offer powerful capabilities for accelerating dialect resource development through retrieval-grounded drafting, corpus navigation, metadata enrichment, and annotation workflow support. However, the same systems pose substantial risks: they can contribute to dialect erasure by privileging prestige varieties, homogenizing orthography, and enabling synthetic feedback loops that reduce linguistic diversity over time. These risks are particularly acute for language varieties characterized by diglossia, limited written standardization, or marginalized speaker communities. This paper makes three contributions. First, we integrate insights from variationist sociolinguistics and corpus linguistics to formalize the generator-eraser paradox as a theoretical framework for understanding the dual nature of LLM-assisted dialect work. Second, we derive 12 community guidelines that operationalize this framework into implementable design requirements for dialect resource creation and documentation. Third, we provide an in-depth case study of Arabic dialects, including a structured comparison of widely used resources, to demonstrate how these guidelines address language-specific challenges including diglossia, orthographic variability, and community governance. The contribution is conceptual and operational rather than experimental, with the goal of enabling dialect communities and resource builders across languages to adopt LLMs without sacrificing authenticity, variation, or sovereignty.

</details>


### [70] [Contextualized Prompting For Stance Detection On Social Media](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06022)
*Tilman Beck,Shakib Yazdani,Simon Kruschinski,Marcus Maurer,Iryna Gurevych*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了上下文信息对LLM零样本立场检测的影响，发现并非所有信息都有益：提示词中加入目标描述有效，而加入用户元数据或历史推文可能因噪声反而降低模型表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交媒体文本短促、含噪且强依赖语境，现有的LLM零样本立场检测方法因缺乏上下文信息，导致对模糊推文的解读能力受限。

Method: 系统性地评估了三种上下文信息（现实环境特征如用户简介、衍生特征如所属党派、以及LLM生成的目标描述）对立场检测零样本提示（Zero-shot Prompting）的影响；跨四个基准数据集（含一个新构建的德语数据集）测试了多种LLM。

Result: 融合上下文信息可提升性能，但效果取决于信息类型：LLM生成的“目标描述”能稳定提升精度；用户元数据效果不一；包含用户历史推文反而可能因为噪声干扰而导致性能下降。

Conclusion: 在社交媒体立场检测中，并非所有上下文信息都有助于提升性能。LLM生成的“目标描述”是有效的，但用户元数据和该用户的其他推文可能因引入噪声而损害性能，表明LLM在区分有用与无关上下文信息方面存在局限性。

Abstract: Stance detection on social media is challenging due to short, noisy, and context-dependent language. While large language models (LLMs) show zero-shot generalization, they are typically prompted without contextual information, which limits their ability to interpret ambiguous posts. In this work, we systematically investigate the impact of incorporating real-world (e.g., user biographies), derived (e.g., political party), and LLM-generated (e.g., target descriptions) contextual features into zero-shot prompting for stance detection on Twitter. Our evaluation spans four benchmark datasets, including a new high-quality German Twitter stance dataset. Across multiple LLMs, we find that integrating contextual information improves performance, but only under specific conditions. LLM-generated target descriptions consistently enhance accuracy, while other user metadata has mixed or even detrimental effects. Notably, we show that the inclusion of other tweets by the same user, often beneficial in supervised learning, can impair performance due to input noise. Our qualitative analysis reveals that LLMs struggle to distinguish task-specific useful information from irrelevant context. Our findings highlight both the promise and challenges of prompting with context information in noisy real-world settings. We publish code and data at this \href{https://github.com/tilmanbeck/stance-context-twitter}{page}.

</details>


### [71] [EGTR-Review: Efficient Evidence-Grounded Scientific Peer Review Generation via Multi-Agent Teacher Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06025)
*Xinpeng Qiu,Wang Yihu,Zhifeng Liu,Xiaochen Wang,Jimin Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: EGTR-Review 是一种通过多智能体教师蒸馏技术，将复杂评审推理过程迁移至高效轻量模型的框架，实现了兼具事实依据、来源可追溯性和低推理成本的自动同行评审生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 LLM 同行评审生成方法存在评价通用、缺乏证据支撑、来源不可追溯的问题，且复杂的多智能体架构导致推理成本过高。

Method: 提出 EGTR-Review 框架：1. 构建多智能体教师系统，负责论文分解、关键要素提取、外部证据检索、标注、逻辑推理及评审合成；2. 采用基于任务前缀的多任务学习，将教师的中间推理轨迹与最终评审内容蒸馏至轻量化学生模型；3. 使用证据加权目标函数（Evidence-weighted objective）以优化对弱监督或非验证性数据的处理。

Result: 实验表明，EGTR-Review 学生模型在自动化指标、LLM-as-Judge 评估及人工评估中均优于现有基线（包括 prompt-based、微调及 Agent 方法），同时大幅降低了 token 消耗和推理时间。

Conclusion: EGTR-Review 框架有效提升了科学同行评审生成的质量与效率。通过多智能体教师蒸馏，该方法能够在保持轻量化模型推理优势的同时，显著增强评审意见的事实依据性和来源可追溯性，为解决自动化评审中的通用性与不可靠性问题提供了有效范式。

Abstract: Scientific peer review generation has attracted increasing attention for reducing reviewing burdens and providing timely feedback. However, existing Large Language Model (LLM)-based methods often produce generic comments with insufficient evidence support and weak source traceability, while complex multi-agent systems incur high inference costs. To address these challenges, we propose EGTR-Review, an Evidence-Grounded and Traceable Review Generation framework via Multi-Agent Teacher Distillation. EGTR-Review first constructs a multi-agent teacher that performs structure-aware paper decomposition, key-element extraction, external scholarly evidence retrieval, evidence-state labeling, verification reasoning, and review synthesis. It then distills both intermediate reasoning trajectories and final review comments into a lightweight student model through task-prefix-driven multi-task learning. An evidence-weighted objective further reduces the influence of weak, missing, or non-verifiable supervision. Experiments on public peer-review datasets show that EGTR-Review (Student) outperforms strong prompt-based, fine-tuned, and structured/agentic baselines across automatic metrics, LLM-as-Judge evaluation, and human evaluation, while maintaining strong factual grounding and source traceability with substantially lower token consumption and inference time. Our code, prompts, configurations, and sample data are available on GitHub.

</details>


### [72] [NAVIRA: Decoupled Stochastic Remasking for Masked Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06031)
*Andrey Fomenko,Maksim Kryzhanovskiy,Svetlana Glazyrina,Roman Ischenko*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对遮罩扩散语言模型因标记依赖导致的错误累积问题，本文提出了NAVIRA策略。它将标记质量评分与重生成过程解耦，并采用随机重遮罩，有效提升了长文本生成的质量与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的遮罩扩散语言模型（如PRISM）在并行生成时存在“修正问题”，即由于预测过程耦合，导致低质量标记（Token）的错误预测会继续影响后续的生成，引发污染。

Method: 提出一种名为NAVIRA的推理时间解码策略：1. 解耦：将模型的前向传播分为“评分”与“生成”两个独立步骤；2. 随机重遮罩：根据评分结果随机选择重遮罩位置，并引入温度控制机制，以在流畅性与多样性之间取得平衡。

Result: 在170M参数的模型上进行实验，结果表明NAVIRA显著提升了生成文本的流畅度。通过控制重遮罩频率，在更大的计算预算下实现了更优的LLM-Judge评价得分，并保持了较好的熵值（多样性）。

Conclusion: 遮罩扩散语言模型的性能不仅取决于学习到的质量评价指标，更取决于高质量的重遮罩策略。NAVIRA通过解耦评分与生成过程及引入随机重遮罩机制，有效解决了早期错误传播问题，提升了生成质量。

Abstract: Masked diffusion language models generate text by iteratively unmasking many tokens in parallel, but this speed comes with a correction problem: tokens generated in the same step are predicted from marginal distributions, and early local dependency errors can later contaminate the context. PRISM addresses this by learning token-level quality scores and remasking unreliable tokens, but its inference rule is coupled: the same forward pass both detects low-quality tokens and computes logits for their replacements, so the erroneous tokens still condition regeneration. We propose NAVIRA, an inference-time decoding policy that separates these two operations and samples remasking positions stochastically. A first forward pass scores tokens; selected tokens are masked; a second forward pass regenerates from the cleaned context. Temperature-controlled remasking reduces repeated correction of the same positions and balances fluency against diversity. In controlled experiments with a 170M masked diffusion language model, decoupling improves fluency, while scheduled stochastic remasking preserves entropy and achieves stronger LLM-judge scores under larger forward-pass budgets. These results show that remasking policy, not only the learned quality signal, is central to reliable masked-diffusion text generation.

</details>


### [73] [English-to-Prakrit Machine Translation via Multilingual Transfer Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06038)
*Om Choksi,Smit Kareliya,Shrikant Malviya,Pruthwik Mishra*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 通过将普拉克里特语映射至相近的印地语标签，成功在不改动模型基础设施的前提下实现了低资源普拉克里特语的翻译能力提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对 IndicTrans2 模型不支持普拉克里特语（Prakrit）的问题，探索在极低资源环境下实现该语言机器翻译的方法。

Method: 利用 IndicTrans2 多语言模型，在不修改架构、词表或分词器的情况下，通过将普拉克里特语（Prakrit）语料映射至印地语（hin_Deva）语言标签进行微调。

Result: 在通过 1,474 对马哈拉施特里普拉克里特语（Maharashtri Prakrit）平行语料进行训练并在 20 个样本的 Ardhamagadhi 测试集上评估后，相比未微调的基准模型，BLEU 分数有所提升。

Conclusion: 脚本兼容的语言路由技术（将普拉克里特语映射到印地语标签）为低资源及不受支持的古语机器翻译提供了一种可行方案，但该方法的有效性在很大程度上受限于数据稀缺性和方言差异。

Abstract: We study English-to-Prakrit machine translation in a low-resource setting where the target language is unsupported by IndicTrans2. We adapt the multilingual model by mapping Prakrit to the Hindi language tag (hin_Deva) without modifying the tokenizer, vocabulary, or architecture. Using a 1,474-pair Maharashtri Prakrit parallel corpus and evaluation on a 20-sample Ardhamagadhi test set, we report corpus BLEU improvements over an untuned baseline. The results indicate that script-compatible language routing can enable feasible transfer to unsupported classical languages, while highlighting limitations due to data scarcity and dialect mismatch. Our code and trained models are released to the public for further exploration https://github.com/D3v1s0m/indictrans2-prakrit-mt.

</details>


### [74] [IA-RAG: Interval-Algebra-Driven Temporal Reasoning for Dynamic Knowledge Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06044)
*Xiaoman Wang,Yaoze Zhang,Wenzhuo Fan,Hongwei Zhang,Ding Wang,Guohang Yan,Song Mao,Botian Shi,Yunshi Lan,Pinlong Cai*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: IA-RAG 是一种层次化时序检索增强生成框架，通过将知识映射为时间区间（Allen 区间代数）并引入子图时间收紧机制，克服了传统 RAG 处理复杂时间逻辑的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 RAG 框架处理时间信息过于简化（仅限静态或粗粒度时间戳），无法捕捉时间间的嵌套、重叠等复杂结构，导致在时序推理任务上表现不佳。

Method: 提出 IA-RAG 框架，包括：1. 将知识表示为区间事件单元（IEUs）；2. 构建层次化主题森林（Thematic Forest）；3. 采用 Allen 区间代数管理时序依赖；4. 引入子图时间收紧机制（Sub-graph Time Tightening）处理不确定边界。

Result: 在 TimeQA、TempReason 和 ComplexTR 等 benchmark 上取得了强大的性能，尤其在复杂的组合时间推理任务中表现突出。

Conclusion: IA-RAG 通过将知识建模为时间区间并利用区间代数进行推理，有效解决了现有 RAG 在处理复杂时间关系（如重叠、包含等）时的缺陷，在时间问答任务上表现优异。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) has shown strong effectiveness in grounding Large Language Models (LLMs) with external knowledge. However, existing RAG and Graph RAG frameworks largely treat knowledge as static or associate time with coarse-grained timestamps or metadata, failing to capture rich temporal structures such as duration, overlap, and containment. We propose IA-RAG, a hierarchical temporal RAG framework that models knowledge as time intervals and performs retrieval under formal temporal constraints. IA-RAG represents facts as Interval Event Units (IEUs) and organizes them into a hierarchical Thematic Forest, where temporal dependencies are governed by Allen's Interval Algebra. To handle incomplete or uncertain temporal boundaries, IA-RAG further introduces a Sub-graph Time Tightening mechanism that refines fuzzy intervals through logical constraints within connected event subgraphs. In addition, IA-RAG supports implicit temporal semantic retrieval through interval-algebra-guided traversal. Experiments on multiple temporal question answering benchmarks, including TimeQA, TempReason, and ComplexTR, demonstrate that IA-RAG achieves strong temporal retrieval and reasoning performance, particularly on complex compositional temporal reasoning tasks. Our code is released at https://github.com/xiaoAugenstern/LogicalRAG_TemporalQA.

</details>


### [75] [Automatic Labelling of Speech Translation Errors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06047)
*Dominik Macháček,Maike Züfle,Ondrej Klejch*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了语音翻译错误标注（STEL）框架以提升语音翻译系统的可靠性，研究发现现有模型在错误检测上仍滞后于人类，且直接进行语音分析与文本分析结合是实现更优错误诊断的关键。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语音翻译系统中的错误会降低系统可信度并可能导致严重后果，但目前行业内缺乏针对语音翻译质量评估与置信度估计的标准化方法。

Method: 提出了一种名为STEL（语音翻译错误标注）的评估框架；构建了一个小规模、真实的端到端评估数据集；对比分析了纯文本模型（如XCOMET）与多模态大模型（如Qwen2.5-Omni）在执行该任务时的表现。

Result: STEL框架在评估现有系统的错误检测能力方面表现有效，发现XCOMET与Qwen2.5-Omni的精度约为人类水平的50%，并证明了多模态与纯文本系统在不同错误类型检测中的互补特质。

Conclusion: 目前的文本评估与多模态模型在语音翻译错误标注任务上表现尚不理想（仅达到人类水平的一半）；直接处理语音信号对错误检测至关重要；现有系统与语音处理模型在识别不同类型的错误上具有互补性。

Abstract: Errors in speech translations reduce trustworthiness of Speech Translation (ST) systems and can have serious consequences. Yet currently there is no established methodology for evaluating confidence and quality estimation of speech translations. To initiate progress in this direction, we propose Speech Translation Error Labelling (STEL). We create an annotation protocol, a small authentic end-to-end evaluation dataset, and we analyse how existing text-only and speech-processing systems perform the STEL task. Our results show that text-only XCOMET and multimodal LLM Qwen2.5-Omni are able to perform the STEL task in roughly half the precision of humans. We also find that direct speech processing is necessary for the STEL task, and that the current text-only and speech-processing systems are complementary in labelling translation-only vs. speech-processing errors in ST.

</details>


### [76] [Multi-task Learning is Not Enough: Representational Entanglement in Dual-output Second Language Speech Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06065)
*Seung Hwan Cho,Young-Min Kim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 多任务学习（MTL）改善L2语音语义识别的同时，可能因编码器层面的任务纠缠导致表面转写质量下降（以英语表现尤为显著），研究建议采取措施减轻这种表示层面的干扰。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多任务学习（MTL）假设共享表示对转写和理解任务均有益，但作者发现该假设在特定语言（如英语）中会导致转写性能退化，需探究其背后的根源。

Method: 通过分析英韩双语L2语音识别的多任务模型，结合Levenshtein编辑距离量化表面与意义的偏差，并利用编码器-解码器一致性分析探测任务在模型内部的表示纠缠情况。

Result: 实验表明：1. MTL虽然提升了意义识别，但恶化了表面转写（尤其在英文字符上）；2. 退化程度与表面-意义的编辑距离正相关；3. 韩语任务在编码器中保持独立，而英语任务则发生深度纠缠，导致解码器受限。

Conclusion: 多任务学习在跨语言（英/韩）L2语音识别中存在“编码器层面的解耦”挑战，直接耦合会导致表面转写性能下降。未来的MTL框架设计应重点缓解编码器的任务纠缠。

Abstract: Second-language (L2) speech recognition often requires transcriptions of pronunciations and intended meanings. Multi-task learning (MTL) is a natural approach because it assumes that shared representations benefit both outputs. However, this paper shows that this assumption does not hold across Korean and English. MTL improves meaning but degrades surface transcription, especially in English, where the degradation scales with surface-meaning divergence measured by Levenshtein edit distance.Encoder analysis links these patterns to encoder-level entanglement, with Korean preserving distinct task representations while English produces nearly identical ones. Cross-task decoder analysis shows that the meaning dual-output decoder adapts with a unique representation, while the surface dual-output decoder remains constrained by the encoder. These findings motivate the design of MTL frameworks that mitigate encoder-level entanglement to reduce surface degradation in dual-output L2 automatic speech recognition.

</details>


### [77] [SkillComposer: Learning to Evolve Agent Skills for Specification and Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06079)
*Qi Zhang,Zhaopeng Feng,Xiaonan Shi,Xiaomeng Hu,Chu Liu,Pengjun Xie,Xiaobin Wang,Jieping Ye,Bryan Hooi,Haobo Wang,Junbo Zhao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SkillComposer 是一个通过“创建、改进、合并”操作实现智能体技能自助演化的框架，有效解决了技能在任务特异性与通用性之间的权衡难题，显著提升了模型在各类基准测试中的推理与执行能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体技能构建方法通常为“一次性提取”，难以解决特定任务技能泛化性差与抽象技能指导不足之间的矛盾，缺乏明确的技能规范化与泛化机制。

Method: 提出了一个包含“创建（create）”、“改进（improve）”和“合并（merge）”三个学习操作的框架，并通过系统性的拒绝采样配方（rejection sampling recipe）进行训练，支持离线构建、在线优化及混合部署模式。

Result: 在 τ²-Bench、LiveCodeBench v6 和 AppWorld 等基准测试中表现卓越，SkillComposer-4B 模型使 27B 规模的执行器在智能体任务和代码任务上分别提升了 4.5 和 3.4 个点，并在未见过的领域展现出良好的泛化能力。

Conclusion: SkillComposer 是一种有效的基于推理时自助进化的框架，能够通过技能的创建、改进与合并，实现技能的通用性与任务特异性之间的平衡，证明了技能组合是一项可迁移的元能力。

Abstract: Agent skills, which consist of reusable strategies that guide agent reasoning and action, have shown strong potential for improving model capability at inference time. However, current skill construction methods treat the problem as one-shot extraction, overlooking a fundamental tension: a skill tailored to the specific task fails to transfer, while the abstracted skill often provides insufficient guidance. We attribute this fragility to the absence of explicit mechanisms for skill specification and generalization. To address this gap, we introduce SkillComposer, a framework that decomposes skill construction into three learnable operations: create, improve, and merge. Trained via systematic rejection sampling recipe, SkillComposer enables language models to self-evolve skills at inference time and supports three deployment modes: offline for building generalized libraries, online for task-specific refinement, and hybrid for combining both. Comprehensive experiments on $τ^2$-Bench, LiveCodeBench v6, and AppWorld show that SkillComposer consistently outperforms baselines. Our SkillComposer-4B improves a 27B executor by up to +4.5 on agent tasks and +3.4 on code tasks, while generalizing across domains and task types unseen during training. Analysis reveals that merge and improve address orthogonal quality dimensions and that skill composition is a transferable meta-ability, providing a practical recipe for skill-augmented inference.

</details>


### [78] [LatentSkill: From In-Context Textual Skills to In-Weight Latent Skills for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06087)
*Aofan Yu,Chenyu Zhou,Tianyi Xu,Zihan Guo,Rong Shan,Zhihui Fu,Jun Wang,Weiwen Liu,Yong Yu,Weinan Zhang,Jianghao Lin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LatentSkill 通过将文本技能“编译”为权重空间的 LoRA 适配器，解决了智能体系统中技能注入的上下文浪费和隐私暴露问题，同时实现了更优的任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体系统通过在 Prompt 中注入文本技能来实现任务重用，但这导致了巨大的上下文开销，且因以明文形式暴露技能内容而存在安全隐患。

Method: 提出 LatentSkill 框架，利用预训练超网络（Hypernetwork）将文本技能转换为“即插即用”的 LoRA 适配器。通过权重空间的算术运算实现技能的组合与缩放，无需在每一步推理中插入冗长的文本技能提示。

Result: 在 ALFWorld 和 Search-QA 任务中，LatentSkill 相比传统的上下文提示基线，不仅显著提升了任务成功率（ALFWorld 分别提升 21.4 和 13.4 个百分点），而且有效减少了预填充 Token（ALFWorld 减少 64.1%，Search-QA 减少 72.2%）。此外，生成的技能 LoRA 表现出结构化的语义几何特性，支持通过缩放系数和参数算术进行精确控制和组合。

Conclusion: LatentSkill 证明了将技能存储在权重空间（LoRA）而非上下文空间中，是扩展 LLM 智能体的一种更高效、模块化且隐私性更好的方案。

Abstract: Agent systems increasingly use textual skills to encode reusable task procedures, but injecting these skills into the prompt at every step incurs substantial context overhead and exposes skill content as plaintext. We present LatentSkill, a framework that converts textual skills into plug-and-play LoRA adapters through a pretrained hypernetwork. LatentSkill stores skill knowledge in weight space rather than context space, removing per-step skill tokens while preserving modular loading, scaling, and composition. On ALFWorld and Search-QA, LatentSkill outperforms the corresponding in-context skill baseline while using substantially fewer prefill tokens: it improves ALFWorld success by 21.4 and 13.4 points on the seen and unseen splits with 64.1% fewer prefill tokens, and improves Search-QA exact match by 3.0 points with 72.2% lower skill-token overhead. Further analysis shows that generated skill LoRAs form a structured semantic geometry, can be precisely controlled via the LoRA scaling coefficient, and can be composed through parameter-space arithmetic when skill components are aligned. These findings suggest that weight-space skills provide an efficient, modular, and less exposed substrate for extending LLM agents.

</details>


### [79] [CHALIS: A Challenge Dataset for Language Identification in Difficult Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06088)
*Michal Tichý,Jindřich Libovický*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对亲缘语言分类困难和非标准文本噪声干扰，作者发布了名为CHALIS的新型基准数据集，发现当前主流语言识别系统在这些挑战环境下表现均欠佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的语言识别系统在处理语言学上高度相似的语言（亲缘语言）以及包含非标准书写特征（如噪声、特殊脚本）的文本时，准确率往往显著下降，缺乏针对此类极端情况的专门评估资源。

Method: 构建了CHALIS（Challenging Language Identification Samples）评测基准，包含两部分：1）针对互通性强的“亲缘语言”对（如捷克语/斯洛伐克语等）的共享句集；2）针对“正字法噪声”的测试集（包括多脚本转写、去变音符号、同形异义词攻击和互联网俚语）。在四个主流语言识别系统上进行了性能评估。

Result: 在四个主流语言识别系统上的评估显示，所有系统在面对亲缘语言（尤其是低资源语言对）和转写输入时均表现出显著的性能恶化，证实了现有技术在复杂语言环境下的鲁棒性不足。

Conclusion: 现有主流语言识别系统在处理亲缘语言和复杂正字法噪声时表现不佳，该评测集揭示了当前技术的局限性，并为未来鲁棒语言识别研究提供了标准。

Abstract: We present CHALIS (Challenging Language Identification Samples), a new benchmark dataset explicitly designed to address difficult cases in language identification: cousin languages and orthographic noise. Our dataset has two parts: First, we collected sentences shared across mutually intelligible language pairs (Czech/Slovak, Spanish/Catalan, Portuguese/Galician, Danish/Norwegian). The second part tests for orthography noise: we transliterate text across multiple scripts, remove diacritics, simulate homoglyph attacks, and use Internet slang. We evaluate four widely used language identification systems on CHALIS and demonstrate that all struggle substantially in these scenarios, especially on lower-resource languages within cousin pairs and on transliterated input. The resource is publicly available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/michal-tichy/CHALIS.

</details>


### [80] [IR3DE: A Linear Router for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06098)
*Eros Fanì,Oğuzhan Ersoy*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了 IR3DE，一种基于岭回归的轻量级 LLM 路由框架，解决了领域专家模型调用中的高效路由与动态扩展难题，实现了低开销下的高性能表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 LLM 路由方法要么仅关注通用模型的成本优化，要么需要昂贵的训练成本来支持领域特定的路由，缺乏一种既能处理领域专家模型、又具备低开销和灵活动态更新能力的路由方案。

Method: 提出了一种名为 IR3DE 的基于岭回归（Ridge Regression）的路由器。它通过将路由问题建模为轻量级的回归任务，为每个输入提示（prompt）快速选择最合适的领域专家 LLM。

Result: 在 Causal Language Modeling (CLM) 设置中表现与现有基准相当；在推理任务设置中表现优于其他基准，达到了 98.4% 的归一化性能；支持模型专家阵列的动态扩展而不需重训。

Conclusion: IR3DE 是一种高效且灵活的推理路由方案，通过线性回归（Ridge Regression）实现了媲美复杂基准模型的路由性能，同时支持领域模型的动态增删，无需重新训练路由器，具有极高的工程实用价值。

Abstract: Foundational Large Language Models (LLMs) demonstrate proficiency on a wide range of general tasks, and achieve remarkable results on various specialized tasks via domain-expert LLMs. With the ever-growing list of available LLMs, inference routers are being proposed to select the most appropriate LLM for each prompt. However, existing routing methods either optimize cost across weak-to-strong generalist LLMs or require substantial training to support domain-expertise routing. In this paper, we propose IR3DE, a Ridge Regression-based Router for Domain Experts that provides cheap and fast routing decisions for each prompt. We evaluate IR3DE in two Causal Language Modeling (CLM) settings where the tasks are next-token prediction for all domains, and one reasoning setting where each domain has its own distinct reasoning task. Despite being a linear router, IR3DE achieves performance comparable to the other baselines in both CLM settings, and surpassing them in the reasoning setting, with a normalized performance of 98.4%. Moreover, IR3DE enables the addition or removal of new domain experts without requiring the router to be retrained from scratch, allowing a dynamic set of LLMs to be served with minimal disruption to the router itself. Our code is available at: github.com/gensyn-ai/IR3DE.

</details>


### [81] [Harnessing Structural Context for Entity Alignment Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06109)
*Xingyu Chen,Yuanning Cui,Zequn Sun,Wei Hu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ContextEA 是一种增强型实体对齐基础模型，通过引入跨图交互编码器和结构化校准解码器，强化了结构化上下文的挖掘与利用，显著提升了跨知识图谱的泛化对齐能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的实体对齐基础模型在编码阶段跨图交互能力较弱，且在候选排序阶段过度依赖粗糙的相似度计算，未充分挖掘和利用结构化上下文。

Method: 提出了 ContextEA 框架：1) 在编码器端，引入 cross-KG interaction encoder，通过锚点桥接统一KG并进行早期关系感知传播；2) 在解码器端，引入 structural calibration decoder，通过多层次（实体、邻域、关系、锚点）结构化证据校准对齐分数。

Result: 在 OpenEA、SRPRS 和 DBP 三个基准测试集的 29 个数据集上，ContextEA 均取得了显著提升，且其预训练版本在性能上已超过经精调后的基线模型。

Conclusion: ContextEA 证明了在实体对齐（EA）基础模型中，显式利用结构化上下文（structural context）是提升模型泛化能力与对齐准确性的有效途径，其预训练模型性能超越了现有的微调基线。

Abstract: Entity alignment (EA) aims to identify equivalent entities across heterogeneous knowledge graphs (KGs) and is a key component of knowledge fusion and cross-KG reasoning. The recent EA foundation model demonstrates that alignment knowledge, once pretrained, can be directly applied to diverse previously unseen KG pairs. However, it still underuses structural context in two places: cross-KG interaction is weak during encoding, and final candidate ranking still relies too heavily on coarse similarity. We address these limitations with ContextEA, an enhanced encoder-decoder framework for transferable EA. On the encoder side, we introduce a cross-KG interaction encoder that unifies the two KGs with anchor bridges and performs earlier relation-aware cross-graph propagation. On the decoder side, we introduce a structural calibration decoder that calibrates alignment scores with entity-level, neighborhood-level, relation-level, and anchor-aware structural evidence. This design strengthens both structural context construction and structural context exploitation while remaining lightweight. Experiments on 29 EA datasets in OpenEA, SRPRS, and DBP show consistent gains over strong transferable baselines. Notably, the pretrained ContextEA already surpasses the finetuned baselines on all three benchmark groups, demonstrating substantially stronger transfer to unseen KGs. These results suggest that explicitly harnessing structural context is an effective direction for improving EA foundation models.

</details>


### [82] [Ouvia: A User-centered Framework for Measuring Usability of Speech Translation in Real-World Communication Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06177)
*Giuseppe Attanasio,Beatrice Savoldi,Daniel Chechelnitsky,Matteo Negri,Marine Carpuat,Maarten Sap,André F. T. Martins*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了Ouvia框架以评估语音翻译在真实场景中的用户可用性，发现现有系统仅有一半交互被认定为可用，且存在明显的人口统计学差异，同时表明QA-based评估指标优于传统评估方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的语音翻译评价主要集中在脱离实际语境的测试集上，过于关注整体翻译质量指标（如BLEU等），而忽视了终端用户在真实交流场景中的实际需求和体验。

Method: 构建了名为Ouvia的评价框架，设计了一个多阶段研究实验，记录了在医疗和日常场景下，由四种不同的ST系统通过翻译完成的超过1750次人机/人人交互任务，涵盖了三种英语方言和两种性别。通过实际用户感知评估来衡量语音翻译的可用性。

Result: 1. 现代语意翻译系统的实际可用性有限，仅约一半的交互被用户评价为“可用”；2. 不同人口统计学群体（方言、性别）在体验上存在显著的可用性差距；3. QA-based（基于问答的）评价指标比标准自动评测指标能更准确地预测现实世界中的可用性。

Conclusion: 现有的语音翻译评价体系过于关注整体质量，忽略了用户在实际场景中的交流需求。研究强调了构建“情境化、以用户为中心”的评价框架的必要性，并指出QA-based评价指标在预测实际可用性方面具有显著优势。

Abstract: Speech translation (ST) is increasingly adopted in user applications, yet its evaluation largely focuses on decontextualized testbeds and holistic quality, rather than end users' communication needs. We introduce Ouvia, an evaluation framework for measuring user-perceived usability of speech translation outputs in real-world settings. Ouvia focuses on one-to-one communication: an English speaker needs to convey a request to a Portuguese speaker, and the message is automatically translated. Through a custom web app and multi-phase study design, we collect more than 1,750 such interactions in healthcare and everyday situations, mediated by four ST systems, involving speakers from three English dialects and two genders. We find that modern ST serves people only to a limited extent -- only around half of interactions are rated as usable -- with significant gaps in reported usability across demographic groups. Moreover, among quality metrics, we find that QA-based evaluation is a substantially stronger predictor of real-world usability than standard approaches. Together, these findings stress the importance of situated, user-centered evaluation frameworks that go beyond holistic quality scores and attend to who the technology serves -- and how well.

</details>


### [83] [The Tell-Tale Norm: $\ell_2$ Magnitude as a Signal for Reasoning Dynamics in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06188)
*Jinyang Zhang,Hongxin Ding,Yue Fang,Weibin Liao,Muyang Ye,Junfeng Zhao,Yasha Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文发现大模型隐藏层状态的l2范数可作为其推理强度的一个内在指标，并以此提出了无需重训练的测试时扩展方法，有效提升了模型的推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前对于大模型内部推理机制缺乏一个本质的、模型内源性的度量标准，旨在填补观测与控制模型推理动态之间的空白。

Method: 1. 利用稀疏自动编码器（SAE）作为诊断探测工具，观察推理特征激活模式；2. 从理论上证明隐藏层状态的l2范数能够界定SAE推理特征的激活强度；3. 提出三种测试时（test-time）扩展技术：自适应逐层推理递归、内源推理状态引导、以及l2引导的响应选择。

Result: 实验证明，模型推理的高强度阶段对应着隐藏层l2范数的显著增加。基于l2范数的推理增强技术在多个架构和基准测试中显著提升了推理性能，且无需额外的数据或训练。

Conclusion: 研究验证了隐藏层状态的二范数（l2 norm）是衡量大模型推理强度的内在指标，并提出了一套无需训练、基于该信号的推理增强技术，为理解和控制模型内部推理过程提供了新范式。

Abstract: Recent work has sought to understand Large Language Models (LLMs) reasoning, yet a principled, model-intrinsic signal that captures its layer-wise reasoning dynamics remains underexplored. We bridge this gap by demonstrating that the l2 norm of hidden states serves as an endogenous signal of the model's reasoning intensity. Using Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs) as a diagnostic probe, we observe that LLMs' internal reasoning is marked by a sharp increase in reasoning feature activations concentrated in late layers. Motivated by this pattern, we establish a formal link between reasoning intensity and the model's latent geometry and theoretically prove that the l2 norm of hidden states bounds the activation strength of SAE reasoning features. Empirical correlation analysis and causal interventions further validate the l2 norm as a faithful indicator, where heightened norms consistently correspond to critical reasoning steps. We then introduce three test-time scaling techniques guided by l2 norms: (i) Adaptive Layer-wise Reasoning Recursion, (ii) Endogenous Reasoning State Steering, and (iii) l2-guided Response Selection, which requires no additional training or data and is compatible with advanced inference engines. Experiments across model architectures and benchmarks show that l2-norm-based techniques significantly improve reasoning performance, offering a principled yet simple lens to perceive and control LLM latent reasoning dynamics. Our code is available at https://github.com/zjy1298/The-Tell-Tale-Norm.

</details>


### [84] [Improving Answer Extraction in Context-based Question Answering Systems Using LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06197)
*Hafez Abdelghaffar,Ahmed Alansary,Ali Hamdi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于预训练大模型（Roberta-base）微调的问答系统，通过在SQuAD1.1数据集上的训练，有效解决了传统问答系统处理复杂问题时精确度不足的问题，显著提升了模型从上下文中提取答案的质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的问答系统在面对复杂或歧义问题时，往往难以准确理解上下文，导致生成答案的连贯性差、精准度低或出现无关回答，亟需提升模型的上下文感知与回答质量。

Method: 基于SQuAD1.1数据集对预训练的Roberta-base模型进行监督微调（Fine-tuning），以增强其上下文理解及答案提取能力。

Result: 微调后的Roberta-base模型表现优异，ROUGE-L得分为86.84%，BLEU得分为28.24%，BERTScore达到95.38%，验证了该方法在上下文问答任务中的高准确度与相关性。

Conclusion: 通过对预训练语言模型进行针对性的微调，能够显著提升问答系统在处理上下文信息时的准确性、可靠性和精确度。

Abstract: Question answering (QA) systems have achieved notable progress with the advent of large language models (LLMs). However, they still face challenges in accurately extracting and generating precise answers from given contexts, particularly when dealing with complex or ambiguous queries. Existing approaches often struggle with contextual understanding, answer consistency, and generalization across diverse domains. In this work, we propose a question answering system based on large language models, where the input consists of a textual context and a corresponding question, and the output is a concise and accurate answer. The motivation behind this research lies in addressing the limitations of current QA systems, particularly their tendency to produce irrelevant or imprecise responses despite having access to the correct context. Our methodology involves fine-tuning a pre-trained LLM on a benchmark QA dataset to improve its contextual comprehension and answer extraction capabilities. Specifically, we utilize the Stanford Question Answering Dataset (SQuAD1.1), which provides high-quality context-question-answer triplets for supervised training and evaluation. Experimental results show that the fine-tuned Roberta-base model achieves the highest performance, attaining a ROUGE-L score of 86.84%, a BLEU score of 28.24%, and a BERTScore of 95.38%. These results indicate strong accuracy and answer relevance, demonstrating the effectiveness of the proposed approach for context-based question answering tasks. Furthermore, the findings confirm that targeted fine-tuning substantially improves the reliability and precision of QA systems.

</details>


### [85] [Dense Contexts Are Hard Contexts: Lexical Density Limits Effective Context in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06203)
*Giovanni Dettori,Matteo Boffa,Danilo Giordano,Idilio Drago,Marco Mellia*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究揭示了“词汇密度”是制约LLM长上下文表现的关键因素。即使在相同长度和位置下，随着信息密度的增加，模型的检索性能会显著下降，证实了有效上下文容量受词汇密度直接影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多关注输入长度和相关信息位置对大模型长上下文性能的影响，该研究旨在探索“词汇密度”（即上下文引入独特信息的速率）是否为导致长上下文表现下降的第三个核心因素。

Method: 使用三个“大海捞针”（find-the-needle）风格的基准测试，在保持输入长度（~12k tokens）和目标位置一致的前提下，通过系统性地改变输入信息的词汇密度，评估了参数规模从9B到685B的开源大模型表现。

Result: 在高密度基准测试中，模型表现出现断崖式下跌，检索准确率从稀疏场景下的接近完美降至高密度场景下的60%以下。通过控制变量验证，降低词汇密度可有效恢复模型性能。

Conclusion: LLM的有效上下文窗口不仅仅取决于输入长度和位置，词汇密度是一个关键且常被忽视的因素。该研究强调了在处理高密度、紧凑型信息输入时，必须考虑词汇密度对模型检索能力的影响。

Abstract: Input length and the position of relevant information are widely cited as the primary causes of degraded LLM long-context performance. Here, we study lexical density -- the rate at which a context introduces distinct information -- as a third, largely overlooked factor that systematically reduces the effective context window of LLMs. We quantify the impact of lexical density on open-weight LLMs (9B-685B) using three "find-the-needle" style benchmarks with identical length (~12k tokens) and controlled needle position, but increasing density of information. We observe a sharp performance collapse in higher-density benchmarks: models that are near-perfect in sparse contexts drop below 60% retrieval score on denser ones. To rule out task-type confounds, we vary and control the density within each benchmark while keeping all other properties unchanged. Reducing density generally restores performance, especially in the high-density regimes where degradation appears. These results show that effective context capacity is a function of lexical density, with direct implications for real-world LLM systems operating on compact, information-rich inputs.

</details>


### [86] [FiLM-Based Speaker Conditioning of a SpeechLLM for Pathological Speech Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06211)
*Fernando López,Santosh Kesiraju,Jordi Luque*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究通过在冻结的 ASR 模型中引入 FiLM 机制注入说话人特征，提出了一种高效、无需微调权重即可提升病理语音识别性能的新方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动语音识别（ASR）在处理标准语言方面已取得显著进步，但针对神经系统疾病导致的病理语音识别仍面临巨大挑战，且传统的微调方法存在效率和泛化性问题。

Method: 提出了一种利用 Feature-wise Linear Modulation (FiLM) 进行说话人调节的方法。具体而言，将基于 x-vector 提取的说话人特征注入到冻结的 ASR Transformer 编码器的每一层中，实现对病理语音的个性化适配，无需更新基础模型权重。

Result: 在西班牙语和英语病理语音数据集上的实验结果表明，该说话人调节方法在 ASR 性能上与主流的适应性（如标准微调、参数高效微调、后处理）策略相当，并验证了模型在保留病理语音识别能力的同时，依然维持对非病理语音的处理能力。

Conclusion: 本研究证明了通过 FiLM 进行说话人调节是一种有效的病理语音识别适配策略，既能达到与现有微调方法相媲美的效果，又具备更好的泛化能力，且不会损害模型在普通语音任务上的性能。

Abstract: Automatic speech recognition (ASR) has advanced remarkably for standard speech; however, pathological speech from neurological conditions remains a significant challenge. We investigate speaker conditioning via Feature-wise Linear Modulation (FiLM), injecting x-vector-derived information into each transformer layer of a frozen ASR encoder to adapt internal representations to individual pathological speakers without modifying base model weights. We benchmark this for the ASR task against standard and parameter-efficient fine-tuning baselines, complemented by post-processing, on Spanish and English pathological speech. Additionally, we evaluate if the adapted model preserves the ability to answer speech-related questions. Results show that speaker-conditioned ASR is competitive with established adaptation strategies while retaining performance on non-conditioned speech.

</details>


### [87] [From Self to Other: Evaluating Demographic Perspective-Taking in LLM Hate Speech Annotation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06266)
*Paloma Piot,Javier Parapar*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究评估了利用大模型（LLM）模拟不同群体在仇恨言论评判上的主观差异，发现仅靠简单的身份提示并不准确，但通过替代预测提示（Vicarious Prompting）结合高性能模型（如Llama 3.1），能有效模拟人类的分歧模式并提升自动标注的质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仇恨言论检测具有极高的主观性，不同人口统计群体对同一内容的看法存在显著差异。由于招募多样化群体进行大规模人工标注成本高昂且难以扩展，利用“人物角色条件化大模型”（Persona-conditioned LLMs）模拟多元视角成为一种潜在解决方案，但其模拟效果及准确性仍缺乏系统验证。

Method: 研究评估了大规模语言模型（LLMs）在仇恨言论检测中模拟不同人口统计群体视角的表现。具体评估了三个维度：群体间意见分歧（inter-group disagreement）、群体内敏感度（in-group sensitivity）以及替代性预测能力（vicarious prediction），并测试了不同Prompt策略（如身份提示与替代预测提示）在Llama 3.1及其他模型上的有效性。

Result: 实验发现，没有单一模型能一致性地覆盖上述所有维度。模型表现高度依赖于特定模型架构，简单的身份提示并不能可靠地产生预期的社会判断差异。研究指出，采用Llama 3.1及替代预测提示的方法，在模拟人类分歧模式方面表现最强，最接近人类真实的评价分布。

Conclusion: 目前的模型无法仅通过简单的身份提示（identity prompts）可靠地模拟所有人类社会判断维度。尽管如此，使用Llama 3.1结合“替代预测提示”（vicarious prompting）在大多数人口统计维度中展现了较好的人类观感模拟能力，为实现更符合人类判断的自动标注提供了一种可靠的配置方向。

Abstract: Hate speech detection is inherently subjective: people from different demographic groups perceive the same content very differently. Collecting enough annotations from multiple demographic groups is costly and difficult to scale. Persona-conditioned Large Language Models (models prompted to adopt a specific demographic identity) have been proposed as a way to simulate diverse perspectives at scale. But do they actually reflect how different groups disagree? We evaluate three aspects of human social judgement: (i) whether personas from different groups disagree in human-like ways (inter-group disagreement), (ii) whether they become more sensitive when content targets their own identity (in-group sensitivity), and (iii) whether they can accurately predict how another group would react (vicarious prediction). Our results show that no model consistently captures all three dimensions, and performance is highly model-dependent and does not emerge reliably from minimal identity prompts alone. However, vicarious prompting with Llama 3.1 yields the highest cross-group agreement in most demographic axes and provides the closest overall approximation to human disagreement patterns, indicating that this configuration may provide a more reliable setting for automatic annotation aligned with human judgements.

</details>


### [88] [Many Circuits, One Mechanism: Input Variation and Evaluation Granularity in Circuit Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06267)
*Alireza Bayat Makou,Jingcheng Niu,Subhabrata Dutta,Iryna Gurevych*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文揭示了电路发现算法中的“幻觉特化”现象：模型中结构迥异的电路往往执行相同的计算功能，结构差异并不必然暗示独立机制。现有的源级评估方法常会高估电路的特异性，应改用边级评估和测试跨条件迁移来更准确地理解模型机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学术界通常将电路发现算法识别出的不同子图结构解释为模型内部不同的计算机制。本研究旨在验证这一假设是否成立，探究结构差异是否真实反映了功能的特化，还是仅仅是结构上的随机变异。

Method: 研究通过变动模型输入统计量（数据频率）但保持任务（字面序列复制）不变，在五个Pythia模型（70M-1.4B）上提取了75个电路。通过跨频带迁移测试、核心电路性能测试、因果干预以及对比源级与边级评估，分析不同结构电路的功能相关性。

Result: 研究发现：1. 不同输入频率提取出的“结构上不同”的电路在功能上是相同的（即发生“幻觉特化”）；2. 存在一个共享核心电路，可恢复至少99%的性能；3. 内部表示在频带间可互换；4. 同一条件下重复提取的电路结构也不尽相同，说明算法在采样一系列等价的子图。

Conclusion: 模型可解释性中，电路的结构差异并不等同于机制的差异。这种“幻觉特化”的存在表明，电路发现算法采样的是等效类中的子图，而非唯一的机制；研究强调了采用边级评估和跨条件迁移测试的必要性。

Abstract: Circuit discovery methods identify subgraphs that explain specific model behaviors, and structural differences between discovered circuits are commonly interpreted as evidence of distinct mechanisms. We test this assumption by varying input statistics while holding the task fixed, and show that the resulting structural differences exhibit apparent specialization but do not correspond to functional differences, a pattern we term phantom specialization. Using Literal Sequence Copying across four token-frequency bands plus a control condition in five Pythia models (70M-1.4B), we extract 75 circuits and find that structurally distinct circuits implement the same computation: band-specific edges transfer broadly across bands, a core shared across most bands recovers at least 99% of circuit performance, and causal interchange interventions confirm that internal representations are interchangeable across frequency bands. Repeated extractions within the same frequency band further suggest that discovery algorithms sample from an equivalence class of valid subgraphs rather than recovering a unique mechanism. Standard evaluation practice obscures this pattern: source-level evaluation inflates apparent faithfulness, while edge-level evaluation reveals the many-to-one mapping from structure to function. Our results show that structural differences between circuits are not sufficient evidence for distinct mechanisms, and that exposing this requires edge-level evaluation and cross-condition transfer tests.

</details>


### [89] [FOXGLOVE: Understanding Goal-Oriented and Anchored Writing Feedback from Experts and LLMs on Argumentative Essays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06271)
*Yijun Liu,Yifan Song,John Gallagher,Sarah Sterman,Tal August*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究发布了FOXGLOVE数据集，通过对比人类专家与LLM的写作反馈，发现两者在反馈策略上存在差异，且LLM的高评价很大程度上取决于其反馈文本的长度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前缺乏对大语言模型（LLM）反馈与专家反馈在写作研究核心维度（目标导向、具体锚定、优先级）上的系统性比较，限制了对LLM在写作教学中应用的深入认知。

Method: 构建了FOXGLOVE数据集（包含69篇高中作文及对应的专家点评和四种LLM生成的点评，共2340条），并结合专家对点评质量的定性评估，通过对比分析法在目标导向、具体句子锚定及反馈优先级等方面进行系统性研究。

Result: 1. 师生在反馈目标分布和位置分布相似；2. AI更倾向于提供更复杂、更少问询的反馈；3. AI反馈在质量维度上评分虽高，但长度因素对其评价结果贡献显著；4. 人机在锚定具体句子时存在显著差异。

Conclusion: LLM生成的写作反馈在质量评价上往往优于人类专家，但这主要归因于其反馈长度更长。该研究揭示了人机在反馈目标和位置上的相似性，以及在锚定具体句子和反馈问询倾向上的差异。FOXGLOVE数据集为深入研究人机协作写作反馈提供了基础。

Abstract: While large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to generate writing feedback, there remains no systematic comparison of LLM and expert feedback on the dimensions that writing research identifies as central to revision: goal-orientation, anchoring to specific sentences, and prioritization. We introduce FOXGLOVE, a dataset of 696 feedback comments written by trained writing instructors on 69 twelfth-grade argumentative essays, paired with 1,644 comments generated from four frontier LLMs under a shared protocol, totaling 2,340 comments. We provide expert quality ratings on a subset of both instructor and LLM comments. We find that instructors and LLMs distribute feedback similarly across goals and essay positions, yet instructors and models diverge on the specific sentences on which to provide feedback. Additionally, we find that models tend to write more complex feedback and use fewer questions than instructors. LLM feedback also receives higher ratings on most dimensions of quality, as rated by instructors, but much of this advantage appears to be attributable to lengthier comments. FOXGLOVE enables systematic comparison of where human and LLM feedback align, diverge, and differ.

</details>


### [90] [LLMs Can Leak Training Data But Do They Want To? A Propensity-Aware Evaluation of Memorization in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06286)
*Gianluca Barmina,Peter Schneider-Kamp,Lukas Galke Poech*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出PropMe框架和SimpleTrace工具，区分了语言模型记忆的“能力（测试被强迫记忆）”与“倾向（实际使用中泄露）”，实验证实通过特定攻击可诱导记忆，但在普通场景下罕见泄露，且持续训练有助于减轻记忆现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的语言模型记忆评估方法多关注模型“被迫”复现训练数据的极限能力（capability），但缺乏对模型在日常使用场景下“主动”泄露训练数据可能性（propensity）的探测。

Method: 1. 提出“PropMe”框架：通过对比前缀诱导攻击与非对抗评估，量化记忆倾向；2. 提出度量转换方法：将现有指标转化为倾向度量指标；3. 研发“SimpleTrace”：基于infini-gram的轻量化溯源流水线，实现生成内容与大规模训练语料的确定性归因；4. 实验设置：在Comma和DFM Decoder模型、Common Pile和Dynaword数据集上进行对比分析。

Result: 1. 发现能力与倾向之间存在显著差距：通过特定前缀攻击能轻易诱导出强记忆信号，但在常规或数据集特定提示下，倾向得分保持在低位。2. 持续预训练（从Comma到DFM Decoder）能有效降低模型对已有训练数据的记忆能力及倾向。

Conclusion: 大模型在“被诱导”（worst-case）下有较强数据复现能力，但在“普通使用”（ordinary use）下罕见发生数据泄露。研究强调评估中应同时包含“最坏情况可提取性”与“日常泄露倾向”，前者用于评估能力上限，后者用于评估实际风险。此外，持续预训练可降低模型对旧数据的记忆倾向。

Abstract: Large language models can reproduce training data, but existing memorization evaluations mostly measure whether models can be forced to do so, rather than whether they do so under ordinary use. We introduce PropMe, a propensity-aware framework for memorization evaluation that contrasts prefix-based capability attacks with non-adversarial evaluations. We propose a metric transformation that, applied to existing functions, allows to create propensity metrics. We further introduce SimpleTrace, a lightweight tracing pipeline built on infini-gram that deterministically attributes model generations to large-scale training corpora and computes verbatim, near-verbatim, and propensity-transformed memorization metrics. Evaluating two fully-open models: Comma and DFM Decoder on two datasets: Common Pile and Dynaword in two languages, we find a consistent gap between capability and propensity: prefix attacks elicit substantially stronger memorization signals than generic or dataset-specific prompts, while propensity scores remain low overall. Thus, the models can reveal training data when directly elicited, but rarely do so in more common non-adversarial settings. We also find that DFM Decoder, which is continually pre-trained from Comma, exhibits reduced memorization and memorization propensity for Common Pile, confirming that memorization capability can decrease when later training emphasizes partially different data. Our results suggest, and we encourage, that memorization audits should report both worst-case extractability and ordinary leakage propensity in order to have a more comprehensive view of this phenomenon.

</details>


### [91] [Decomposing Factual Sycophancy in Language Models: How Size and Instruction Tuning Shape Robustness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06306)
*Victor De Marez,Luna De Bruyne,Walter Daelemans*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文将事实性奉承拆解为“真实边际”和“操纵敏感度”两个维度，发现规模和指令微调对这两者有不同的动态影响，说明仅评估翻转率是不够的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的“翻转率”指标混淆了模型对真理的固有偏好与对外部压力做出妥协的倾向，无法清晰区分导致事实性奉承的潜在机制。

Method: 将事实性奉承解构为“真实边际”（truth margin，对真理的基础偏好）和“操纵敏感度”（manipulation sensitivity，对压力偏移的程度）两个通道。通过对56个参数从0.3B到32B的开源模型在13种操纵类型下进行测试，分析模型规模与指令微调对上述两个渠道的影响。

Result: 1. 模型脆弱性主要由规模决定；2. 指令微调在小型模型中可能降低稳健性，但在大型模型中通常会增强稳健性；3. 指令微调主要提升了真实边际；4. 基础模型随规模增长提高了真实边际但增加了敏感度，而指令微调模型随规模增长不仅提升了边际，还降低了敏感度。

Conclusion: 事实性奉承（Factual sycophancy）并非单一的标量属性，不能仅靠“翻转率”（flip rates）来衡量。评估应采用渠道特定（channel-specific）、操纵特定（manipulation-specific）且考虑规模因素的细分方法。

Abstract: Factual sycophancy occurs when a language model abandons a correct, verifiable answer under social pressure. Because a flip occurs only when pressure toward a false answer exceeds the model's neutral preference for the truth, flip rates conflate two mechanisms: the strength of that baseline preference (truth margin), and how far pressure shifts it (manipulation sensitivity). We decompose factual sycophancy into these channels and use them to separate the effects of size and instruction tuning across 56 open-weight models spanning 0.3B-32B parameters and 13 manipulation types. We find that vulnerability is governed mainly by size, but instruction tuning changes how size acts: small instruction-tuned models can become less robust, whereas large instruction-tuned models usually become more robust. Instruction tuning primarily increases truth margin, but its behavioral effect depends on manipulation type. Scaling also changes the two channels differently: base models gain margin but become mildly more manipulation-sensitive, whereas instruction-tuned models gain margin faster and become less sensitive. Factual sycophancy is therefore not a single scalar property. Evaluations should report channel-specific, manipulation-specific, and size-conditioned robustness rather than flip rates alone.

</details>


### [92] ["Chi nas dal soch el sent de legn" -- Auditing Text Corpora for Lombard](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06349)
*Edoardo Signoroni,Pavel Rychlý*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究通过对伦巴第语语料的人工审计发现，大规模网页爬取数据存在严重的质量缺陷和代表性偏差，强调了在资源建设中优先考虑语言变体多样性和专家参与的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多语言因缺乏高质量数据集而导致NLP资源匮乏，但通过自动爬取获取大规模数据往往隐藏了严重的数据质量和代表性偏差问题。

Method: 对伦巴第语（Lombard）现有的平行和单语语料库进行人工审计，并对其正字法组成、网页抓取数据的真实有效性以及语料在不同变体间的地理分布偏差进行了深入分析。

Result: 发现现有网页抓取数据存在严重的问题：大量的语言误识别、样板文本及非语言噪声；且存在严重的地理/变体偏差，高水平数据倾向于西伦巴第语，而东伦巴第语被边缘化。

Conclusion: 应当摒弃单纯追求数量的网页爬取策略，转而采取多样性感知、社区驱动的高质量数据整理方法，以解决语料库中的语言偏差与数据质量问题。

Abstract: Several of the world's languages are still under-resourced in terms of Natural Language Processing (NLP) tools. This is mostly due to the lack of high-quality datasets to train, develop, and evaluate systems and models for several tasks, such as Machine Translation (MT). We conduct a manual audit of the parallel and monolingual corpora available for Lombard, an under-resourced language continuum from Italy. Our analysis reveals that the perceived abundance of web-scraped data is an illusion, with massive datasets plagued by severe language misidentification, boilerplate text, and non-linguistic noise. Furthermore, we analyze the orthographic composition of the valid Lombard portions across web-scraped datasets, curated corpora, and benchmarks. Our findings show conflicting orthographical systems and severe representational bias across all corpora: high-quality data is heavily skewed towards Western Lombard varieties, with Eastern ones left on the margins. This underscores the need for variety-aware, community-driven data curation rather than purely quantity-driven scraping.

</details>


### [93] [EDIT: Evidence-Diagnosed Intervention Training for Rule-Faithful LLM Grading](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06350)
*Zhihao Wu,Linhai Zhang,Taiyi Wang,Runcong Zhao,Peter Andrews,Cesare Aloisi,Yulan He*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对LLM量规评分中不仅需准确率、更需遵循评分标准的挑战，本文提出了EDIT框架，通过定位并修正推理偏差及校准信念变化，显著提升了模型在该任务上的表现与忠实度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的信用分配和干预方法主要针对数学推理等封闭任务，无法有效解决量规评分中需要依据评分标准和证据进行推理的问题，导致模型难以纠正评分推理中的偏差或监控信念变化。

Method: 提出了名为EDIT（Evidence-Diagnosed Intervention Training）的两阶段训练框架：第一阶段EDIT-SFT利用模型后验概率和输入接地分数定位错误推理步骤，并结合量规核对表进行修正；第二阶段EDIT-RL通过“信念引导的奖励塑形”来校准评估器，惩罚有害的信念漂移。

Result: 在两个真实的跨学科评分基准测试中，EDIT在域内和跨域数据集上的表现均显著优于传统的监督微调（SFT）和强化学习（RL）基线模型，且消融实验证实内部状态诊断机制是性能提升的核心。

Conclusion: EDIT框架通过细粒度的推理修正和基于信念的奖励机制，有效提升了LLM在量规评分任务中的忠实度与准确性，证明了利用模型内部状态进行诊断是解决推理偏差的关键。

Abstract: Reliable rubric grading requires more than accurate score prediction. Each judgement must be grounded in the mark scheme and evidence from the student answer. Existing credit-assignment and intervention methods, primarily designed for self-contained reasoning tasks such as mathematics reasoning, struggle in this setting because they do not identify where grading reasoning goes wrong or how the model's belief about the final mark changes during reasoning. We propose Evidence-Diagnosed Intervention Training (EDIT), a two-phase framework for training more rubric-faithful LLM graders. First, EDIT-SFT locates problematic reasoning steps using internal model signals: posterior belief over the final mark and input-grounding scores. It then revises only these local steps with help from a rubric checklist. Second, EDIT-RL calibrates the grader with belief-guided reward shaping, penalising large harmful belief drifts while still allowing helpful exploration. Experiments on two real-world, multi-subject grading benchmarks demonstrate that EDIT consistently outperforms strong supervised fine-tuning and reinforcement learning baselines on both in-domain and out-of-domain splits, with ablation studies confirming that internal-state diagnostics drive these gains.

</details>


### [94] [Emergent Language as an Approach to Conscious AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06380)
*Zengqing Wu,Chuan Xiao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种利用多智能体强化学习中涌现语言（EL）的生成式方法，通过移除人类语言先验，成功观察到智能体在任务压力下演化出自我指涉的复杂通信结构，从而为研究意识结构提供了一种客观的实验路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的意识评估方法（检查表法或架构工程法）难以排除系统中表现出的“意识特征”是否仅是模仿人类语言先验的伪影（artifacts），通过生成式方法研究意识结构的溯源。

Method: 引入基于多智能体强化学习的生成式方法（EL），即智能体从无语言、无自我概念及极少人类数据接触的初始状态出发，仅通过任务压力驱动语言与通信结构的演化，从而排除人类语言先验的干扰。

Result: 在微型实验环境中，智能体成功演化出自我指涉的通信，并产生了一种并非由预设任务或架构设计的“回声失配检测机制”（echo-mismatch detection circuit）。

Conclusion: 通过多智能体强化学习中的涌现语言（EL）方法，证明了具备自我指涉特征（如回声失配检测机制）的类意识结构可以在没有任何人类语言先验的情况下产生，从而为研究意识提供了客观的生成式框架。

Abstract: The question of whether artificial systems can be conscious remains open, in part because existing approaches either evaluate systems against theory-derived checklists (discriminative) or engineer consciousness-inspired modules directly (architectural); both leave open whether observed structures are artifacts of human language priors. We propose a generative methodology: emergent language (EL) in multi-agent reinforcement learning, where agents start from minimal (no language, no concept of self, minimal exposure to human text) and develop communication under task pressure alone, ensuring causal attributability to task demands rather than inherited human language priors. We position our methodology by discussing how EL serves as a generative tool for studying consciousness-relevant structure, including the role of environment complexity and the interpretation of emergent communication. As a proof of concept, we instantiate this methodology in a minimal environment and show that agents develop self-referential communication, including an echo-mismatch detection circuit that is not predicted by task structure or architecture alone but emerges from a specific environmental affordance.

</details>


### [95] [CollabSim: A CSCW-Grounded Methodology for Investigating Collaborative Competence of LLM Agents through Controlled Multi-Agent Experiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06399)
*Jiaju Chen,Bo Sun,Yuxuan Lu,Yun Wang,Dakuo Wang,Bingsheng Yao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对多智能体系统（MAS）中协作能力评估缺失的问题，本文提出了评估框架 CollabSim，并通过实验证明其能有效量化智能体的协作水平及交互表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前 MAS 研究过分关注个体能力（推理/规划/工具使用）或最终任务结果，忽略了智能体在建立共识、维护共享理解及解决分歧等“协作能力”上的欠缺，亟需针对协作能力的系统性评估工具。

Method: 开发了 CollabSim 模拟框架，结合基于协作理论的定义、受控的交互条件操纵，以及对智能体内部状态的动作级探测技术。

Result: 通过对四种大型语言模型进行实验，验证了 CollabSim 能够有效捕捉交互条件的影响，区分不同模型表现的模式，并揭示任务特定因素对智能体设计的复杂影响。

Conclusion: CollabSim 为评估多智能体系统（MAS）的协作能力提供了系统性框架，研究揭示了现有模型在协作维度上的不足，并为未来提升智能体协作能力指明了方向。

Abstract: Multi-agent systems (MAS) built on large language models have shown growing promise, with their effectiveness resting on agents' ability to coordinate through text-based channels much as human teams do. Yet recent study suggests that MAS often falter not because agents lack individual task-solving ability, but because they lack collaborative competence: the capacity to establish common ground, maintain shared task understanding, balance individual and collective incentives, and repair misalignment as interaction unfolds. Decades of research in Computer-Supported Cooperative Work have characterized these requirements for human teams coordinating under constrained communication, yet existing MAS evaluations focus mainly on task outcomes or single-agent proficiency in reasoning, planning, and tool use. To enable a systematic analysis of agents' collaborative competence in MAS, we introduce CollabSim, a configurable simulation framework that combines a theory-grounded definition of collaborative capabilities, controlled manipulation of interaction conditions, and action-level probing of agents' internal states. Experiments across four LLMs show that CollabSim can capture condition effects, separate model performance patterns, and reveal task-dependent effects of agent design.

</details>


### [96] [A Komi-Yazva--Russian Parallel Corpus and Evaluation Protocol for Zero- and Few-Shot LLM Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06420)
*Petr Parshakov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文发布了首个科米-亚兹瓦语-俄语平行语料库，并建立了一套针对濒危语言机器翻译的严谨评估测试平台，探究了LLM在资源极度匮乏条件下的翻译效果与评估方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对科米-亚兹瓦语这种极其缺乏资源的濒危语言，现有评估方法在处理数据稀缺和数据泄露方面存在不足，缺乏针对该类语言的规范化平行语料及严谨的评估协议以探究LLM的翻译潜力。

Method: 构建了包含457个对齐句对的平行语料库，并制定了包含故事级交叉验证、确定性检索少样本提示、输出严格验证、参考/判官综合评价及不确定性估计的评估协议。对比了多种主流大语言模型在零样本（zero-shot）和检索增强小样本（few-shot）场景下的翻译能力。

Result: 研究发现现代LLM在科米-亚兹瓦语到俄语的翻译中表现出非平凡（non-trivial）的翻译能力，但性能受模型系列及提示策略影响显著；检索增强少样本提示优于零样本，但随着上下文增加，性能提升趋于平缓。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了一个濒危语言科米-亚兹瓦语（Komi-Yazva）的平行语料库及评估框架，强调了在资源极度匮乏的情况下，评估方法、指标选择及故障处理对模型性能评估结果至关重要。研究表明，基于检索的少样本提示对于提升翻译质量有效，但性能提升存在上限。

Abstract: We present the first Komi-Yazva--Russian parallel corpus together with an explicit evaluation protocol for studying LLM translation in an endangered, extremely low-resource setting. The dataset contains 457 aligned sentence pairs from 74 narrative texts and is accompanied by documented provenance, sentence-level alignment, and story identifiers that enable leakage-aware evaluation. We use this setup to compare modern large language models on Komi-Yazva-to-Russian translation under severe parallel-data scarcity in zero-shot and retrieval-based few-shot regimes. The protocol includes story-level cross-validation, deterministic retrieval for few-shot prompting, strict validation of generated outputs, complementary reference-based and judge-based metrics, and story-level uncertainty estimates. Across models, LLMs produce non-trivial translations, but performance varies strongly by model family and prompting regime. Retrieval-based few-shot prompting consistently improves over zero-shot prompting, while gains beyond a small retrieved context remain limited. The results show that evaluative conclusions in this setting depend materially on metric choice and failure handling, so the paper frames the corpus as both a dataset contribution and a reproducible evaluation testbed for endangered-language machine translation.

</details>


### [97] [Reinforcement Learning Elicits Contextual Learning of Unseen Language Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06428)
*Hanxu Hu,Zdeněk Šnajdr,Pinzhen Chen,Jannis Vamvas,Rico Sennrich*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于强化学习的方法，使大语言模型学会利用上下文中的语言知识来翻译极低资源语言，从而比传统的监督微调或上下文学习获得更好的零样本翻译性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 LLMs 在处理极低资源语言翻译时，通常依赖于持续训练或在上下文中编码语法书，这些方法容易产生过拟合，且缺乏零样本迁移能力。作者认为模型需要获得一种“利用上下文语言知识”的元技能，而非仅仅记忆特定语言。

Method: 提出了一种利用富含语言上下文的强化学习（RL）方法，使用表面层面的翻译指标（chrF）作为奖励信号进行训练，旨在培养模型在上下文中提取和应用语言知识的能力。

Result: 实验表明，尽管奖励信号轻量，RL 训练的模型能有效提取并应用上下文中的语言信息，在处理完全未见过的语言时，翻译效果优于上下文学习（ICL）和监督微调（SFT）。

Conclusion: 基于结果反馈的强化学习（RL）可以超越传统的数学和编程推理任务，成为一种通过上下文进行语言学习的有效方法，能够使 LLMs 具备利用上下文语言知识的元能力。

Abstract: Prior work has shown that large language models (LLMs) can translate unseen or low-resource languages by undergoing continued training or even by encoding a grammar book in their context. However, both methods typically overfit specific languages, with limited zero-shot transfer at test time. To translate extremely low-resource languages at scale, we argue that LLMs must acquire the meta-skill of utilizing in-context linguistic knowledge rather than memorizing specific languages. In this paper, we propose a reinforcement learning (RL) approach to unseen language translation given rich linguistic context, using a surface-level translation metric (chrF) as the reward. Empirically, despite the lightweight reward, our RL-trained models effectively extract and apply relevant linguistic information from the provided context, leading to better translations on completely unseen languages than in-context learning or supervised fine-tuning. Our analyses suggest that outcome-based RL can extend beyond conventional reasoning tasks like math and coding to serve as a recipe for language learning from context.

</details>


### [98] [Revising Context, Shifting Simulated Stance: Auditing LLM-Based Stance Simulation in Online Discussions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06443)
*Xinnong Zhang,Wanting Shan,Hanjia Lyu,Zhongyu Wei,Jiebo Luo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究通过反事实语境审计发现，大语言模型模拟的社交媒体用户立场极易受上下文变化影响，而非反映真实的个人信念。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然LLM常被用于模拟社交媒体用户行为，但尚不明确其模拟出的观点是基于真实的个人信念，还是仅仅对对话语境中无关信息的敏感反应。对此进行审计有助于理解LLM的立场模拟机制。

Method: 本文引入了“反事实语境修订”（counterfactual context revision）框架：首先推断目标用户立场；随后通过多种受控策略（文本及多模态模因）修订对话语境；最后对比修订前后的立场差异，并使用“平均定向立场偏移”和“立场转换率”两个指标进行衡量。

Result: 实验结果显示，无论是文本修订还是多模态修订，LLM在不同极化偏好机制下均表现出显著且稳健的立场转换，表明模型输出对语境高度敏感，而非保持固定的用户信念。

Conclusion: 该研究提出了一套用于审计大语言模型（LLM）立场模拟能力的评估框架，揭示了LLM在处理社交媒体语境时的敏感性，并强调了利用LLM模拟在线舆论动力学既有机遇也存在风险。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used to simulate social media users and infer how individuals may respond to online discussions. However, it remains unclear whether these simulations reflect precise user-specific beliefs or whether they are highly sensitive to semantically independent changes in conversational contexts. In this work, we study counterfactual context revision as a framework for auditing LLM-based stance simulation. Given an original online conversation, we first infer a target user's stance toward a specific topic. We then apply controlled revision strategies to the conversational context and simulate the user's stance again under the revised context. We compare text-only revision strategies with a multimodal one that incorporates meme-based context and evaluate two main effectiveness metrics, i.e., average directional stance shift and stance transition rate. The results reveal effective and robust stance transitions in both text-only and multimodal strategies across different polarization-preference mechanisms. Our study contributes an evaluation framework for understanding the context sensitivity of LLM-based stance simulation. More broadly, it highlights both the promise and risk of using LLMs to simulate online opinion dynamics.

</details>


### [99] [Latent Reasoning with Normalizing Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06447)
*Guancheng Tu,Xiangjun Fu,Suhao Yu,Yao Tang,Haoqiang Kang,Lianhui Qin,Yizhe Zhang,Jiatao Gu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对传统文本链式思考（CoT）低效且表达受限的问题，作者提出了NF-CoT框架。它通过在LLM中使用正规化流，将推理过程显性化为连续的潜状态，在完整保留大模型自回归生成特性和概率建模优势的同时，显著提升了代码推理的性能与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的Chain-of-Thought（CoT）虽能提升推理能力，但强制将所有中间计算转化为离散的文本符号不仅效率低，且限制了模型处理不确定性或复杂语义表达的能力。传统的潜推理方法通常以牺牲模型原生的自回归生成、概率采样及训练可追踪性为代价，因此需要一种既能利用潜空间灵活性，又能保留传统LM优势的推理方案。

Method: 提出了NF-CoT架构，在LLM内部署与TARFlow风格兼容的正规化流模型。通过一个NF头生成压缩的连续思维状态，与传统LM头的文本生成处于同一因果流中。该方法通过蒸馏明确的文本CoT来训练潜空间，支持直接的策略梯度优化，并兼容标准的键值（KV）缓存机制。

Result: 在代码生成任务中，NF-CoT的通过率超过了基准的显式CoT方法及现有的其他潜推理方法，同时大幅降低了中间推理过程的计算开销。

Conclusion: NF-CoT成功将正规化流（Normalizing Flows）集成至大语言模型中，实现了在保留高效概率建模能力的同时，利用连续潜变量空间进行中间推理。该方法在提升性能的同时显著降低了推理计算代价，证明了轻量级、概率可控的潜推理是替代传统离散文本链式思考（CoT）的可行路径。

Abstract: Large language models often improve reasoning by generating explicit chain-of-thought (CoT), demonstrating the importance of intermediate computation. However, textual CoT forces this computation through a discrete, serial, and communication-oriented token stream: each reasoning step must be verbalized before the model can proceed, even when the underlying update is semantic, uncertain, or only partially formed. Latent reasoning offers a higher-bandwidth alternative by performing intermediate computation in compact continuous states before committing to text. Yet existing latent-reasoning methods often sacrifice key advantages that make CoT effective in autoregressive language models, including native left-to-right generation, probabilistic sampling, compatibility with KV-cache decoding, and tractable likelihood estimation. We propose NF-CoT, a latent reasoning framework that preserves these advantages by modeling continuous thoughts with normalizing flows. NF-CoT instantiates a TARFlow-style normalizing flow inside the LLM backbone, defining a tractable probability model over compact continuous thoughts distilled from explicit CoT. Continuous-thought positions are generated by an NF head, while text positions are generated by the standard LM head within the same causal stream. This design provides exact likelihoods for latent thoughts, enables probabilistic left-to-right decoding with the original KV cache, and supports direct policy-gradient optimization in the latent reasoning space. On code-generation benchmarks, NF-CoT improves pass rates over explicit-CoT and prior latent-reasoning baselines while substantially reducing intermediate-reasoning cost.

</details>


### [100] [Human Adults and LLMs as Scientists: Who Benefits from Active Exploration?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06464)
*Mandana Samiei,Eunice Yiu,Anthony GX-Chen,Dongyan Lin,Jocelyn Shen,Blake A. Richards,Alison Gopnik,Doina Precup*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了主动探索对解决“合取因果学习障碍”的影响。结果表明，主动干预能改善人类对合取规则的学习，但合取规则在推断难度和效率上仍落后于析取规则；此外，研究发现大语言模型在推断准确率上已接近人类，但在策略效率上仍有不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既往研究表明，在被动观察模式下，人类在学习合取因果规则（多因同时出现才生效）时存在困难，本研究旨在探究当赋予学习者“主动探索”的代理权时，这种认知偏差是否依然存在。

Method: 采用改进的“Blicket Detector”（布利克探测器）任务，允许参与者进行主动干预（主动探索）以识别合取或析取因果规则；并将其表现与多种大语言模型（LLM）在该任务下的表现进行对比。

Result: 1. 主动探索能帮助成人克服合取因果学习的困难；2. 在效率上，合取规则的学习仍需比析取规则更多的测试次数；3. 大语言模型在假设推理上表现出色，但在探索策略的效率上通常不及人类，且遵循类似的合取推断难于析取推断的模式。

Conclusion: 主动探索能显著提升人类识别合取因果规则的能力，但合取规则的推断难度仍高于析取规则。大语言模型在假设推断上虽接近人类水平，但在探索效率上存在差异且同样表现出合取-析取推理差距。

Abstract: A long-standing finding in the causal learning literature is that adults struggle to identify conjunctive causal rules, where an effect requires the simultaneous presence of multiple causes, while performing better in disjunctive settings. However, most demonstrations of this ``conjunctive handicap'' rely on passive observation paradigms with limited evidence, where learners have no control over evidence generation. This paper asks whether this bias persists when adults are granted agency through active exploration. Using a modified ``blicket detector'' task, adult participants freely intervened to identify causal objects under conjunctive or disjunctive rule structures. We show that active exploration substantially improves adults' conjunctive causal reasoning, although conjunctive rules still require more tests to infer than disjunctive rules. We further compare human performance to a range of large language models in the same setting. While some state-of-the-art models approach human-level performance on hypothesis inference accuracy, they often exhibit less efficient exploration strategies and similar conjunctive-disjunctive performance gaps.

</details>


### [101] [You Only Index Once: Cross-Layer Sparse Attention with Shared Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06467)
*Yutao Sun,Yanqi Zhang,Li Dong,Jianyong Wang,Furu Wei*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: CLSA通过跨层共享路由索引技术，解决了长上下文大模型推理中稀疏注意力机制的效率与精度矛盾，大幅提升了推理速度和吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长上下文解码中存在效率瓶颈，结构化块稀疏方法会导致质量下降，而传统的令牌稀疏方法（Top-k routing）虽然精度高但因计算开销大导致端到端加速有限。

Method: 提出跨层稀疏注意力机制（CLSA），基于KV共享架构（如YOCO），通过单一索引器计算一次Top-k token选择并跨层复用该路由索引，从而大幅度摊销 routing 开销。

Result: CLSA在保持高精度的同时实现了显著加速，在128K上下文长度下，解码速度提升达7.6倍，总体吞吐量提升达17.1倍。

Conclusion: CLSA通过跨层共享KV缓存和路由索引，成功打破了稀疏注意力在模型性能与推理速度之间的权衡，为高性能长上下文大模型提供了一个高效的架构解决方案。

Abstract: Long-context inference in modern LLMs is increasingly constrained by decoding efficiency, especially in reasoning-heavy settings where models generate long intermediate chains of thought. Existing sparse attention methods often face a practical efficiency-quality trade-off. Structured block sparse methods typically provide stronger acceleration but incur noticeable quality loss, while token sparse methods are usually more accurate yet deliver limited end-to-end speedup because top-k routing over the full cache remains expensive. In this work, we propose cross-layer sparse attention (CLSA), which is built on top of KV-sharing architectures such as YOCO. The core idea is to share not only the KV cache across cross-decoder layers, but also the routing index.
