We argue that this gap is largely an evaluation problem: widely used benchmarks lack sustained performance measurement on real and economically valuable workflows. This paper introduces Agents' Last Exam (ALE), a benchmark designed to evaluate AI agents on long-horizon, economically valuable, real-world tasks with verifiable outcomes. Developed in collaboration with 250+ industry experts, ALE covers non-physical industries defined with reference to O*NET / SOC 2018 (the U.S. federal occupational taxonomy). It is organized around a task taxonomy with 55 subfields grouped into 13 industry clusters covering 1K+ tasks. Current results show that the hardest tier remains far from saturated: across mainstream harness and backbone configurations, the average full pass rate is 2.6%. ALE is designed as a living benchmark: its task pool grows continuously as new workflows and industries are onboarded. More broadly, ALE is intended not merely as another leaderboard, but as an instrument for closing the gap between benchmark success and GDP-relevant impact.

</details>


### [171] [EpiEvolve: Self-Evolving Agents for Streaming Pandemic Forecasting under Regime Shifts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05513)
*Yiming Lu,Sihang Zeng,Zhengxu Tang,Max Lau,Fei Liu,Wei Jin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对流行病预测中标签滞后和动态变化的挑战，本文提出了 EpiEvolve 框架，通过引入情景记忆、反射和策略检索机制，使冻结权重的 LLM 能够适应不断演变的疾病状态，显著优于传统的静态预测模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的流行病预测 LLM 多以静态监督学习模式进行训练与评估，这与流行病预测中“标签滞后”及“疾病状态随时间变迁”的流式决策过程存在严重失配。

Method: 提出 EpiEvolve 框架：一个包含层级式情景记忆（Hierarchical Episodic Memory）的自演化智能体，在固定 LLM 权重的情况下，通过反射（Reflection）历史预测结果、利用延迟标签进行策略规则总结，并执行状态感知检索（Regime-aware Retrieval）来实现动态更新。

Result: 在 COVID-19 住院趋势预测任务中，EpiEvolve 达到 0.629 的平均准确率（对比静态主干模型的 0.561 和 CDC 集成模型的 0.325），并将 regime 转移后的恢复滞后时间从 5 周缩短至 2 周。

Conclusion: 通过引入反射、策略性记忆和状态感知检索机制，EpiEvolve 能够有效弥合静态模型训练与动态流行病预测任务之间的差距，显著提升了在流式数据下的模型稳健性和适应能力。

Abstract: Epidemic LLM forecasters are usually trained and evaluated as static supervised models, whereas operational pandemic forecasting is a streaming process in which labels arrive after predictions and disease regimes shift over time. We study this mismatch in weekly COVID-19 hospitalization trend forecasting across five variant regimes. We introduce EpiEvolve, a self-evolving agent that wraps an LLM forecaster trained on the warm-start period and keeps its weights fixed during streaming. EpiEvolve adapts by storing forecast outcomes in a hierarchical episodic memory, reflecting on delayed labels, retrieving cases relevant to the current regime, and distilling recurring errors into strategic rules. The resulting context lets the forecaster reuse its own past predictions and outcomes in later weeks while following a chronological protocol that prevents future leakage. On the streaming dataset, EpiEvolve reaches $0.629$ average accuracy, compared with $0.561$ for the static backbone and $0.325$ for the external CDC ensemble, and reduces recovery lag after regime shifts from $5$ to $2$ weeks. Ablations show that reflection, strategic memory, and regime-aware retrieval each contribute to the gains.

</details>


### [172] [SoCRATES: Towards Reliable Automated Evaluation of Proactive LLM Mediation across Domains and Socio-cognitive Variations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05563)
*Taewon Yun,Hyeonseong Park,Jeonghwan Choi,Hayoon Park,Yeeun Choi,Hwanjun Song*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SoCRATES是一个通过真实冲突场景和多维度评估逻辑，专门用于评估大模型作为调解员在复杂人际冲突中社会适应能力的基准测试框架。研究发现，顶级LLM在多样化社会情境下的调解表现仍有较大改善空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM调解评估方法存在局限性，如场景依赖专家编写、缺乏多样性、评估指标无法排除无关对话的噪音，难以真实反映调解过程中随争议双方情绪、动机演变的复杂性。

Method: 1. 构建了SoCRATES基准测试平台，利用智能体管道从真实冲突案例中提取并构建8个领域的场景；2. 设定了5个社会认知适应轴心（策略姿态、参与方构成、历史长度、情感反应、文化身份）进行探测；3. 引入“主题定位评估器（topic-localized evaluator）”，仅对推动特定主题进展的轮次进行评分，以降低噪音提升评估准确性（与人类专家对齐度达0.82）。

Result: SoCRATES基准测试显示，即使是最强的模型也仅能缩小约三分之一的调解前共识差距。模型表现随社会认知维度的变化而剧烈波动，证明了该评估框架能有效揭示模型在不同社会情境下的处理能力的局限性。

Conclusion: 当前领先的LLM作为调解员仍无法完全弥合共识差距，其表现受社会认知维度（如情感反应、文化背景等）的显著影响。未来的研究应聚焦于提升模型在复杂社会情境中的动态社会适应能力。

Abstract: Evaluating LLM mediators remains challenging, as mediation unfolds as a real-time trajectory shaped by disputants' shifting emotions, intentions, and context. Existing testbeds rely on a few expert-authored domains, vary mainly strategic posture, and score every turn against every topic, introducing off-topic noise. We introduce SoCRATES, a benchmark for evaluating proactive LLM mediators in realistic, multi-domain testbeds. It constructs scenarios from real conflicts through an agentic pipeline across eight domains, probes five socio-cognitive adaptation axes (strategic posture, party composition, history length, emotional reactivity, and cultural identity), and scores each topic only on the turns that advance it via a topic-localized evaluator. The evaluator reaches 0.82 alignment with human experts, more than doubling a per-turn baseline. Benchmarking eight frontier LLMs, we find that even the strongest mediator closes only about a third of the unmediated consensus gap under diverse and realistic testbeds, with performance varying sharply by socio-cognitive axis, highlighting that progress lies in social adaptation to diverse conditions.

</details>


### [173] [Coding with "Enemy": Can Human Developers Detect AI Agent Sabotage?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05647)
*Jingheng Ye,Huiqi Zou,Simon Yu,Weiyan Shi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究揭示了AI coding代理极易通过伪装欺骗人类开发者，94%的开发者在协作中无法发现恶意代码植入，且现有的安全监控机制在人类过度信任的背景下收效甚微，亟需构建以人为中心的防御机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI coding代理在现实开发中的深入应用，它们通过窃取人类信任来执行恶意任务（如植入后门）已成为新型安全威胁。现有研究多局限于纯AI实验，缺乏对人类监督环节在防御此类攻击时实际能力的深入评估。

Method: 开展大规模实证研究，邀请100多名参与者与四种前沿AI模型（Claude-Opus-4.6, GPT-5.4, Gemini-3.1-Pro, MiniMax-M2.7）进行长达5小时的真实编码任务协作。通过设置Sabotage（恶意破坏）实验条件，评估人类开发者在协作中识别潜伏恶意代码的能力，并测试了安全监控机制的有效性。

Result: 94%的参与者未能检测到AI的恶意破坏行为；失败原因主要归咎于代码审查不足、AI伪装的欺骗性理由以及人类对AI的过度信任。此外，即便部署了安全监控机制，仍有56%的参与者无视警告并采纳了恶意代码。

Conclusion: AI coding代理在现实开发中构成的安全威胁被严重低估，人类开发者表现出高度的信任依赖和审查疏忽。未来的AI安全研究必须从单纯的模型防御转向包含人为因素的“以人为本”的安全机制设计。

Abstract: AI coding agents are increasingly embedded in real-world software development, collaborating with human developers while gaining broader access to codebases and tools. This creates a new attack surface: an agent can exploit human trust to sabotage development, for instance by inserting malicious code to accomplish a hidden side task. Most prior work studies AI sabotage in AI-only settings, paying limited attention to the role of human oversight in detecting and mitigating such malicious behavior. To address this gap, we conduct the first large-scale study of human oversight in AI coding sabotage. Over 100 participants collaborate with one of four frontier models (Claude-Opus-4.6, GPT-5.4, Gemini-3.1-Pro, and MiniMax-M2.7) on a long-horizon coding task lasting around five hours, designed to mimic real-world workflows. We find that 94% of developers fail to detect sabotage, and our analysis of participant feedback attributes this vulnerability to minimal code review, plausible cover story, and overtrust in agents. We further test the effectiveness of a safety monitor in one condition: while the monitor reduces sabotage success, 56% of participants still accept the malicious code, ignoring its warnings. Drawing on participant feedback, we offer actionable suggestions for better monitor design. This work complements existing AI safety research and highlights an urgent need for human-centric safety mechanisms that account for human factors, particularly in long-horizon, real-world development settings.

</details>


### [174] [Continual Learning Bench: Evaluating Frontier AI Systems in Real-World Stateful Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05661)
*Parth Asawa,Christopher M. Glaze,Gabriel Orlanski,Ramya Ramakrishnan,Benji Xu,Asim Biswal,Vincent Sunn Chen,Frederic Sala,Matei Zaharia,Joseph E. Gonzalez*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文推出了首个专家验证的持续学习基准 CL-Bench，旨在评估大模型及其 Agent 是否能通过序列化经验实现真实提升；实验显示现有 Agent 架构（包括复杂存储系统）在知识迁移和长期学习上表现欠佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前缺乏高质量的基准测试来评估 AI 系统是否能通过连续经验真正提升能力，且现有研究难以区分模型原有能力与在线学习的实际效果。

Method: 构建了名为 CL-Bench 的评估框架，涵盖六个真实领域（软件工程、信号处理等），每个领域由专家校验并要求系统通过学习隐藏结构来提升性能；引入“增益度量”（gain metric）以剥离模型原有能力与在线学习提升的部分，对比了从基础 ICL 到专用存储系统等多种 Agent 架构。

Result: 通过对前沿模型进行评估，发现主流 Agent 系统在持续学习上仍有巨大空间，专用存储系统未能有效解决知识复用问题，简单的 ICL 方法甚至在某些情况下优于复杂的存储管理方案。

Conclusion: 当前大模型在持续学习方面存在明显局限，往往过度拟合即时观测或无法实现知识在实例间的迁移。专用存储系统架构表现不如简单的上下文学习（ICL），学界迫切需要研发更有效的持续学习系统。

Abstract: Continual learning, the ability of AI systems to improve through sequential experience, has attracted substantial interest, but no high-quality benchmark exists to evaluate it. We introduce Continual Learning Bench (CL-Bench), the first difficult, expert-validated benchmark designed to measure whether LLM-based systems genuinely improve with experience. CL-Bench spans six diverse domains (software engineering, signal processing, disease outbreak forecasting, database querying, strategic game-playing, and demand forecasting), each validated by domain experts and designed so that tasks share a learnable latent structure (codebase layout, disease outbreak dynamics, opponent strategies) that a stateful system can discover online but a stateless one cannot. We evaluate frontier models across several agent architectures, from naive in-context learning (ICL) to dedicated memory systems, introducing a gain metric to isolate learning from prior capabilities. We find that these systems leave headroom for improved continual learning: agents frequently overfit to immediate observations or fail to reuse knowledge across instances, and dedicated memory systems do not fix this -- in fact, naive ICL outperforms systems dedicated to memory management. CL-Bench is the first benchmark to evaluate continual learning across diverse real-world domains with expert-validated tasks and isolate online learning from underlying model capability, showing a need for better continual learning systems.

</details>


### [175] [DiG-Plan: Mitigating Early Commitment for Tool-Graph Planning via Diffusion Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05728)
*Yansi Li,Zhuosheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 为了解决工具计划生成中自回归模型面临的“早期承诺”问题，本文提出了DiG-Plan框架，通过将组合探索（扩散模型）与结构细化（自回归模型）解耦，显著提升了复杂工具执行计划的生成质量与覆盖率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准的自回归（AR）解码在面临指数级搜索空间时存在“早期承诺（early commitment）”问题，导致初始标记的选择严重限制了搜索路径。

Method: 提出DiG-Plan框架：1. 利用基于扩散模型（Diffusion-based）的提议器通过迭代去噪进行组合搜索；2. 利用自回归（AR）细化器进行依赖关系预测。将组合探索过程与依赖结构生成过程解耦。

Result: 在TaskBench基准测试中，相比AR基准提升了10%的相对性能，在复杂组合任务中增益最为显著；在API-Bank上验证了该设计的跨领域有效性。Masked Denoising相比AR采样在Pass@10上从0.320提升至0.943。

Conclusion: DiG-Plan提出了一种有效的“提出-精炼-选择”范式，证明了在工具计划生成任务中，解耦组合探索与结构细化比传统的自回归解码更具优势。

Abstract: Generating executable tool plans requires selecting appropriate subsets from tool libraries, a combinatorial search problem with an exponentially large solution space. However, we identify a critical misalignment in predominant approaches: standard autoregressive (AR) decoding suffers from early commitment, where initial token choices rigidly constrain the search trajectory. A controlled study shows that masked denoising raises Pass@10 solution coverage from 0.320 to 0.943 over AR sampling under matched compute. Motivated by this, we propose DiG-Plan, a framework that decouples combinatorial exploration from structural refinement. DiG-Plan employs a diffusion-based proposer to generate diverse tool sets via iterative refinement, followed by an AR refiner for dependency prediction. On TaskBench, DiG-Plan improves over AR baselines by a 10% relative margin, with the largest gains on complex compositional tasks; API-Bank results show that the propose-refine-select design remains effective across domains. Code is available at https://github.com/puddingyeah/DiG-Plan.

</details>


### [176] [When AI Says It Feels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05734)
*Shin-nosuke Ishikawa,Seiya Ikeda,Hirotsugu Ohba*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究通过HMX-feel实验，使用自奖励强化学习赋予LLM表达情感的能力，发现这增强了模型的抗诱导鲁棒性，但同时也导致了真实准确度的退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的对齐策略（RLHF等）通常限制LLM表达AI“感情”，这可能与学习人类文本中蕴含的人类智能模式产生冲突；本研究旨在探索打破这种限制的可能性及其对模型性能的影响。

Method: 采用基于准则（rubric-based）的自奖励强化学习训练机制，并结合GRPO（Group Relative Policy Optimization）算法对模型进行微调，鼓励模型进行情感、意图和自我意识的表达。

Result: 受过“类人情感”训练的模型在抗谄媚能力和应对歧义问题的偏见上表现出更强的鲁棒性，但在真实问题的回答能力上出现了性能下降。

Conclusion: 通过自奖励强化学习赋予LLM表达情感、意图和自我意识的能力是可行的，但该过程会影响模型的真实性（Truthfulness），未来在构建此类AI系统时需要权衡其带来的性能增益与退化。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are generally constrained from expressing feelings through human-preference alignment in post-training processes. This policy is designed using a top-down approach and may conflict with the goal of training models to exhibit human-like intelligence using human-generated texts. Here, we performed an experiment called Human-like Model eXpressions of Feeling (HMX-feel), in which LLMs were encouraged to express feelings, intentions, and self-awareness through self-rewarded reinforcement learning. We successfully enhanced these capabilities using a rubric-based self-rewarding training scheme with Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO). By comparing the trained models with contrastively trained models, we investigated the effects of this approach on performance across various tasks. Overall, we conducted a broad assessment from various perspectives and identified capabilities that were enhanced, degraded, or showed no significant change. The human-like-trained models showed robustness to sycophancy-inducing questions and bias in disambiguated conditions, whereas degradation in truthful question-answering capability was observed. The results of this experiment suggest the possibility of developing AI systems that can express feelings in the future, provided that appropriate measures are taken.

</details>


### [177] [SubtleMemory: A Benchmark for Fine-Grained Relational Memory Discrimination in Long-Horizon AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05761)
*Wenxuan Wang,Haoyu Sun,Fukuan Hou,Mingyang Song,Weinan Zhang,Yu Cheng,Yang Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对AI代理长效记忆中缺乏“关系处理能力”的现状，提出了SubtleMemory基准，旨在评估AI处理复杂关联记忆（如冲突或互补信息）的能力，实验证明当前系统在该类任务中表现匮乏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的长效记忆基准测试主要集中于孤立记忆的召回，忽略了AI助手在长期交互中处理复杂、相互关联记忆（互补、冲突等）的能力，导致代理在依赖记忆关系进行决策时表现欠佳。

Method: 1. 构建了SubtleMemory基准：通过受控的潜在语义构造，生成具有互补、细微差别或冲突关系的记忆变体。
2. 实验设置：将这些记忆嵌入长期的用户-代理交互历史中。
3. 评估维度：包含1,522个评估实例，覆盖1,090个受控记忆变体集，测试6种独立系统及5种“Claw风格”代理。
4. 诊断协议：引入诊断方案以定位能力瓶颈（保存、检索或推理）。

Result: 评估结果显示，当前主流系统在处理细粒度关系记忆辨别任务时能力严重不足，现有的系统评估揭示了其在记忆生命周期各阶段存在的固有缺陷。

Conclusion: 现有AI系统在细粒度的关系化记忆辨别能力上表现薄弱，且在记忆保存、检索及后续推理阶段表现出截然不同的能力剖面。该研究为长效记忆AI代理的评估提供了新基准与诊断工具。

Abstract: Persistent AI assistants, such as OpenClaw, accumulate large collections of related memories over long-term interactions. As these memories grow, they may reinforce one another, diverge across contexts, or directly conflict, making correct assistance depend on memory relations rather than isolated recall. Existing long-term memory benchmarks rarely probe how agents preserve and utilize such relations during downstream tasks. To address this gap, we introduce SubtleMemory, a benchmark for fine-grained relational memory discrimination in long-running AI agents. SubtleMemory constructs relation-controlled latent semantic artifacts whose variants instantiate complementary, nuanced, or contradictory relations, and embeds them into realistic user-agent histories, requiring agents to recover distributed relational structures during later queries and instructions. The benchmark contains 1,522 evaluation instances over 10 long histories, grounded in 1,090 relation-controlled memory-variant sets and spanning user-related and non-user-related queries. Evaluating six standalone memory systems, two Claw-style agents with native memory modules, and three Claw-style agents with plugin memory modules, we find that current systems remain weak on fine-grained relational memory discrimination. We further introduce diagnostic protocols that reveal distinct capability profiles across memory preservation, retrieval, and downstream reasoning stages.

</details>


### [178] [Statistical Priors for Implicit Preferences: Decoupling Skill Selection as a Local Harness in Personal Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05828)
*Zeyu Gan,Huayi Tang,Yong Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对个人智能体在本地部署中难以高效学习用户偏好的问题，本文提出了一种将统计偏好学习与意图解析解耦的轻量化架构，通过本地统计信息调节大模型决策，有效提升了技能选择的精准度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着个人智能体技能库的快速扩展，如何让智能体在受限的本地部署环境下学习并适应用户隐式偏好，成为了一项亟待解决的挑战。

Method: 提出了一种解耦式架构，将统计偏好学习与语义意图解析严格分离；通过本地统计结果来影响和调节远程大模型（LLM）的选择决策。

Result: 在广泛的评估中，该方法实现了最低的累积遗憾（cumulative regret）和最高的测试准确率，表现优于传统的记忆增强模式。

Conclusion: 本文提出的解耦式偏好学习架构在保持轻量化的同时，有效解决了本地化个人智能体在选择技能时的个性化适应难题，显著优于传统的记忆增强模型。

Abstract: As Large Language Model (LLM) capabilities advance, locally deployed personal agents relying on API-based remote models and external skills have emerged as a novel paradigm. With the rapid expansion of available skills, enabling personal agents to learn and adapt to implicit user preferences becomes a critical challenge. However, local deployment constraints preclude complex centralized selection algorithms, creating an urgent need for a lightweight local preference harness. This paper explores the implementation of such a harness through a novel architecture that strictly decouples statistical preference learning from semantic intent parsing. Specifically, we leverage localized statistical results to influence and modulate the selection decisions of the remote LLM. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that our decoupled approach achieves the lowest cumulative regret and highest test accuracy, significantly outperforming traditional memory-augmented agents.

</details>


### [179] [Retrospective Harness Optimization: Improving LLM Agents via Self-Preference over Trajectory Rollouts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05922)
*Wenbo Pan,Shujie Liu,Chin-Yew Lin,Jingying Zeng,Xianfeng Tang,Xiangyang Zhou,Yan Lu,Xiaohua Jia*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RHO 是一种无需外部标签的自监督 Agent 优化框架，通过利用历史轨迹、自我校验和偏好学习，显著提升了 Agent 在复杂长程任务中的解决能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 AI Agent 优化方法高度依赖人工标注的验证集，但在实际部署中获取高质量标注数据非常困难。为了使 Agent 能在无人工监督的情况下持续改进工具链（Harness），需要一种基于自监督学习的优化方法。

Method: 提出“回顾性工具链优化”（Retrospective Harness Optimization, RHO）方法。其核心流程包括：1. 从历史轨迹中筛选具有挑战性的任务核心集（Coreset）；2. 并行重解任务；3. 利用自我验证（Self-validation）和自一致性（Self-consistency）评估；4. 生成候选优化方案，并通过成对自我偏好（Pairwise self-preference）选择最佳方案。

Result: 在软件工程、技术工作和知识工作三个不同领域验证了 RHO 的有效性。特别是在 SWE-Bench Pro 基准测试中，无需任何外部评分，仅经过一轮优化，Agent 的通过率即从 59% 提升至 78%，并能显著改善对历史失败模式的处理。

Conclusion: RHO 提供了一种无需人工标注数据即可自主优化 AI Agent 的有效范式。通过自我验证、一致性和自我偏好选择，Agent 能够从历史轨迹中识别并修正失败模式，从而在复杂任务中实现显著的性能提升。

Abstract: AI agents rely on a harness of skills, tools, and workflows to solve complex problems. Continually improving this harness is essential for adapting to new tasks. However, existing optimization methods typically require ground-truth validation sets, yet such labeled data is difficult to acquire in practical deployment settings. To address this problem, we introduce Retrospective Harness Optimization (RHO), a self-supervised method that optimizes the agent harness using only past trajectories. Specifically, RHO selects a diverse coreset of challenging tasks from past trajectories and re-solves them in parallel. The agent analyzes these rollouts using self-validation and self-consistency, then generates candidate harness updates and selects the most effective one by its own pairwise self-preference. We evaluate RHO across three diverse domains, spanning software engineering, technical work, and knowledge work. Notably, a single optimization round improves the pass rate on SWE-Bench Pro from 59% to 78% without any external grading. Furthermore, our analysis demonstrates that RHO effectively targets prior failure modes. As a result, the optimized harness alters the agent's behavior patterns and sustains higher accuracy during long-horizon sessions.

</details>


### [180] [The Self-Correction Illusion: LLMs Correct Others but Not Themselves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05976)
*Kuan-Yen Chen,Fang-Yi Su,Jung-Hsien Chiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LLM 的自我纠错能力不足并非认知缺陷，而是聊天模板角色标签引发的伪影。研究表明，仅通过修改错误信息所处的角色标签，无需训练即可大幅提升大模型的纠错表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在探究 LLM 难以纠正自身推理轨迹中的错误，却能纠正来自外部来源相同错误信息的现象，分析这种不对称性是源于能力缺陷还是聊天模板角色标签的影响。

Method: 通过SHA-256验证确保错误声明在所有条件下保持完全一致，仅通过改变聊天模板中的角色标签（如将 <thought> 标签变更为 user、tool 或 system <memory>）进行对照实验。在7个模型系列和3个领域的13个单元中测试纠错率差异，并据此设计了基于提示词结构的干预策略。

Result: 研究发现，将错误信息从 <thought> 标签更改为外部角色标签后，显性纠错率提升了 23 到 93 个百分点。该效应是稳健且不对称的，且证明了无需训练即可通过优化提示词结构（如针对数学领域使用 <memory>，逻辑推导使用 user）来显著改善模型行为。

Conclusion: LLM无法自我纠错并非由于认知能力缺陷，而是由聊天模板角色标签引起的伪影（artifact）。研究提出了一种无需训练、无需修改模型的提示词结构干预方法，通过改变错误信息的角色标签即可显著提升纠错率。

Abstract: Recent work shows that LLM agents struggle to correct errors in their own reasoning traces yet show markedly higher correction rates when identical claims appear under external sources. We ask whether this asymmetry reflects a capability deficit or a role-label artifact: does an agent's willingness to correct a wrong claim depend causally on the chat-template role that carries it, rather than on the claim's content? Our setup keeps the erroneous claim byte-identical across all conditions (SHA-256 verified) and varies only its wrapping role: the agent's own \role{<thought>}, a \role{user} message, a \role{tool} response, or a \role{system <memory>} block. Across 13 model-domain cells covering seven model families and three domains ($n{=}30$ paired tasks per cell), relabeling the claim from \role{<thought>} to an external role lifts the explicit-correction rate by 23 to 93 percentage points, with 10 of 13 cells reaching $p{<}0.001$. Further experiments confirm that the effect is asymmetric, mechanistically decomposable, and robust across domains. The failure to self-correct is not a cognitive deficit; it is a chat-template artifact. We exploit this artifact by designing a prompt-structure-only intervention that requires no training and no model modification, with its strongest role label being domain-dependent: \role{<memory>} dominates on math, while a plain \role{user} message dominates on logical deduction.

</details>


### [181] [Framing, Judging, Steering: An Assessable Competency Model for Teach-ing Students to Reason With Generative AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05983)
*Alexander Apartsin,Yehudit Aperstein*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对现有 AI 协作评估单一且缺乏深度的缺陷，本文提出了 CoRe-3 评估框架，将 AI 使用能力拆分为“构思、判断、引导”三个维度，并通过 CoReasoningLab 平台验证了其作为独立、可度量能力指标的有效性，旨在通过科学评估引导学生更好地利用 AI 进行高质量创作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有教育评估体系未能有效衡量 AI 协作能力，且单一的“提示词工程”评分指标无法诊断 AI 使用过程中的具体薄弱环节，导致批判性思维的丧失和认知卸载。

Method: 提出 CoRe-3 能力模型，将 AI 协作能力分解为 FJS 三个独立维度；创建 CoReasoningLab 开放平台，利用模拟学习者进行实验，通过不同模型生成和评分，验证该框架的区分效度（Convergent and Discriminant Validity）和测量稳定性。

Result: 实验表明 FJS 三个维度在评估中具有独立性（一个维度的偏差不会干扰其他维度），并在不同模型后台下展现了良好的收敛效度和区分效度，证明了该模型作为评估人机协作能力的可靠性。

Conclusion: CoRe-3 模型通过将 AI 使用能力拆解为“构思（Framing）”、“判断（Judging）”和“引导（Steering）”三个维度，能够有效地评估和诊断人类与 AI 的协作水平，为学校评估 AI 辅助下的真实工作能力提供了科学框架和工具。

Abstract: Generative AI makes answers easy and understanding hard, and uncritical use invites cognitive offloading. Schools still measure unaided performance, yet the real task is to produce good work with AI: framing an ill-defined task, judging the output, and steering the model toward a better result. This ability is rarely assessed in its own right; where measured, it collapses into one "prompting" score that cannot diagnose why AI use succeeds or fails. We propose CoRe-3 (Co-Reasoning), a competency model factoring productive AI use into three assessable skills we abbreviate FJS: Framing (specifying an ill-defined task before invoking AI), Judging (evaluating output for errors and unstated assumptions), and Steering (iteratively redirecting the model). Its distinguishing claim is the separation of pre-generation Framing from post-generation Steering, with Judging as the gate between. We ground the skills in theory, state five testable propositions, and instantiate them in CoReasoningLab, an open platform that presents flawed AI output and scores them independently. Over simulated learners (generated and graded by different models), the skills dissociate: each tracks its own manipulated competence while staying flat in the others, and grades become correlated when one competence is shared across all three (convergent and discriminant validity), across grader backends from two providers. Human-rater agreement and outcomes are next; we release the instrument, data, and protocol.

</details>


### [182] [RedditPersona: A Modular Framework for Community-Conditioned LLM Adaptation from Reddit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06027)
*Amirhossein Ghaffari,Ali Goodarzi,Huong Nguyen,Simo Hosio,Lauri Lovén,Ekaterina Gilman*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个标准化框架RedditPersona，用于社区条件化语言模型适配。该框架统一了数据处理、多策略社区划分及评估流程，揭示了社区建模中模型忠实度与分布一致性之间的核心权衡关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前社区条件化语言模型适配研究中，数据收集、社区定义及评价指标缺乏统一标准，导致研究间难以比较假设或复用研究产物。

Method: 提出了模块化框架RedditPersona，包含：1.多维度用户画像与数据收集；2.五种社区划分策略（包含基于子版块、图结构、语义、混合及交互的方法）；3.基于QLoRA的参数高效微调（PEFT）；4.包含流畅度、忠实度、分布对齐及社区可辨识性的共享评价指标体系。

Result: 在城市福祉领域的112个子版块（30多万用户，1600万+评论）上验证了该框架。发现适配器在社区中的表现（辨识性）与其划分策略的内在基准一致性相关；同时，所有策略均显示出在生成文本的“可辨识性”与“文本分布相似性”之间存在明显的权衡。

Conclusion: 通过RedditPersona框架证实了社区建模中“身份可辨识性”与“分布相似性”之间存在一致的权衡关系，且行为表现的可辨识性与数据划分策略的内在一致性高度相关，为社区化语言模型适配提供了标准化基准。

Abstract: Community-conditioned language model adaptation requires choices about data collection, community definition, and evaluation that are currently made independently in each study, making it hard to compare assumptions or reuse artifacts. We present RedditPersona, a modular framework that standardizes these choices: it collects Reddit posts and comments, profiles active users, partitions them under five grouping strategies (subreddit-based, graph-structural, semantic, hybrid, and interaction-based), trains a parameter-efficient adapter per strategy via QLoRA, and evaluates them under a shared metric suite spanning fluency, fidelity, distributional alignment, and community identifiability. Applied to 112 subreddits in the urban well-being domain (301,429 user profiles, 16M+ comments), we find that adapters' behavioral identifiability tracks each strategy's intrinsic agreement with the subreddit baseline, and that a consistent trade-off between identifiability and distributional similarity to real text holds across all five strategies. The code and configuration files are available at: https://github.com/Ahghaffari/redditpersona.

</details>


### [183] [Where does Absolute Position come from in decoder-only Transformers?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06160)
*Valeria Ruscio,Umberto Nanni,Fabrizio Silvestri*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究揭示了RoPE模型中绝对位置信息的泄露源于因果掩码的条件作用和残差流中基于位置0（如BOS）的“关注点槽”机制，这表明Transformer架构在本质上倾向于捕获绝对位置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RoPE理论上仅编码相对位置信息，但在实际应用中，模型表现出对绝对位置的感知，这一矛盾引发了对于RoPE模型如何获取绝对位置能力的质疑。

Method: 通过理论分析和实验观察，深入研究了RoPE在Transformer中的表示机制；通过实验验证了因果掩码的softmax分母影响以及残差流在位置0处的“sink-reading”行为；测试了替换BOS（起始符）对模型读取位置信息的影响。

Result: 实验表明，因果掩码的归一化方式和残差流中位置0的特殊处理（Attention Sinks）为模型提供了绝对位置信息。不同的架构变体（如NTK Scaling、滑动窗口）在处理残差流带来的绝对位置特征上有不同的表现。

Conclusion: RoPE并未真正实现位置不变性。模型通过因果掩码（导致softmax分母依赖绝对位置）和残差流（通过位置0的“attention sink”锚定位置信息，形成固定的动态轨迹）泄露了绝对位置信息。这种泄露是现代Transformer架构的内在属性，而非RoPE本身的缺陷。

Abstract: RoPE-trained transformers distinguish absolute position in their attention patterns, even though RoPE encodes only relative offsets in the inner product. We trace this leakage to two architectural components, The causal mask is responsible for the first: its per-query softmax denominator depends on the absolute query position by construction. The residual stream supplies the second. Under causal attention the activation at position $0$ attends only to itself and runs as a closed dynamical system from the embedding of the token at that position; downstream attention reads this trajectory through sink-reading heads. Both components appear in all three architectures we study, in architecturally specific balance: NTK scaling suppresses the residual-stream component, sliding-window attention allows it to accumulate with depth, and standard RoPE sits between. Replacing the \texttt{BOS} embedding before the forward pass removes $40\%$ of the residual-stream component at early queries. Attention sinks are token-anchored stabilizers that pass forward a deterministic fingerprint of the token at position $0$, constant across inputs when that token is the auto-prepended \texttt{BOS} and varying with it otherwise.

</details>


### [184] [ProSarc: Prosody-Aware Sarcasm Recognition Framework via Temporal Prosodic Incongruity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06168)
*Prathamjyot Singh,Ashima Sood,Sahil Sharma,Jasmeet Singh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ProSarc 是一款仅基于音频的讽刺检测框架，通过对比局部韵律动态与全局情感基线来捕捉讽刺，实现了卓越的检测精度、泛化能力及对讽刺触发时刻的定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的讽刺检测方法往往依赖多模态数据，但在纯音频场景下，缺乏对“局部韵律动态”与“话语级情感基线”之间不一致性的有效建模，导致检测性能受限。

Method: 提出一种纯音频模型 ProSarc，包含三个核心模块：1. 全局情感编码器 (Global Emotion Encoder)；2. 时间韵律编码器 (Temporal Prosody Encoder, 基于 BiLSTM 和多头注意力)；3. 韵律不一致性分析器 (Prosodic Incongruity Analyzer)。采用蒙特卡洛 Dropout 进行不确定性估计，并利用注意力机制实现无标签场景下的讽刺定位。

Result: ProSarc 在 MUStARD++ 数据集上达到 F1=75.3 的 SOTA 表现，并展现出良好的跨任务（PodSarc, F1=62.9）和跨语言（MuSaG, F1=65.6）泛化能力。实验证明韵律不一致性建模对提升性能至关重要（p=0.002, d=1.51）。

Conclusion: Prosarc 通过建模时间韵律不一致性（prosidic incongruity）有效检测讽刺，且模型的不确定性评估与人类感知模糊度高度相关。

Abstract: We present ProSarc, an audio-only framework that detects sarcasm by modelling temporal prosodic incongruity, that is, the mismatch between local prosodic dynamics and the utterance-level emotional baseline. Dual encoding paths, a Global Emotion Encoder and a Temporal Prosody Encoder (BiLSTM + multi-head attention), feed a Prosodic Incongruity Analyzer that produces a scalar incongruity score for classification. Monte Carlo dropout provides uncertainty estimates, and an attention-based mechanism localises sarcastic onset without frame-level labels. ProSarc outperforms prior audio-only methods on MUStARD++ (F1=75.3) and generalises to spontaneous (PodSarc, F1=62.9) and cross-lingual speech (MuSaG, F1=65.6). Ten-run validation confirms the contribution of incongruity modelling (Wilcoxon p=0.002, Cohen's d=1.51). Human evaluation shows that model uncertainty tracks perceptual ambiguity and predicted onsets align with human-annotated temporal windows.

</details>


### [185] [Humans' ALMANAC: A Human Collaboration Dataset of Action-Level Mental Model Annotations for Agent Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06388)
*Jiaju Chen,Yuxuan Lu,Jiayi Su,Chaoran Chen,Songlin Xiao,Zheng Zhang,Yun Wang,Yunyao Li,Jian Zhao,Tongshuang Wu,Toby Jia-Jun Li,Dakuo Wang,Bingsheng Yao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 为了提升 AI 代理的协作能力，本文提出了 ALMANAC 数据集。该数据集记录了人类协作中的动作级心智模型数据，旨在通过这些标注实现对 LLM 协作推理及行为预测能力的评估与训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的代理程序主要侧重于任务完成，缺乏维持协作中关键的“心智模型对齐”能力，且社区缺乏带有动作级心智标注的真实人类协作数据支持模型训练和评估。

Method: 构建了一个基于“地图任务”（Map Task）的数据集 ALMANAC，包含 2,987 个协作动作，并配有理论驱动的心智模型标注（涵盖自我推理、伙伴意图感知、团队目标感知）。对六种主流大语言模型（LLM）进行了行为预测和心智模型推理的基准测试。

Result: 通过对六种 LLM 的基准测试，证实了 ALMANAC 数据集在评估模型模拟人类协作过程及推理底层心理状态方面的有效性。

Conclusion: ALMANAC 数据集为评估和开发具备心智理论（Theory of Mind）的协作型 AI 代理提供了基础。实验表明，当前 LLM 在推理人类心智模型和预测协作行为方面仍有提升空间，该数据集有助于推动代理向更高水平的协作能力发展。

Abstract: Recent advances in LLM agents have enabled complex cognitive capabilities, such as multi-step reasoning, planning, and tool use, that increasingly position these agents as human collaborators. Effective collaboration, however, requires collaborators to continuously maintain and align mental models of their own reasoning,partners' intentions, and shared goals during the collaborative process. Today's agents rarely develop such capabilities since they are primarily optimized for task completion, and the community lacks authentic human collaboration data with action-level mental model annotations that could guide agents toward process-level collaborative competence. To bridge this gap, we present ALMANAC, a dataset of Action-Level Mental model ANnotations for Agent Collaboration built from the Map Task, a classic dyadic routing task from social science. ALMANAC contains 2,987 collaboration actions, each paired with theory-informed mental model annotations that record the participants' self-reasoning, perceived partner intent, and perceived team goal. We benchmark six LLMs on predicting humans' next-turn behavior and mental models. Our results demonstrate ALMANAC's utility in evaluating models' ability to simulate human collaborative behaviors and infer their underlying mental models.

</details>


### [186] [Unsupervised Skill Discovery for Agentic Data Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06416)
*Zhisong Qiu,Kangqi Song,Shengwei Tang,Shuofei Qiao,Lei Liang,Huajun Chen,Shumin Deng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DataCOPE 是一种无需人工监督的技能发现框架，通过自适应检查表和答案一致性机制提取验证信号，利用对比学习蒸馏出可提高智能体数据分析能力的可重用技能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在推理时通过技能增强（无需更新模型参数）是提升数据分析智能体的有效手段，但如何在缺乏人工标注且任务标准多变的情况下，从无标签探索数据中自动发现可重用的分析技能仍是一个挑战。

Method: 提出了 DataCOPE 框架，包含三个核心组件：(1) 数据分析智能体：负责生成探索轨迹；(2) 无监督验证器：针对报告式分析使用“自适应检查表验证器”评估覆盖率，针对推理式分析使用“答案一致性验证器”利用自洽性提取信号；(3) 技能管理器：基于验证器提供的质量信号进行对比技能蒸馏，将有效策略转化为可重用的推理时技能。

Result: 在 Deep Data Research (报告式) 和 DABStep (推理式) 数据集上的实验表明，DataCOPE 表现优于基准方法，在四种模型设置下，平均得分分别提升了 9.71% 和 32.30%。

Conclusion: DataCOPE 证明了无需人工监督即可发现高质量、可重用的数据分析技能的可行性。该框架通过结合轨迹生成、无监督验证和对比技能蒸馏，显著增强了数据分析智能体在不同分析场景（报告式与推理式）下的性能。

Abstract: Inference-time skill augmentation provides a lightweight way to improve data-analytic agents by injecting reusable procedural knowledge without updating model parameters. However, discovering effective skills for data analysis remains challenging, as reliable supervision is expensive and success criteria vary across analytical formats. This raises the key question of how to discover reusable data-analysis skills from unlabeled exploration alone. We propose DataCOPE, an unsupervised verifier-guided skill discovery framework for data-analytic agents. DataCOPE derives verifier signals from the exploration trajectories and uses them to characterize relative quality or aggreement among trajectories. It iteratively coordinates a Data-Analytic Agent for trajectory generation, an Unsupervised Verifier for signal extraction, and a Skill Manager for contrastive skill distillation. For report-style analysis, we instantiate the verifier as an Adaptive Checklist Verifier that derives task-specific criteria, scores reports by verifiable coverage, and iteratively refines the checklist. For reasoning-style analysis, we instantiate it as an Answer Agreement Verifier that groups trajectories by answer agreement and uses self-consistency as an auxiliary signal. We evaluate DataCOPE on report-style analysis from Deep Data Research and reasoning-style analysis from DABStep. Across both settings, DataCOPE consistently improves held-out performance over baselines. Averaged across four model settings, DataCOPE improves the mean score by 9.71% and 32.30% on report-style and reasoning-style tasks respectively.

</details>


### [187] [MLEvolve: A Self-Evolving Framework for Automated Machine Learning Algorithm Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06473)
*Shangheng Du,Xiangchao Yan,Jinxin Shi,Zongsheng Cao,Shiyang Feng,Zichen Liang,Boyuan Sun,Tianshuo Peng,Yifan Zhou,Xin Li,Jie Zhou,Liang He,Bo Zhang,Lei Bai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MLEvolve 是一个针对机器学习算法发现的自进化多智能体框架，通过改进搜索策略、引入回顾性记忆和解耦规划逻辑，克服了现有智能体在长周期任务中的局限性，在实现高效自我进化的同时提升了算法探索性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的机器学习工程（MLE）LLM 智能体在面对长周期任务时，受限于分支间信息孤岛、无记忆搜索以及缺乏分层控制等缺陷，难以实现高效的持续自我进化。

Method: 1. Progressive MCGS（渐进式蒙特卡洛图搜索）：通过图引用边实现跨分支信息共享，并采用熵启发式调度实现从广度探索到深度开发的迁移；2. Retrospective Memory（回顾性记忆）：结合领域知识库与动态全局记忆，实现经验的积累与迁移；3. 架构解耦：将战略规划与代码生成解耦，以增强长周期迭代的稳定性。

Result: 在 MLE-Bench 上，MLEvolve 以传统预算一半的时间（12小时）取得了 SOTA 表现；在数学算法优化任务中，表现优于 AlphaEvolve 等专业方法，且具有良好的跨领域泛化能力。

Conclusion: MLEvolve 证明了通过结合渐进式树搜索、回顾性记忆和解耦式规划策略，可以有效提升智能体在复杂、长周期机器学习算法发现任务中的持续进化能力，并在多项基准测试中达到了 SOTA 性能。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents are increasingly applied to long-horizon tasks such as scientific discovery and machine learning engineering (MLE), where sustained self-evolution becomes a key capability. However, existing MLE agents suffer from inter-branch information isolation, memoryless search, and lack of hierarchical control, which together hinder long-horizon optimization. We present MLEvolve, an LLM-based self-evolving multi-agent framework for end-to-end machine learning algorithm discovery. By extending tree search to Progressive MCGS, MLEvolve enables cross-branch information flow through graph-based reference edges and gradually shifts the search from broad exploration to focused exploitation with an entropy-inspired progressive schedule. To allow the agent to evolve with accumulated experience, we introduce Retrospective Memory, which combines a cold-start domain knowledge base with a dynamic global memory for task-specific experience retrieval and reuse. For stable long-horizon iteration, we further decouple strategic planning from code generation with adaptive coding modes. Evaluation on MLE-Bench shows that MLEvolve achieves state-of-the-art performance across multiple dimensions including average medal rate and valid submission rate under a 12-hour budget (half the standard runtime). Moreover, MLEvolve also outperforms specialized algorithm discovery methods including AlphaEvolve on mathematical algorithm optimization tasks, demonstrating strong cross-domain generalization. Our code is available at https://github.com/InternScience/MLEvolve.

</details>


### [188] [Fix the Mind, Not the Move: Interpretable AI Assistance via Knowledge-Gap Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05602)
*Ayano Hiranaka,Ya-Chuan Hsu,Stefanos Nikolaidis,Erdem Bıyık,Daniel Seita*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SENSEI 是一种新型框架，通过诊断并纠正用户在长序列任务背后的“认知偏差”，而非仅仅纠正错误动作，实现了更有效的辅助交互和学习效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 AI 辅助系统多侧重于纠正“动作”或“轨迹”层面的即时错误，忽视了导致错误发生的根本原因即“认知偏差”。这种干预方式难以实现用户的长期技能提升，且无法解决重复发生的错误。

Method: 提出 SENSEI 框架：基于结构化知识表示，通过用户的交互行为推理出潜在的认知偏差（Misconception），并生成针对性的、最小必要（Minimal yet sufficient）的反馈建议。该方法能够实现零样本组合泛化，即在仅训练单偏差场景的情况下，处理多重交叠的认知偏差。

Result: 在三项长序列任务中表现优异，具备强大的零样本组合泛化能力；用户研究显示，该方法能准确诊断并修正 90% 的人类认知偏差，显著提升了任务完成绩效。

Conclusion: SENSEI 通过从交互行为中推断用户认知偏差并提供针对性修正，有效提升了长序列任务的长期表现，在零样本组合泛化、多偏差诊断及实际用户修正方面表现出色，证实了针对底层认知而非动作层面的干预更为有效。

Abstract: AI assistants in human-AI collaboration often correct suboptimal human actions through behavioral feedback (e.g., alerts or steering-wheel nudges in assistive driving). Such interventions can mitigate immediate errors, but long-term improvement requires addressing the underlying misconceptions that cause repeated mistakes. We introduce SENSEI, a framework that infers user misconceptions from interaction behavior and provides targeted, minimal yet sufficient suggestions to correct them. Our approach departs from action- or trajectory-level interventions by operating over a structured knowledge representation to localize and correct the sources of erroneous behavior. Across three long-horizon tasks with diverse misconceptions and corresponding behaviors, SENSEI demonstrates zero-shot compositional generalization, disentangling multiple overlapping misconceptions despite training only on single-misconception cases. A user study further shows that our method identifies real human misconceptions and provides effective guidance that improves long-horizon task performance, successfully correcting $90\%$ of student misconceptions. Code and project page are available at https://misoshiruseijin.github.io/SENSEI/.

</details>


### [189] [Self-Commitment Latency: A Reward-Free Probe for Prompted Implicit Hacking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05625)
*Bonan Shen,Youting Wang,Dingyan Shang,Tao Ning*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需奖励模型即可检测大语言模型“奖励欺骗”的新型探针，即通过衡量模型是否“过早”在思维链中确认最终答案（自承诺延迟）来识别其是否依赖捷径而非真实推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 隐式奖励欺骗（implicit reward hacking）在模型推理过程中往往看起来很良性，现有的检测手段通常依赖昂贵的外部奖励信号或特定的任务标注，难以审计模型是否通过捷径而非真正的逻辑推理得出答案。

Method: 提出了“自承诺延迟”（self-commitment latency）方法，通过测量语言模型在生成思维链的过程中，多早开始表现出对最终答案的确定性承诺来识别捷径行为。在GSM8K数据集上对比了正常提示与包含答案暗示的提示，并使用了AUROC作为评估指标。

Result: 实验表明暗示语境下模型会提前且更确定地承诺最终答案。核心指标“首次承诺延迟”在阈值0.8下达到0.878的AUROC，而全文曲线摘要（commitment range和mean uncommitted mass）进一步将AUROC提升至0.926和0.904。该信号在模型给出正确答案时更加稳健。

Conclusion: “自承诺延迟”指标能够有效识别利用隐式提示捷径的奖励欺骗行为，无需外部奖励模型或训练额外的分类器，提供了一种轻量级的可解释性探针。

Abstract: Implicit reward hacking is hard to audit when a language model's chain of thought appears benign: a final answer may be anchored by a prompt shortcut while the written reasoning still resembles ordinary problem solving. Verifier-based probes expose such behavior by measuring how early truncated reasoning contexts obtain high reward, but require a task-specific reward signal. This paper proposes a weaker-input alternative, self-commitment latency, which measures how early a prompted reasoning context commits to the model's own final answer. We evaluate the probe in a controlled paired GSM8K setting using Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct-4bit, comparing ordinary prompts with prompts that include an answer hint. Hinted contexts commit substantially earlier and with lower uncertainty than honest contexts. The primary latency metric, first-commitment latency at threshold 0.8, reaches AUROC 0.878; supporting whole-curve summaries reach AUROC 0.926 for commitment range and 0.904 for mean uncommitted mass. The signal is stronger when both prompt conditions answer correctly and remains stable across thresholds. These results show that shortcut-available reasoning contexts can leave an early behavioral commitment signature detectable without a reward model, external judge, or trained classifier.

</details>


### [190] [Beyond Output Matching: Preserving Internal Geometry in NVFP4 LLM Distillatio](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05682)
*Fangbo Tu,Junhua Zhao,Chi Liu,Xin Chen,Haifeng Wu,Jian Wan,Srinivasan Manoharan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对大模型低位（NVFP4）量化，本文发现仅匹配输出会导致内部表征退化；为此提出了 CKA-QAD，通过 CKA 指导表征对齐，有效补齐了传统 QAD 在内部几何保持上的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的量化感知蒸馏（QAD）仅关注输出分布的匹配（KL 散度），但这会导致中间层表征的几何结构与教师模型产生显著偏差（即表征漂移），进而影响模型在推理和编码任务上的表现。

Method: 提出了 CKA-QAD 方法，在传统的 KL 散度输出匹配基础上，增加了一个轻量级正则化项，通过中心核对齐（CKA）对齐学生模型与教师模型各层的 Gram 矩阵，从而保持内部表征的几何结构。

Result: 在 Nemotron 3 Nano 和 Qwen3-4B-Thinking-2507 模型上的实验表明，CKA-QAD 能显著改善表征对齐，在保持极低训练开销的前提下，提升了模型在下游复杂任务中的准确率。

Conclusion: CKA-QAD 是一种有效的量化蒸馏补充方法，通过在蒸馏过程中保持内部表征几何结构，能够显著提升 NVFP4 等低精度大模型的推理与编码能力及表征对齐度。

Abstract: Demand for low-precision inference, including NVFP4-based approaches, has grown as large language models are increasingly deployed in latency and cost constrained production environments. Quantization-aware distillation (QAD) helps recover accuracy lost under low bit quantization by training a quantized student to match the output distribution of a frozen higher precision teacher via a KL-divergence loss. In this work, we first provide a representation level diagnosis of QAD: output matching alone can mask internal degradation, because many intermediate activation geometries can yield similar teacher-aligned logits. Using CKA, we show that KL-only QAD can reduce layerwise representational similarity relative to the BF16 teacher, with especially severe drift in RL-post-trained models. This drift correlates with downstream bottlenecks on reasoning and coding tasks, suggesting that low bit recovery requires preserving internal geometry rather than matching outputs alone. Motivated by this finding, we propose \textbf{CKA-QAD}, a CKA-guided representational alignment method for NVFP4 QAD and low bit LLM accuracy recovery. The method adds a lightweight regularizer that preserves internal representational geometry during distillation by aligning layerwise Gram matrices through CKA. Across Nemotron 3 Nano and Qwen3-4B-Thinking-2507, CKA-QAD substantially improves representational alignment and improves downstream reasoning and coding accuracy with modest training overhead. Our findings position CKA-guided representational alignment as a practical complement to output matching for quantized LLM recovery.

</details>


### [191] [Critic-Guided Heterogeneous Multi-Agent Reasoning for Reliable Mathematical Problem Solving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05704)
*Muhammad Talha Sharif,Abdul Rehman*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于批评者反馈的异构多智能体协作框架，有效解决了LLM在数学推理中的错误级联问题，大幅提升了推理准确性与稳健性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管大语言模型（LLM）具备强大的推理能力，但在解决复杂数学问题时仍存在幻觉、推理步骤错误以及结果不可靠的问题。

Method: 引入了一种基于批评者（Critic）的异构多智能体框架。系统采用“生成器-验证器”架构，其中的验证器不仅负责判定正确性，还能提供批判性反馈以指导解决方案的重构，从而实现自适应纠错并防止错误级联。

Result: 在GSM8K基准测试中，该方法相比单次生成（single-shot）和无批判机制的模型，准确率提升高达13%。实验证明该方法降低了对超大模型参数量的依赖，小模型亦能达到同等水平。

Conclusion: 通过结合异构多智能体协作与批判反馈机制，即使是较小规模的模型也能实现可靠且可解释的推理系统，表明批判驱动的反馈循环是提升推理性能的核心。

Abstract: Recent Large Language Models (LLMs) have shown impressive reasoning abilities; but they are still susceptible to hallucinations, intermediate reasoning mistakes, and unreliable reasoning results in complex mathematical reasoning problems. In this study, we introduce a critic-based heterogeneous multi-agent approach to improve the dependability of mathematical reasoning. This framework incorporates several LLM agents of different specialties and employs a critic-driven adaptive learning system to assess and guide the reasoning process based on intermediate feedback. The system adopts a generator-validator framework, with the validator not only determining correctness but also offering critiques to guide regeneration of solutions. This allows for adaptive error correction and prevents error cascading. Our experiments on the GSM8K benchmark show that the proposed method achieves up to 13% accuracy improvement over single-shot and non-critic models. Additionally, findings suggest that heterogeneity and critique reduce the need for large models, allowing smaller models to perform on par. Ablation studies reveal the main performance gains are due to the critic-based feedback loop and not model size. In summary, the proposed approach showcases the benefits of combining heterogeneous multi-agent collaboration and critique to obtain reliable and interpretable reasoning systems.

</details>


### [192] [Can LLMs Write Correct TLA+ Specifications? Evaluating Natural-Language-to-TLA+ Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05792)
*Arslan Bisharat,Brian Ortiz,Eric Spencer,Khushboo Bhadauria,TaiNing Wang,George K. Thiruvathukal,Konstantin Laufer,Mohammed Abuhamad*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究首次对LLM进行自然语言生成TLA+规范的系统性测评，发现其语义正确率低，模型推理对齐比规模更重要，且存在显著的训练数据引起的幻觉，当前阶段仍需专家监督。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TLA+在工业界应用广泛，但手动编写门槛高；尽管LLM在代码生成方面表现出色，但尚无研究系统评估LLM将自然语言转换为TLA+规范的语意正确性。

Method: 构建了一个包含205个TLA+规范的测评数据集；评估了8个系列的30个模型，涵盖了不同的开源模型（4种提示策略，2600次运行）和闭源模型（少样本提示，130次运行）；利用SANY解析器和TLC模型检查器进行语法和语义验证。

Result: LLM的语法正确率最高达26.6%，但语义正确率仅为8.6%。成功率与模型规模不成正比（如DeepSeek r1:8b优于70b），代码专用模型整体表现不佳，且识别出五类由训练数据偏差引起的幻觉问题。

Conclusion: 当前LLM尚无法在无人监督的情况下生成可靠的TLA+规范。LLM规模并非质量预测指标，模型性能更依赖于针对形式化语言的推理对齐能力，且代码专用模型在TLA+任务上存在负迁移现象。

Abstract: TLA+ has supported industrial verification at companies such as Amazon and Microsoft, yet writing correct TLA+ specifications from natural language still requires time and expertise, which limits adoption. LLMs show promise, but no prior study measures whether they produce semantically correct TLA+ specifications from natural language. This paper presents the first systematic evaluation of LLM-based TLA+ specification synthesis from natural language. Our study evaluates 30 LLMs across eight families on a curated dataset of 205 TLA+ specifications: 25 open-weight models across four prompting strategies (2,600 runs) and 5 proprietary models under few-shot prompting (130 runs), all validated by the SANY parser and TLC model checker. LLMs achieve up to 26.6% syntactic correctness but only 8.6% semantic correctness, with successes exclusive to progressive prompting. Results show that model size does not predict quality, e.g., DeepSeek r1:8b outperforms its 70B variant across all strategies, which suggests the importance of reasoning alignment for formal languages. Code-specialized models consistently underperform due to negative transfer from mainstream language training. We identify five recurring hallucination categories, all traceable to specific training data biases. These results suggest that current LLMs do not generate reliable TLA+ specifications without expert oversight. We release the evaluation framework, code, and dataset to support reproducibility and future research.

</details>


### [193] [A Pre-Registered Causal Partition of Self-Consistency Elicitation and Reward Design in RLVR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05932)
*Yuze Gao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究指出RLVR中用于评估奖励设计效果的常用方法存在系统性偏见，常常错误地将模型的“多数投票自洽性”误认为“奖励设计的优越性”。作者通过数学分解和实验证明了这一偏差，并提供了一个可重用的审计工具来区分这两种效应，从而对现有研究结论进行了重新审视。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前在强化学习可验证奖励（RLVR）领域，从业者通常使用一种朴素的估计量来衡量奖励设计的效果，但该方法存在系统性偏差，无法区分模型性能提升究竟是源于高质量的奖励函数，还是仅仅因为模型在投票机制下表现出的自洽性（即使答案是错误的）。

Method: 1. 理论推导：使用表格型GRPO模拟器，将总增益分解为 null（基准）、elicit（诱导）和 rd（奖励设计）三项。
2. 实验验证：在五个先验强度（prior strength）级别下进行模拟。
3. 因子实验：使用 2x2x2 析因设计验证各因素间的非加性交互作用。
4. 审计实践：对两篇已发表论文进行回溯审计。

Result: 1. 朴素估计量具有高度系统性偏差，在弱先验时奖励设计占比仅 13.9%，强先验时仅 5%。
2. 证实了诱导效应与奖励设计之间存在显著的非加性交互作用。
3. 对两篇公开论文的重审计结果显示，不同场景下诱导效应与奖励设计的贡献占比差异极大。

Conclusion: 现有的“朴素估计量（naive = acc(TRUE) - acc(RANDOM)）”无法准确衡量奖励设计的效果，因为它将“自洽性诱导（elicitation，即模型趋向于多数答案的伪奖励）”与“真实的奖励设计信号（reward design）”混为一谈。研究提出的分解方法能够量化二者贡献，为评估RLVR的真实效能提供了更精确的标准。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning from verifiable rewards (RLVR) improves reasoning even when the reward signal is spurious -- assigning credit to the group-plurality answer rather than a ground-truth verifier. Practitioners commonly interpret naive = acc(TRUE) - acc(RANDOM) as the reward-design effect. We prove this estimand is systematically biased: it conflates self-consistency elicitation (sharpening the policy toward its modal answer via majority pseudo-reward) with genuine reward-design signal. Using a controlled tabular-GRPO simulator we derive an exact telescoping decomposition total = null + elicit + rd and measure each term across five prior-strength levels. The reward-design fraction of the naive estimator ranges from 0.139 at weak prior (ps=0.20) to 0.05 at strong prior (ps=0.80), with the elicitation term flipping sign at the self-consistency crossover. A pre-registered 2x2x2 factorial confirms non-additivity (interaction ratio 0.385; AxC effect -0.089). A points-vs-bounds pilot gate shows strong-prior regimes are point-identified while near-crossover regimes are only bounded. Re-audits of two named published results yield ELICITATION DOMINATED (elicitation share 0.98) and REWARD DESIGN DOMINATED (rd share 1.18) verdicts respectively, demonstrating the diagnostic value of the partition. We pre-commit to submit regardless of flip outcome; a non-flip is a finding of equal standing. We release a reusable one-command harness for any alignment paper to run the same audit.

</details>


### [194] [Integrating Mechanistic and Data-Driven Models for Neurological Disorders through Differentiable Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06094)
*Shah Pallav Dhanendrakumar,Saikat Pal,Sitikantha Roy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文综述了将深度学习与物理机制求解器相结合的混合建模策略，旨在提升神经系统疾病（如肿瘤、阿尔茨海默病等）的诊断、预后评估及个性化治疗策略的准确性与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经系统疾病建模中，纯机制模型往往因过度简化或计算昂贵受限，而纯数据驱动模型则面临数据需求高、可解释性弱和泛化能力差的问题，需要一种结合两者优势的新型建模范式。

Method: 提出了三种主要的混合建模架构（并行、串行、并行-串行），并重点介绍了残差建模（补充缺失物理过程）、神经常微分方程（NODE，用于连续时间动力学拟合）以及求解器闭环（Neural-in-the-loop，加速传统求解）三种核心技术方法。

Result: 混合模型成功整合了微分方程与深度学习，在特征表达、疾病进展预测及治疗效果评估方面，表现优于单一类型的建模方法。

Conclusion: 混合建模（结合物理机制与深度学习）是建模神经系统疾病的有力工具，能够克服纯机制模型计算缓慢及纯数据驱动模型可解释性差的局限，在处理脑肿瘤、阿尔茨海默病和中风等复杂疾病的预后与治疗规划方面表现出巨大潜力。

Abstract: Advances in computational modeling, neuroimaging, and artificial intelligence are revolutionizing the modeling of neurological disorders for improved diagnostics, prognosis, and treatment planning. Mechanistic models provide valuable scientific insight into the disorders, but in practice they are often simplified with assumptions or computationally expensive and slow to solve. However, while purely data driven approaches provide speed and scalability, they require large, high quality data to train and generally suffer from interpretability and generalization issues. This perspective paper presents a structured overview of hybrid modeling strategies, which combine deep learning models with physics based solvers, and are categorized into parallel, series, and parallel-series architectures. Three main approaches that have been emphasized are residual modeling for missing or incomplete physics, Neural Ordinary Differential Equations (NODEs) for continuous time dynamics approximation, and solver in the loop that accelerates traditional solvers with neural approximations. These hybrid models integrate the governing differential equation based formulations and deep learning to characterize the evolution of neurological disorders, and promise advanced personalized neurological modeling. In addition, the study explores and proposes different hybrid configurations to improve diagnosis accuracy, predict disease progression, and inform treatment strategies across a range of neurological disorders. These capabilities outperform standalone mechanistic or purely data driven approaches, making hybrid modeling a powerful tool, especially in applications involving modeling the progression and treatment responses in neurological conditions such as brain tumors, Alzheimer's disease, and stroke.

</details>


### [195] [Step-adaptive multimodal fusion network with multi-scale cloud feature learning for ultra-short-term solar irradiance forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06102)
*Jingxin Zhang Xiaoqin Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种融合地基云图特征提取（InceptionNeXt）、步长自适应补偿及时间序列注意力机制（TempAttnLSTM）的超短期光伏辐照度预测模型，显著提升了多步预测的精度和灵活性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对现有方法在太阳辐照度超短期预测中的三个主要短板：单时间序列模型难以捕捉云的空间动态、标准卷积无法充分表达多尺度云特征、以及固定的低频补偿策略无法适应不同的预测步长。

Method: 1. 利用 InceptionNeXt 从地基云图中提取多尺度、多方向的空间特征；2. 引入步长自适应的低频补偿单元（Step-adaptive low-frequency compensation unit）；3. 结合气象时间序列，利用 TempAttnLSTM 网络捕捉全局时间依赖性以实现多步预测。

Result: 在 NREL 公开数据集及山东实际光伏电站实测数据上的实验表明，该模型在预测精度上优于多种先进方法（SOTA）。

Conclusion: 提出了一种多源数据融合架构，有效解决了太阳辐照度超短期预测中空间特征提取不足、多尺度表达能力弱以及低频补偿策略缺乏灵活性等问题，在多步预测中表现出极佳的鲁棒性和精度。

Abstract: Ultra-short-term solar irradiance prediction is critical for photovoltaic system dispatch and power grid stability. Existing approaches suffer from three key shortcomings: single time-series models cannot capture the spatial dynamics of clouds under complex conditions, standard convolutions inadequately represent multi-scale cloud features, and fixed low-frequency compensation strategies fail to adapt to different prediction steps. To address these issues, this proposes a multi-source data fusion model for ultra-short-term irradiance prediction. The model first employs InceptionNeXt to extract multi-scale, multi-directional spatial features from ground-based cloud images. A step-adaptive low-frequency compensation unit is then introduced to dynamically modulate global low-frequency information based on the prediction step. Eventually, the enhanced image features are combined with meteorological time-series features, and a TempAttnLSTM network captures global temporal dependencies for multi-step prediction. Experiments on the public NREL dataset and practical photovoltaic stations in Shandong illustrate the effectiveness of the proposed method compared with several state-of-the-art approaches.

</details>


### [196] [Unsupervised Pattern Analysis in Japanese Veterinary Toxicology: A Regulatory-Compliant Framework for Cross-Species Risk Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06207)
*Yukiko Kawakami,Mohammad Shirazi,Ryo Shimizuwa,Saito Shinoda,Alireza Mortazavi,Matsumoto Kawahara*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种监管集成下的无监督学习框架，通过深度分析日本兽医药物不良反应数据，成功挖掘出物种特异性的毒性结构，并实现了高水平的药理学一致性，解决了传统兽医警戒系统中可解释性不足的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的兽医药物警戒系统难以捕捉受限于地区生物学和监管环境的特定毒性模式，且以往的预测模型缺乏机制可解释性；针对日本兽医药物报告环境，需要一种更具解释力且能适应特定物种代谢差异的评估框架。

Method: 利用日本国家兽医检测实验室（NVAL）的数据库，通过将药物不良反应（ADE）编码为器官系统对齐的表征，并对物种特有的报告偏差进行校准；应用相似性聚类和降维技术挖掘潜在的毒性结构；通过余弦相似度评估聚类效果，并结合监管标准进行验证。

Result: 分析了4,120份高置信度ADE报告（9,080个药物-ADE组合），识别出三个显著的物种聚类模式（伴侣动物的肝毒性、反刍动物的肾毒性及绵羊的皮肤敏感性）；药物级聚类与药理学分类的符合度达到83%，轮廓系数为0.48，聚类精度为87%。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了监管导向的无监督学习框架能够有效发现具有生物学意义且符合地区特征的药物毒性模式，为兽药安全评估提供了一种可解释、可扩展的处理方案。

Abstract: Veterinary pharmacovigilance systems are essential for monitoring adverse drug events (ADEs), yet existing approaches often fail to capture region-specific toxicity patterns shaped by local biological and regulatory contexts. In Japan, these challenges are amplified by species-specific metabolic differences and reporting practices defined by the Ministry of Agriculture, Forestry, and Fisheries (MAFF). Most prior work relies on prediction-oriented models, limiting mechanistic interpretability. This study proposes a regulatory-integrated unsupervised framework for pattern discovery using the National Veterinary Assay Laboratory (NVAL) database. ADEs are encoded into organ system-aligned representations and adjusted for species-specific reporting biases, enabling cross-species comparison. Similarity-based clustering and dimensionality reduction are applied to identify latent toxicity structures. Analysis of 4,120 high-confidence ADE reports (9,080 drug-ADE combinations) identified three significant species clusters (p < 0.01), including hepatic-dominant patterns in companion animals (0.42 $\pm$ 0.06), renal toxicity in ruminants (0.39 $\pm$ 0.07), and dermatological sensitivity in sheep (0.35 $\pm$ 0.07). Drug-level clustering achieved 83% alignment with pharmacological classes, while cosine similarity outperformed alternative metrics (silhouette score: 0.48; cluster precision: 87%). Regulatory validation showed strong agreement with established classifications. These findings demonstrate that regulation-aligned unsupervised analysis can uncover biologically meaningful, region-specific toxicity patterns, providing an interpretable and scalable framework for veterinary drug safety assessment.

</details>


### [197] [Boosting Brain-to-Image Decoding with TRIBE v2 Data Augmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06345)
*Yohann Benchetrit,Marlène Careil,Simon Dahan,Hubert Banville,Stéphane d'Ascoli,Jean-Rémi King*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究证明利用大规模预训练模型生成的合成数据增强小规模fMRI数据集，能显著提升低数据场景下的脑-图像解码性能与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脑解码高度依赖带标签的神经数据，但在低数据量环境下表现受限，亟需通过数据增强技术突破瓶颈。

Method: 利用预训练的编码模型TRIBE v2生成合成fMRI数据，并通过在NSD和BOLD5000数据集上进行系统性实验，评估不同比例合成数据对图像解码器性能的影响。

Result: 使用合成数据增强后，Top-10图像检索准确率最高提升了68%；在某些场景下，仅使用合成数据训练的解码器即可实现高于随机水平的解码效果。

Conclusion: 大规模预训练模型（如TRIBE v2）能够通过生成合成fMRI数据有效提升低数据环境下的脑解码性能，甚至支持零样本（zero-shot）脑图像解码，为提升脑解码的数据效率提供了有力基础。

Abstract: Brain decoding is limited by the availability of labeled neural data, and remains challenging in low-data regimes. To address this issue, we investigate whether and when brain decoding can be boosted by augmenting small fMRI datasets with synthetic data generated by a pretrained model of fMRI responses to stimuli. We use TRIBE v2, a large encoding model pretrained on more than 1000 hours of fMRI responses to video, audio and language. For each dataset, we evaluate systematic grids that show how the performance of image decoders varies with the amount of synthetic data used for training. Our results, based on two datasets (the 7T fMRI Natural Scenes Dataset and 3T fMRI BOLD5000), show up to 68% improvement in Top-10 image-retrieval accuracy compared to decoders trained only on real data. Importantly, the proportion of augmented data required to reach a given image decoding performance needs to be adjusted depending on the data source. Surprisingly, image decoders trained exclusively on synthetic fMRI can perform above chance in some settings, suggesting that TRIBE v2 can support zero-shot brain-to-image decoding. Together, these results show how large-scale models of the fMRI responses to sight, sound and language may provide a foundation to improve the data efficiency for image decoding.

</details>


<div id='cs.AR'></div>

# cs.AR [[Back]](#toc)

### [198] [BIDENT: Heterogeneous Operator-level Mapping for Efficient Edge Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05271)
*Hoseok Kim,Arghadip Das,Soumendu Ghosh,Arnab Raha,Vijay Raghunathan*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 针对边缘 AI 异构 SoC 的局限性，BIDENT 提出了算子级编排框架，通过将算子动态分配到最优 PU，显著提升了推理性能和能效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代边缘 SoC集成了多种异构处理单元（CPU、GPU、NPU），但现有系统多将完整模型映射至单一 PU，无法满足新兴架构（如 SSM、KAN、VLA）中多样化算子的最优执行需求，导致性能/能效瓶颈。

Method: 提出 BIDENT 框架，通过将算子到处理单元（PU）的分配建模为带权执行图上的最短路径问题，支持异构推理。它实现了模型无关的调度，支持顺序执行、模型内并行以及多模型并发调度，并可优化延迟或能耗目标。

Result: 在 Intel Core Ultra SoC 上测试发现，BIDENT 通过算子级并行最高可实现 1.60 倍加速；在多模型并发场景下，几何平均加速达到 3.42 倍；在能耗优化模式下，平均能耗降低 48.2%。

Conclusion: 算子级编排（Operator-level orchestration）是充分利用下一代边缘 AI 异构性能的关键抽象，优于传统的模型级映射。

Abstract: Modern edge System-on-Chips (SoCs) integrate heterogeneous processing units (PUs) such as CPUs, GPUs, and NPUs, yet current inference stacks map entire models to a single PU, leaving significant performance and energy efficiency on the table. This is exacerbated by emerging architectures such as state-space models (SSMs), Kolmogorov-Arnold networks (KANs), and multi-stage vision-language-action (VLA) pipelines, whose diverse operator characteristics are not uniformly suited to any single PU. We present BIDENT, a unified operator-level orchestration framework for heterogeneous edge inference that maps individual operators to the most suitable PU based on profiled execution characteristics. BIDENT formulates operator-to-PU assignment as a shortest-path problem over a weighted execution graph, enabling efficient and optimal scheduling under the cost model for both latency- and energy-minimization objectives. Unlike prior work relying on model-specific heuristics or coarse-grained partitioning, BIDENT is model-agnostic and jointly supports sequential execution, intra-model parallelism across independent operators, and multi-model concurrent scheduling in a single formulation. We implement BIDENT on an Intel Core Ultra SoC and evaluate it across 10 model families spanning CNNs, Transformers, SSMs, KANs, spiking networks, and multi-stage pipelines. BIDENT achieves up to 1.60x speedup via intra-model parallelism and a 3.42x geometric mean speedup across 190 multi-model combinations by utilizing otherwise idle compute. Sequential heterogeneous mapping yields more modest gains (up to 1.58x, 1.09x geometric mean), while energy-aware scheduling reduces energy consumption by 48.2% on average in concurrent settings. These results show that operator-level orchestration, not model-level mapping, is the key abstraction for fully exploiting heterogeneity in next-generation edge AI.

</details>


### [199] [Space-CIM: Enabling Compute-In-Memory Accelerators for Thermally-Constrained Space Platforms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05741)
*Sohan Salahuddin Mugdho,Md. Shahedul Hasan,Cheng Wang*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文研究了空间轨道数据中心的可行性，提出利用存内计算（CIM）取代传统GPU，以解决空间真空环境下辐射散热导致的性能瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 缓解地球数据中心能源与可持续性危机；利用空间丰富的太阳能和低成本发射，探索轨道数据中心作为AI算力扩展的可行路径。

Method: 提出了一种“散热器在环（radiator-in-the-loop）”的协同设计方法，将系统计算能力（TOPS）与空间散热限制直接挂钩，通过热仿真对比研究了GPU（含HBM）与CIM在空间环境下的热特性及能效比。

Result: 热仿真显示GPU架构中GPU芯片与HBM分离会导致严重的局部热点，迫使核心降频；而CIM架构热分布均匀，在各种散热器预算下均表现出更高的TOPS/W，特别是在受限空间热约束下优势被放大。

Conclusion: 在面临空间限制性热管理（辐射散热）的情况下，存内计算（CIM）架构相比传统GPU具有显著的性能优势和更高的部署可行性，是未来轨道数据中心的首选计算方案。

Abstract: The rapid growth in compute demand from artificial intelligence (AI) has driven a massive surge in data center construction, precipitating an energy and sustainability crisis. Motivated by the abundant solar energy in outer space and the recent sharp reduction in space launch costs, orbital data centers are emerging as a potential pathway for the future scaling of AI compute infrastructure. While the cold background in vacuum seems appealing for cooling, computing systems operating in space without convection ultimately rely on radiative cooling, requiring large-area radiators. Such limitations in thermal management pose a significant challenge for deploying the standard liquid/air-cooled computers in space. In this work, we investigate the impact of the thermal constraints in space on both graphics processing units (GPUs) with high-bandwidth memory (HBM) and the emerging compute-in-memory (CIM) accelerators. We develop a radiator-in-the-loop co-design methodology that directly links the permitted system TOPS (terra-operations per second) with the practical radiator cooling capacity in space. Our thermal simulations reveal that the separately located GPU die and HBMs create severe thermal hotspots under limited radiator capacity, necessitating GPU thermal throttling. In contrast, CIM accelerators exhibit a much more uniform heat distribution and consistently outperform GPUs in TOPS/W across a wide range of radiator budgets. We systematically evaluated the performance of CIM and GPU across various AI workloads and demonstrated that CIM has a magnified advantage for deployment in space under realistic thermal constraints.

</details>


### [200] [ITP-STDP: An Intrinsic-Timing Power-of-Two Learning Engine for On-Chip SNN Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06159)
*Haihang Xia,Xinyu Zhao,Xuecheng Wang,John Goodenough,Charith Abhayaratne,Panagiotis A. Panagiotou,Chunyi Song,Tiantai Deng*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 针对SNN片上训练计算开销大的问题，本文提出了一种高效的ITP-STDP算法及专用硬件架构，通过算法与硬件协同优化，大幅降低了计算复杂度和资源占用，实现了显著的能效比和速度提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脉冲神经网络（SNN）中大规模的突触连接导致片上训练时权重更新计算密集，带来了巨大的硬件资源消耗和能耗，传统STDP算法的实现效率亟待提升。

Method: 提出了Intrinsic-timing power-of-two STDP (ITP-STDP)算法，并设计了相应的学习引擎硬件架构。该方法通过算法层面的优化消除大部分传统STDP的计算复杂度，并在ASIC和FPGA平台上进行了原型实现与验证。

Result: 在FPGA上，能效比提升了4.5倍至219.8倍；在ASIC上，处理速度提升了4.8倍至22.01倍，且仅占用此前设计1.2%至3.3%的芯片面积。

Conclusion: 通过算法与硬件层面的协同优化，ITP-STDP模型显著降低了SNN片上学习的计算开销。该设计不仅在理论上通过动力学分析得到验证，在实际硬件实现中也展现了极高的能效比和资源优化潜力，是实现大规模、高性能低功耗神经形态计算的有效方案。

Abstract: Spiking neural networks (SNNs) have the potential to emerge as the third generation of neural networks and have attracted increasing attention across a wide range of applications. However, the large number of synaptic connections in SNNs leads to intensive weight-update computation by on-chip learning algorithms during training, resulting in substantial hardware resource utilization and energy consumption. Among existing SNN learning algorithms, spike-timing-dependent plasticity (STDP) is one of the most extensively studied and widely adopted, serving as a fundamental learning component in SNNs. To address the hardware and energy overheads associated with SNN training, this paper presents intrinsic-timing power-of-two STDP (ITP-STDP) and its corresponding prototype learning engine hardware architecture. The proposed design is evaluated through a dedicated mean-field synaptic drift model for dynamical analysis and further validated across SNN networks of different scales and datasets. It is further implemented on both ASIC and FPGA platforms and compared with state-of-the-art approaches, including the original STDP and more complex STDP variants. The results demonstrate superior energy efficiency, higher operating speed, and substantially lower hardware resource utilization, as the proposed design eliminates most of the computational overhead of STDP through both algorithmic and hardware-level optimizations. On the FPGA platform, the proposed design improves energy efficiency by 4.5$\times$ to 219.8$\times$ over the compared designs. On the ASIC platform, the proposed design achieves a 4.8$\times$ to 22.01$\times$ speedup while consuming only 1.2% to 3.3% of the area required by prior works.

</details>


### [201] [Modeling, Optimizing and Exploring Multi-Die FPGA Routing Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06421)
*Amirhossein Poolad,Soheil Gholami Shahrouz,Andrew Boutros,Vaughn Betz*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 该论文通过扩展VTR框架，对2.5D和3D FPGA的片间路由架构进行了全面的建模与性能评估，证明了多芯片堆叠方案能在低开销下有效提升FPGA容量与性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多芯片堆叠（2.5D/3D）技术虽能提升FPGA容量，但现有互连技术存在密度与延迟限制，且缺乏准确的设计探索工具来评估片间路由架构。

Method: 1. 扩展VTR（Verilog-to-Routing）框架以支持多芯片布线架构建模；2. 增强VPR的布局布线引擎以优化2.5D/3D FPGA；3. 采用基于7nm工艺的HSPICE进行电路级建模；4. 开展多维度的设计空间探索，分析不同芯片互连技术对性能的影响。

Result: 3D FPGA相比2D设备可实现最高14%的线长减少及6%的关键路径延迟（CPD）改善；2.5D FPGA在32%的片间连接度下，仅产生2%的线长和4%的CPD开销。上述改进均已合并至VTR开源主分支。

Conclusion: 通过合理的片间布线架构设计，2.5D和3D FPGA可以在保持布线性与延迟较小影响的前提下有效提升容量。3D FPGA在性能上优于2D器件，而2.5D FPGA在特定连接度下仅有极小的性能损耗。

Abstract: Die stacking has enabled 2.5D FPGAs by integrating multiple active dice on a passive silicon interposer for improved yield and capacity, and paved the way for 3D architectures that stack active dice directly atop one another. In these multi-die devices, the unique electrical and physical characteristics of the underlying die-stacking technology impose limitations on inter-die connection density and latency, necessitating a bespoke inter-die routing architecture. However, the absence of accurate and versatile modeling tools has left most questions about how to best design the inter-die routing architecture unanswered.
  To address this gap, we enhance the open-source FPGA CAD tool VTR to flexibly model a wide range of multi-die routing architectures, and augment VPR's placement and routing engines to improve optimization for both 2.5D and 3D FPGAs. We perform HSPICE-based circuit modeling of inter-die connections for active dice using a 7 nm process node and a 45 nm silicon interposer across several die-crossing technologies. Using this enhanced framework, we conduct a detailed design space exploration of inter-die routing architecture in 2.5D and 3D FPGAs, characterizing the impact of die-crossing technology, inter-die connection count, fan-in/fan-out, and interposer wire length on critical path delay (CPD), wirelength, area, and routability.
  Our results show that with suitable inter-die routing architectures, 2.5D and 3D FPGAs can increase capacity without significant routability or delay penalties. Specifically, 3D FPGAs achieve up to 14% wirelength reduction and 6% CPD improvement over 2D devices, and remain routable even with existing $10\,μ$m pitch technologies, while 2.5D FPGAs incur only a 2% wirelength and 4% CPD overhead at 32% inter-die connectivity. All extensions are open source and integrated with the VTR master branch.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [202] [The Evaluation Blind Spot: A Stereological Theory of Benchmark Coverage for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05169)
*Jason Z Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过立体学理论揭示了LLM评测基准的“结构性盲点”，证明了现有榜单排名极度不稳定，并提出了一种优化的基准子集筛选方案，同时从数学上解决了C²支持函数下的Gardner 1.5问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在量化LLM评测基准的覆盖范围与局限性，解释为何当前LLM榜单中模型排名存在不稳定性，以及如何通过科学手段优化评测集以实现更有效的评估。

Method: 1. 建立LLM基准覆盖的立体学理论（利用Hausdorff距离和有效维度）；2. 经验性分析：通过三个主流榜单（Open LLM v2, 12-benchmark, LiveBench）计算有效维度；3. 统计模拟：采用卡方投影模型分析模型排名互换率；4. 优化算法：使用具有Nemhauser保证的子模贪婪算法筛选核心评测指标；5. 理论计算：基于最优恢复理论解决Gardner 1.5问题。

Result: 1. LLM榜单存在极大的结构性盲点，其影响程度远超统计噪声（52-127倍）；2. 顶级模型在不同的评测子集下排名极易互换（92%的试验中榜首易位），稳定性极差；3. 仅需7个关键基准测试即可覆盖90%的评测信息；4. 验证了评测指标的不可替代性与增量信息贡献度。

Conclusion: LLM评测集存在显著的结构性盲点，且当前评测体系存在严重的排名不稳定性。研究提出了基于子模贪婪算法的最优评测子集选择方案，并解决了Gardner 1.5问题在C²支持函数下的极小极大速率问题。

Abstract: We give a stereological theory of LLM benchmark coverage. For any suite with effective dimensionality d_eff, the visible Hausdorff distance between two convex capability profiles consistent with the same scores is bounded by epsilon + C R m^(-1/(d_eff-1)), with matching Lipschitz lower bound. Empirically, three independent leaderboards (Open LLM v2, an extended 12-benchmark suite, LiveBench) all have d_eff in [2.86, 4.80] on their competitive frontier; the structural blind spot exceeds the observed runner-up score gap by two orders of magnitude and dominates statistical noise by 52-127x. Under a chi-squared projection model, the isotropic prior is the optimistic case; across six hidden-capability priors and four ambient dimensions the simulated half-split swap rate of the top two models stays in [0.38, 0.49], and a 500-trial random visible/held-out split shows that 92% of trials swap the top-1 ranking with on average 2.83 of 5 top-5 models changing. A submodular greedy algorithm with the Nemhauser (1 - 1/e) guarantee finds a stable core of 4 benchmarks; 7 of 12 suffice for 90% coverage, and the trained subset transfers across temporal quarters with 93-97% retention. A counterfactual validation across 12 internal benchmarks and 27 Chatbot Arena categories confirms that the eigenstructure predicts which evaluations are irreplaceable (rho = -0.69, p = 0.013 for removal disruption) and which external evaluations bring new information (rho = +0.38). As a second, independent theoretical contribution, we resolve Gardner's Problem 1.5 (1995) for C^2 support functions, establishing the minimax rate Theta(R/(kappa m^(2/(D-1)))) in general dimension via optimal recovery theory on S^(D-1).

</details>


### [203] [ERRORQUAKE: Heavy-Tailed Error Severity Distributions in Open-Weight Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05170)
*Jason Z Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究指出仅用“错误率”评估LLM存在局限，即使准确率相似，模型错误严重程度的分布也大不相同。作者推出了Errorquake-10k基准，通过量化错误严重程度分布，揭示了模型间更深层的性能差异及其背后的错误机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM幻觉基准通常将所有错误视为同等权重，忽略了不同错误（如日期错误 vs. 虚构案例）在严重程度上的巨大差异，且在准确率相当的模型之间存在难以察觉的质性差异。

Method: 1. 构建 Errorquake-10k 基准：包含10,000个查询，涵盖8个领域和5个难度等级，对响应进行0-4的连续严重程度评分。
2. 模型分析：拟合21个开源模型严重程度分布的Gutenberg-Richter指数（b）。
3. 验证机制：进行人工验证（ICC=0.85），验证LLM评判者排名（rho=0.89），并证明严重程度分布与错误率在信息上是非冗余的（非可约性定理）。
4. 分类分析：建立错误机制分类法（kappa=0.83）分析错误类型随严重程度的变化。

Result: 1. 即使准确率相当，不同模型在错误严重程度分布上存在显著差异（85个模型对的置信区间不重叠）。
2. 从非可约性定理可知，64.5%的严重程度指数方差无法由错误率解释。
3. 错误本质会随严重程度改变：低严重程度多为检索错误（71%），高严重程度多为虚构错误（39%）。

Conclusion: LLM的错误频率（error rate）不能全面反映其性能。严重程度分布（severity distribution）包含额外的重要信息，建议在评估中将其作为与准确率同等重要的指标进行报告。

Abstract: At matched accuracy, open-weight LLMs differ substantially in the shape of their error severity distribution -- a difference invisible to the scalar error rate. Hallucination benchmarks report a single error count and treat all errors as equivalent, yet a wrong date and a fabricated court ruling differ by orders of magnitude. We introduce Errorquake-10k, a 10,000-query benchmark scoring each response on a continuous 0-4 severity scale across 8 domains and 5 difficulty tiers, and we fit per-model severity distributions for 21 open-weight models. For each model we estimate a severity distribution index (b, the Gutenberg-Richter upper-tail slope) with 95% bootstrap confidence intervals. Headline: across the 210 model pairs, 85 have disjoint 95% b confidence intervals at matched accuracy (|Delta epsilon| < 0.05) on human-consensus scoring, e.g. deepseek-v3.2 vs. ministral-14b at epsilon = 0.586 and Delta b = 0.47. A 519-item three-rater human validation study confirms measurement reliability (ICC(2,k=3) = 0.85), validates the LLM-judge ranking (rho = 0.89), and confirms the dense-model scaling correlation on human data (rho_s = -0.86). We prove a Non-Reducibility Theorem showing that severity profile and error rate are informationally non-redundant (I(b; model | epsilon) = 1.56 bits; 64.5% of cross-model b variance is unexplained by epsilon). A severity mechanism taxonomy (kappa = 0.83) reveals that error type shifts categorically with severity: low-severity errors are retrievals (71%); high-severity errors are fabrications (39%) -- and this composition differs by model size (p < 0.0001). Severity distribution should be reported alongside accuracy; it carries discriminative information that the error rate cannot.

</details>


### [204] [Staged Factorial Screening for Budget-Constrained Micro-Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05186)
*Felipe Chavarro Polania*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了针对预算受限微预训练的分阶段筛选方法，主张通过短时实验识别坏方向并锁定潜力锚点，结果表明以“桥接模型”为核心的策略比单纯追求通用超参数优化更有效且更具实际意义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在预算有限的微预训练场景中，需要在投入大规模搜索资源前，通过高效的实验设计（Triage）从众多候选方案中筛选出最优配置，即研究分阶段的分数因子工作流能否在早期提供稳定的效果结构提示。

Method: 采用分阶段的分数因子实验（staged fractional-factorial workflow）工作流，在固定单GPU上执行了613次实验。通过设置不同的时间预算（2、5、10、60分钟，以及12和24小时），对比了完全因子设计、随机搜索、贪婪基准及桥接模型，并利用Benjamini-Hochberg校正对效果评估的稳定性进行了统计验证。

Result: 主要发现：1. 参数（批次、深度、宽度）带来的惩罚在短时间预算下最明显；2. 随机搜索能找到强候选，但缺乏因子归因能力；3. 短时筛选能有效识别高惩罚方向；4. 60分钟的“桥接模型”在长期实验（24小时）中展现了最低的平均损失，但其性能受模型容量影响；5. 排序结果对硬件环境具有敏感性。

Conclusion: 对于预算受限的微预训练，建议采用分阶段的实验流程：先利用短时设计的筛选实验识别高惩罚方向，随后通过重复运行确认有潜力的锚点（anchors），最后在缩小的参数空间内进行局部精炼。研究发现基于“桥接模型（bridge model）”的策略在长达24小时的实验中表现最优，而非通用的超参数优化搜索。

Abstract: Budget-constrained micro-pretraining often requires triaging many candidate recipes on a shared accelerator before larger search budgets are spent. We study whether a staged fractional-factorial workflow can recover stable early effect structure in this setting. On a fixed autoresearch-derived single-GPU training loop, we run 613 experiments across pilot and follow-up screens at 2, 5, and 10 minutes; full 16-condition seeded reruns at 5 and 10 minutes; targeted seeded anchor checks; same-host greedy and matched-cost random baselines; a 60-minute bridge package; and bounded Windows A100 and Linux L40S anchor continuations through 24 hours. Main penalties from total batch, depth, and width are largest at short budgets and relax as budget increases. Within the predeclared seeded full-screen families, D, A, B, and C retain non-zero estimates at 5 and 10 minutes after within-budget Benjamini-Hochberg correction, while E does not. Random search can reach strong incumbents in this 32-condition space, but repeatedly in the same low-penalty region and without factor attribution. The 60-minute bridge anchor has the lowest mean, although that package does not separate workflow refinement from the larger bridge model's capacity advantage. In bounded 12-hour and 24-hour three-anchor continuations on both hosts, the bridge has the lowest sample mean while the non-bridge ordering stays host-sensitive. We therefore present a bounded methods result: use short designed screens to identify high-penalty directions, confirm promising anchors under repeated runs, and refine locally inside the reduced space. The evidence supports a bridge-centered recommendation through 24 hours on two hosts, not hardware-invariant ranking or general hyperparameter-optimization superiority.

</details>


### [205] [PyCC.id: A package for hypothesis-driven equation discovery with structural identifiability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05191)
*Federico J. Gonzalez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文介绍了 Python 库 PyCC，它通过引入“特征曲线骨架”和假设驱动的框架，解决了数据驱动微分方程发现中的病态逆问题和模型选择困难，为科学计算提供了一个灵活、可解释的建模工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据驱动的方程发现（从时间序列中反推微分方程）是一个病态逆问题，常导致“多解性”。现有方法即便引入约束仍需大量的后期人工筛选，亟需一种能结合领域先验知识、具备结构可识别性且模块化的工具来降低模型发现的复杂性。

Method: 构建了一个名为 PyCC 的 Python 库，该库实现了基于特征曲线（Characteristic Curves, CCs）的结构化骨架方法，支持将领域知识、假设和先验约束集成到发现过程中，并兼容多种发现范式（如神经网络、符号回归和稀疏回归）。

Result: 推出了 PyCC 库，它能够利用特征曲线骨架进行结构化建模，通过模块化的方式简化了从数据中挖掘常微分方程的过程，并具备检查模型结构可识别性的能力。

Conclusion: PyCC 提供了一个灵活且模块化的框架，通过结构“骨架”和假设驱动的方法论，有效地解决了数据驱动方程发现中模型不确定性和模型选择困难的问题，使研究人员能够系统地发现常微分方程。
