Abstract: Data-driven equation discovery is fundamentally an inverse problem that seeks to infer the governing differential equations of a system directly from time-series measurements. A known issue is the ill-conditioned nature of the inverse problem, which frequently produces multiple mathematical models that fit the data similarly well. One path to address this issue is by incorporating known hypotheses and constraints into the training phase beforehand. While this approach effectively reduces the search space, it still results in multiple candidate models, forcing practitioners to rely on post-hoc manual filtering based on their own domain expertise. A recent approach incorporates structural `skeletons' inspired by characteristic curves (CCs), defining a hypothesis-driven methodology. In this methodology, practitioners define a skeleton, which is associated with a family of ordinary differential equations (ODEs), and then add their hypotheses and priors based on their domain knowledge to refine the obtained model iteratively. An important advantage of this approach is that some skeletons have demonstrable structural identifiability properties, which are useful for checking whether the skeleton is correct or should be discarded. Furthermore, this formalism enables the use of multiple equation discovery paradigms due to its modularity (such as neural networks, symbolic regression, and sparse regression). In this work, we present the Python library PyCC, which condenses these efforts into a flexible tool that allows researchers and engineers to seamlessly define their skeletons and hypotheses to discover ODEs from time-dependent data.

</details>


### [206] [Temporal Preference Concepts and their Functions in a Large Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05194)
*Ian Rios-Sialer,Shantanu Darveshi,Shuai Jiang,Avigya Paudel,Anastasiia Pronina,Ipshita Bandyopadhyay,Justin Shenk*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究通过机械可解释性方法定位并分析了LLM内部的时间偏好表征，发现模型处理长期权衡的能力不稳定且与常人差异显著，并初步证明了可通过干预机制实现对模型时间决策的显式控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前大语言模型越来越多地参与需要权衡短期利益与长期后果的决策，但其内部如何表示及处理此类权衡机制尚不明确，制约了决策的可信度与可靠性。

Method: 1. 定位分析：使用基于梯度的归因分析（gradient-based attribution）和激活修补（activation patching）技术，在Qwen3-4B-Instruct-2507中定位与时间偏好相关的子图。2. 几何分析：在定位的中间层至高层残差流中分析时间跨度的编码几何结构。3. 行为分析：通过对比实验评估模型时间折扣（temporal discounting）偏好与人类的差异，并验证偏好的环境不稳定性。4. 实验干预：利用转向向量（steering vectors）评估改变模型时间偏好的可行性。

Result: 1. 成功定位了LLM内部负责时间偏好的关键节点子图。2. 发现模型的时间跨度信息被编码在特定层的残差流几何结构中。3. 发现模型对未来的折扣程度远低于人类，且这种偏好在不同环境下极其不稳定。4. 初步验证了通过转向向量干预并调节模型时间偏好的有效性。

Conclusion: 机械可解释性方法能够有效定位并干预大语言模型的时间偏好，为实现LLM规划与推理的可靠控制提供了技术路径。未来在处理长期后果决策时，应从隐式依赖转向显式控制。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly being deployed to make decisions that require trading off near-term gains against long-term consequences, yet little is known about how they internally represent or resolve these tradeoffs. In this work, we causally localize an underlying subgraph for temporal preference in a distilled LLM (Qwen3-4B-Instruct-2507), identifying mid-to-upper-layer nodes through converging evidence from gradient-based attribution and activation patching. We find that the geometry of time horizon is encoded in the residual stream at the expected localized layers. A behavioral analysis reveals that unintervened LLMs discount the future several times less steeply than humans, yet this preference is unstable across contexts, motivating explicit control rather than implicit reliance on training. Finally, we find suggestive evidence that steering vectors can shift temporal preference. Our work demonstrates how mechanistic interpretability can bring us closer to reliable control over how LLMs plan and reason

</details>


### [207] [State commitment learning: training language models to distinguish computation from memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05201)
*Fei Ding,Yongkang Zhang,Runhao Liu,Yuhao Liao,Zijian Zeng,Huiming Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对推理模型过度依赖无效中间思考过程的问题，本文提出了 CERL，通过反事实训练鼓励模型只保留必要的状态信息，从而提升了推理的稳健性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的推理模型无法区分“计算用Token”和“持久状态Token”，导致未能被丢弃的无效推理路径、死胡同信息会误导模型后续的决策。

Method: 提出“状态承诺学习”（State Commitment Learning）框架及“持久状态充分性”（Persistent-State Sufficiency）准则；引入“反事实擦除强化学习”（CERL），即在训练中同时评估保留隐式思维与擦除隐式思维的路径，并仅当擦除后的路径结果依然正确时给予奖励。

Result: 在数学、长链逻辑、科学问答及多轮工具使用等广泛评估中，CERL 显著降低了模型对隐式思维的依赖，表现超越了单纯基于正确性的强化学习和长文本监督微调（SFT）基线。

Conclusion: CERL 能够有效通过训练让模型区分“持久状态”与“临时计算”，在减少推理对隐式思维依赖的同时，保持甚至提升了任务的准确性，解决了推理模型中“死胡同”干扰后续决策的问题。

Abstract: Reasoning language models do not distinguish tokens used for computation from tokens that constitute persistent state: once generated, all hidden thoughts remain in context and influence future predictions. As a result, downstream reasoning may depend on failed attempts, dead ends, and private scratch work that should not be safely relied on later. We recast this phenomenon as a new training objective, state commitment learning: training models to explicitly distinguish information that should be committed as persistent state from temporary computation that can be discarded. We define a counterfactual criterion, persistent-state sufficiency, which makes it trainable and measurable whether an answer remains usable after hidden thoughts are erased. We then propose Counterfactual Erasure RL (CERL), which evaluates, under the same prefix, both a path that keeps hidden thoughts and a path that erases them, and gives reward only when the erasure path remains correct. We also introduce the Erasure Dependence Protocol and show across mathematics, long-chain logic, scientific QA, and multi-turn tool-use evaluation that CERL substantially reduces answer dependence on hidden thoughts without sacrificing accuracy, consistently outperforming correctness-only RL and long-answer SFT baselines.

</details>


### [208] [Adaptive Oscillatory-State Alignment for Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06010)
*Zhangyao Song,Ziqiong Li,Xiangfei Qiu,Chao Zha,Yinfei Xu,Tao Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AOSNET是一种基于希尔伯特变换的新型预测框架，通过自适应振荡状态对齐技术，克服了传统定长方案在处理非平稳时间序列时的匹配缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的周期性预测方法（如固定模板、全局频谱分量）无法应对真实时序中振荡行为的演变（振幅、相位、频率的动态变化），导致模型与实际动态不匹配。

Method: 提出AOSNET框架：1. 使用希尔伯特变换提取解析信号描述符；2. 建立可学习的全局振荡先验；3. 通过描述符条件门（descriptor-conditioned gate）实现局部状态的自适应对齐，动态修正偏差。

Result: 在八个基准数据集上达到SOTA或极具竞争力的精度；推理速度快；在针对非平稳性（振幅调制、相位漂移等）的合成实验中证明了该方法随非平稳性增强而具有更显著的优势。

Conclusion: AOSNET通过将周期性预测从“固定模板匹配”转化为“自适应振荡状态对齐”，有效解决了现实世界时间序列中振幅调制、相位漂移和局部频率变化带来的不确定性，在多个基准测试中达到了SOTA水平。

Abstract: Long-term time series forecasting benefits from inductive biases that expose recurring temporal structure. Existing periodic forecasting methods typically model recurrence through predefined periods, global spectral components, or fixed learnable templates. However, real-world temporal dynamics are rarely rigidly periodic: oscillatory behavior often evolves through amplitude modulation, phase drift, and local frequency variation. Under these conditions, fixed-template periodic modeling can become fundamentally mismatched to the underlying temporal states. We propose AOSNET, a Hilbert-guided forecasting framework that reformulates periodic forecasting from fixed template matching to adaptive oscillatory-state alignment. AOSNET extracts analytic-signal descriptors from both the observed sequence and a learnable global oscillatory prior, then adaptively aligns local states through a descriptor-conditioned gate that selectively preserves reliable observations while softly correcting mismatched regions. The learned prior serves not as a rigid repeated template but as a flexible oscillatory reference interpreted through local state dynamics. Experiments on eight benchmarks demonstrate state-of-the-art or highly competitive accuracy with fast inference speed. Controlled synthetic studies isolating amplitude modulation, phase drift, and local frequency variation confirm that the advantage of oscillatory-state alignment consistently increases as non-stationarity intensifies.

</details>


### [209] [Gradient Descent with Large Step Size Restores Symmetry in Deep Linear Networks with Multi-Pathway](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05219)
*Hee-Sung Kim,Sungyoon Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了使用较大步长梯度下降训练多路径深度线性网络时，网络倾向于通过“边缘稳定”动力学将信号分布在多条路径上，而非梯度流所预测的单一路径特化，从而解释了为何大步长促进了特征的共享表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既往基于梯度流的研究推测多路径神经网络会出现“赢者通吃”的路径特化现象，但这一理论与实际训练中常观察到的现象不符，特别是在较大步长下的表现。因此，作者旨在探究离散梯度下降的步长对路径竞争及表示学习的具体影响。

Method: 理论分析与证明。对比了连续梯度流（Gradient Flow）与具有较大步长的离散梯度下降（Gradient Descent），并通过分析损失函数的锐度（sharpness）随路径数量和深度的变化，揭示了不同训练阶段的动力学差异。

Result: 1. 证明了单路径解对应极端尖锐的极小值（sharp minima）；2. 分布式信号表示能有效降低锐度；3. 训练过程分为两个阶段：早期遵循梯度流预测的对称性破缺，后期通过较大步长带来的震荡效应驱动信号在路径间重分配。

Conclusion: 深度学习中较大的梯度下降步长会诱发“边缘稳定（Edge of Stability）”现象，从而打破梯度流预测的单一路径特化，促使网络在多路径间重新平衡信号，最终形成共享表示。

Abstract: Recent analyses of multi-pathway Deep Linear Networks use Gradient Flow to predict a "winner-takes-all" specialization in which path symmetry breaks and each feature concentrates in a single pathway. In this work, we show that discrete Gradient Descent (GD) with a large step size tells a different story. We prove that single-path solutions are sharp minima, whereas distributing signals across pathways reduces sharpness by a factor that decreases with both the number of pathways and depth. Consequently, while early training reproduces the depth-driven symmetry breaking predicted by GF, oscillations at the Edge of Stability subsequently override this tendency and drive the network into a re-balancing phase, where signals redistribute across pathways. Together, these results clarify how depth shapes pathway competition and explain why large-step GD favors shared representations rather than persistent single-pathway dominance.

</details>


### [210] [Differentiable Efficient Operator Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05232)
*Xiaohuan Pei,Jiyuan Zhang,Yuanfan Guo,Weiguo Feng,Tao Huang,Cho-Jui Hsieh,Chang Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种可导的框架，将多种多模态令牌缩减技术统一到一个搜索空间中，通过自动搜索提升了推理效率和模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多模态模型常依赖人工设计的令牌缩减方法（如剪枝、合并、池化等），这些方法往往是孤立存在的，缺乏统一的优化框架，且难以捕捉最优的交互动态。

Method: 提出“高效算子搜索”（Efficient Operator Search），通过构建一个包含层激活、保留预算和算子行为的可导搜索空间，联合优化令牌（token）的缩减位置、数量及处理方式。该框架在满足计算预算和成本约束的情况下，利用可导策略进行端到端优化。

Result: 实验证明，该方法不仅能涵盖现有的主流手动设计方案，还能发现更高效的混合算子，在多模态任务中特别是在极端视觉令牌缩减的情况下，取得了更优的准确率-效率权衡。

Conclusion: 研究表明，高效的多模态模型推理可以从手动设计算子转变为自动化的可导算子搜索，且自动发现的混合算子优于单一的手动设计方案。

Abstract: Efficient multimodal foundation models often rely on manually designed token-reduction operators, such as pruning, merging, pooling, and adaptive reweighting. Although these operators appear different, we show that they can be interpreted as distinct regimes of a shared operator space. Based on this view, we introduce Efficient Operator Search, a differentiable framework that jointly searches where to reduce tokens, how many tokens to retain, and how reduced token information should be processed. The proposed search space parameterizes layer activation, retention budget, and operator behavior, while the search policy optimizes task performance under one-sided budget and cost constraints. This formulation recovers representative hand-designed baselines as special cases and further discovers hybrid operators beyond isolated manual designs. Experiments on multimodal benchmarks show that the searched operators achieve competitive accuracy-efficiency trade-offs, especially under aggressive visual-token reduction. These results suggest that efficient multimodal inference can be reframed from manual operator design to differentiable operator search.

</details>


### [211] [DiffSlack: Learning under Nonlinear Inequality Constraints via Learnable Slack Variables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05247)
*Ziqian Wang,Chenxi Fang,Zhen Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DiffSlack是一种用于神经网络的可微分投影层，通过可学习的松弛变量和阻尼高斯-牛顿法，高效地将复杂的非线性不等式约束嵌入网络输出，实现了高质量、满足硬约束的预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的硬约束方法在处理大规模非线性问题时，存在结构限制过严或计算开销过大的问题，难以满足复杂工程需求。

Method: 1. 将不等式约束转化为包含可学习松弛变量的等式约束；2. 使用阻尼高斯-牛顿（Damped Gauss-Newton）方法进行投影；3. 引入两阶段课程学习（Curriculum learning）以稳定训练并提高约束满足度。

Result: 在车辆路径规划任务中，与现有学习方法相比，DiffSlack在同等计算预算下实现了更高的规划成功率和更强的几何约束满足度，并在实车测试中验证了轨迹的可执行性。

Conclusion: DiffSlack通过提供一种可微分的投影层，为将硬不等式约束嵌入神经网络提供了一种实用且可扩展的解决方案，特别适用于大规模非线性工程应用（如自动驾驶路径规划）。

Abstract: Enforcing nonlinear inequality constraints in neural networks remains challenging, especially when the output is subject to many coupled constraints. Existing hard constraint methods often impose structural restrictions on the constraint set or introduce substantial computational overhead for large-scale nonlinear problems. Here, we propose DiffSlack, a differentiable projection layer for nonlinear inequality-constrained neural prediction. DiffSlack reformulates inequalities as equalities with learnable slack variables, which are predicted as part of the augmented network output and provide a data-driven warm start for damped Gauss-Newton projection. The projection layer maps raw predictions onto the augmented feasible manifold while preserving end-to-end differentiability. A two-stage curriculum further stabilizes training and improves constraint satisfaction. We evaluate DiffSlack on vehicle path planning with 200 nonlinear inequality constraints from collision avoidance, curvature limits, and waypoint spacing. Compared with existing learning-based baselines, DiffSlack achieves a higher planning success rate and stronger geometric constraint satisfaction under a comparable inference budget. Ablation studies further show that the hard projection layer reduces sensitivity to supervision quality. Closed-loop tracking in CARLA and real-world vehicle experiments confirms the executability of the generated trajectories. These results demonstrate that DiffSlack provides a practical and scalable approach to embedding hard inequality constraints into neural networks for engineering applications.

</details>


### [212] [Alpha-RTL: Test-Time Training for RTL Hardware Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05253)
*Peilong Zhou,Zhirong Chen,Cangyuan Li,Haoyu Gao,Kaiyan Chang,Ziming Qu,Ying Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TTT-RTL是首个支持RTL设计测试时训练的LLM框架，通过将EDA反馈集成到策略更新循环中，实现了比静态LLM策略更优的硬件电路自动化综合与优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM辅助RTL设计方法多依赖预训练的通用策略，且测试阶段仅能进行搜索，无法针对特定硬件设计任务进行动态适配与持续优化。

Method: 提出TTT-RTL框架，在测试阶段对LLM进行强化学习微调：1. 通过EDA工具进行语法与仿真校验；2. 利用合成PPA指标评分；3. 通过PUCT索引的状态池重用高分方案；4. 采用自适应KL约束控制器（Adaptive KL-budget controller）稳定策略更新。

Result: 在RTLLM v2.0基准测试下，PPA乘积降低了65.1%，较最强基线提升26.1%；在工业级C910 FPU模块上实现了59.4%的ADP缩减。

Conclusion: 通过测试时训练（Test-time Training）将LLM与EDA反馈循环相结合，能显著超越冻结策略LLM的性能，证明了LLM在RTL生成中从实现“功能正确”迈向“物理性能优化”的可行性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have shown increasing promise in generating
  functionally correct register-transfer-level (RTL) hardware designs.
  Recent systems improve further through EDA-integrated reinforcement
  learning with syntax, simulation, and PPA rewards, but train a general
  RTL generator before deployment while test-time approaches search with
  a frozen policy. We instead perform reinforcement learning at test time,
  allowing the LLM policy to adapt to executable EDA feedback for the
  specific RTL problem at hand. We propose TTT-RTL, to our knowledge the
  first per-design test-time training framework that closes the loop
  between an LLM policy and an EDA pipeline for RTL optimization. TTT-RTL
  samples candidate implementations, verifies them through syntax checking
  and simulation, scores valid designs using synthesis-derived PPA product,
  reuses high-reward variants through a PUCT-indexed design-state pool,
  and updates the policy with an entropic policy-gradient objective. To
  stabilize policy updates under sparse or plateaued rewards, we introduce
  an adaptive KL-budget controller that adjusts the entropy constraint
  using reference KL, effective sample size, and reward saturation signals.
  On RTLLM v2.0 under Nangate 45nm, TTT-RTL reduces the geometric-mean
  PPA product by 65.1% over the reference, outperforming the strongest
  published frozen-policy agent baseline at 26.1%. On an industrial
  XuanTie C910 FPU leading-zero-anticipation unit under Sky130, TTT-RTL
  achieves a 59.4% ADP reduction, and ablations confirm that policy
  adaptation, state reuse, and KL-budget control each contribute. These
  results suggest that test-time training with executable EDA feedback can
  move LLM-based RTL generation beyond functional correctness toward
  physically optimized hardware.

</details>


### [213] [Sharp Low-Degree Thresholds for Planted-vs-Planted Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05266)
*Anda Skeja,Daniel Gutiérrez Espinoza,Fiona Skerman,Alexander S. Wein*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次建立了植入式模型中低度多项式测试的尖锐阈值理论，证明了在特定场景下区分不同植入机制的难度等同于恢复问题，并阐明了弱测试与任务间的本质性能差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了填补植入式设置（planted-vs-planted settings）下低度多项式测试尖锐阈值理论的缺失，并探究由两个结构化植入机制生成的数据之间的可区分性极限。

Method: 1. 引入植入式测试的理论架构，扩展了传统的潜变量展开（latent-variable expansion）方法；2. 开发了识别并剔除非信号贡献（non-signal contributions）的新技术，用于匹配低度多项式算法的上下界。

Result: 1. 给出了植入子矩阵（planted submatrix）和植入稠密子图（planted dense subgraph）模型中计数社区问题的尖锐阈值；2. 证明了该测试阈值与已知的低度恢复阈值在常数级别上完全一致；3. 区分了“强测试”的尖锐阈值特性与“弱测试”平滑过渡的特性。

Conclusion: 该研究建立了植入式（planted-vs-planted）统计推断问题的尖锐阈值理论，证明了低度多项式测试在特定模型中具有与恢复（recovery）问题一致的尖锐阈值，并揭示了弱测试（weak testing）与强测试（strong testing）在阈值性质上的本质区别。

Abstract: We establish the first sharp thresholds for low-degree polynomial tests in planted-vs-planted settings, where the goal is to determine with vanishing error which of two structured planted mechanisms generated the observed data. We prove matching low-degree upper and lower bounds for counting communities in the planted submatrix and planted dense subgraph models. The resulting testing threshold coincides, down to the sharp constant, with the known low-degree recovery threshold. In contrast, the task of weak testing, where the goal is to outperform random guessing, does not have a sharp threshold but rather a smooth transition, which we identify. To prove our results, we develop a framework for planted-vs-planted testing that builds on a latent-variable expansion originating in low-degree recovery and employs new methods to identify and prune non-signal contributions.

</details>


### [214] [Scaling Laws for Behavioral Foundation Models over User Event Sequences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05257)
*Rickard Brüel Gabrielsson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究揭示了行为基础模型的缩放定律：与语言模型不同，行为模型的计算最优配置必须将特定的业务评估指标集成到缩放策略中，且通常表现为小型嵌入器配合大规模序列训练的效果最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 行为基础模型（如推荐、支付、欺诈检测）在大规模序列数据上应用广泛，但目前缺乏类似于语言模型那样完善的计算缩放定律（Scaling Laws）指导，导致无法确定最优的模型配置策略。

Method: 研究了两部分架构（特征事件嵌入器 + 解码器 Transformer），在 $10^{15}$-$10^{19}$ 次训练 FLOPs 的规模下，通过 600 次实验联合调整四类关键维度：参数分配比例、临界批次大小、模型与数据分配、以及冻结嵌入器后的负样本采样数。

Result: 1. 计算最优架构倾向于使用极小的嵌入器（约 2% 参数）；2. 训练数据需求在低预算时较高，随计算量增加趋向于 Chinchilla 启发式；3. 损失函数与实际业务指标（Ranking metrics）存在差异，且这种差异随规模缩放；4. 在大模型预算下，负采样受限于内存而非计算功耗。

Conclusion: 行为基础模型具有独特的缩放行为，计算最优配置（参数分配、数据比例、负采样数量）与评估指标及计算预算密切相关。与语言模型不同，行为模型的缩放定律要求将评估指标纳入考虑，且其小型嵌入层（～2%参数）在不同计算规模下均表现最优。

Abstract: Foundation models are increasingly trained on sequences of user actions in recommendation, payments, fraud, and commerce, but these models still lack the kind of compute calibration that scaling laws provide for language models. We study a common two-part behavioral-model architecture: a feature-based event embedder maps each multi-modal item to a vector, and a decoder-only transformer predicts the next event from the resulting sequence. Across roughly 600 runs on real interaction data, spanning $10^{15}$-$10^{19}$ training FLOPs, we jointly vary four deployment-relevant axes: the two-part parameter split, critical batch size, model/data allocation, and the number of sampled negatives used after freezing the embedder. A small embedder ($s^{\star}\!\approx\!2\%$ of parameters) is compute-optimal at every budget we test because embedder parameters are both more expensive per step and exposed to far more repeated items than contextualizer parameters. Compute-optimal training is data-heavy relative to text at low compute, but its $D/N$ ratio moves toward the Chinchilla heuristic as compute increases. The sampled training objective and deployed ranking metrics disagree in ways that themselves scale: critical batch size, optimal negative count after freezing, and the agreement between loss and ranking quality all shift with compute and with the chosen evaluation metric. For negative sampling, larger budgets increasingly prefer more negatives; by $10^{19}$ FLOPs the active constraint is candidate-axis memory rather than FLOPs. In behavioral foundation models, the evaluation metric is therefore part of the scaling law: changing it can change the compute-optimal recipe.

</details>


### [215] [Flash-WAM: Modality-Aware Distillation for World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05254)
*Arman Akbari,Ci Zhang,Arash Akbari,Lin Zhao,Yixiao Chen,Weiwei Chen,Xuan Zhang,Geng Yuan,Yanzhi Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Flash-WAM 针对多模态世界动作模型的推理延迟问题，提出了一种模态感知的蒸馏算法，在实现单步实时推理的同时，有效克服了模态间噪声分布不对称带来的性能损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的世界动作模型（WAMs）虽然在控制任务中表现优异，但因需要进行多次去噪而无法满足机器人实时控制的需求；而传统的一致性蒸馏方法无法处理视频与动作模态间不对称的采样分布，导致性能下降。

Method: 提出了一种模态感知的蒸馏框架 Flash-WAM。针对视频和动作流不同的信噪比（SNR）分布，分别采用不同的一致性函数参数化方案：动作流采用线性梯度缩放（linear-gradient-scaling），视频流采用方差保持（variance-preserving）策略，以适应各自的噪声体制。

Result: 在 NVIDIA L40S 上，Flash-WAM 将推理速度提升了 23 倍（从 8.1 秒压缩至 348 毫秒），实现实时推理；同时在仿真环境（RoboTwin 2.0 和 LIBERO）及真实世界实验（Unitree G1）中保持了高度的任务成功率，显著优于传统的蒸馏方法。

Conclusion: Flash-WAM 通过模态感知蒸馏策略成功解决了世界动作模型（WAMs）在推理速度与任务成功率之间的权衡问题，实现了单步推理的实时性能，同时保持了接近原始模型的任务完成质量。

Abstract: World-action models (WAMs) jointly generate future video and robot actions through iterative diffusion, achieving strong performance on manipulation benchmarks but requiring tens of denoising steps, a cost that precludes real-time control. Step distillation has emerged as the natural remedy, but off-the-shelf methods break down in the joint video-action setting because video and action streams use different SNR-shifted noise schedules and reach training with substantially different marginal noise distributions, an asymmetry that single-modality distillation methods cannot accommodate. We introduce \textbf{Flash-WAM}, a modality-aware step-distillation framework inspired by consistency distillation that selects the consistency function for each modality to match its noise regime: a linear-gradient-scaling parametrization for the action stream's low-noise regime, paired with a variance-preserving parametrization for the video stream's high-noise regime, grounded in a structural analysis of the consistency-function family that characterizes the achievable gradient scaling under the consistency boundary condition. Instantiated on LingBot-VA, Flash-WAM compresses inference to a single step in each modality. On RoboTwin 2.0, this reduces per-chunk latency from $8.1$ seconds to $348$ ms on NVIDIA L40S, a $23{\times}$ speedup that enables real-time inference. Flash-WAM preserves task success on simulation benchmarks ($85.5\%$ RoboTwin 2.0, $95.7\%$ LIBERO) and substantially recovers real-world performance ($60\%$ average on a Unitree G1 humanoid robot), while naive consistency distillation drops to $24\%$ at the same step budget.

</details>


### [216] [Statistically Reliable LLM-Based Ranking Evaluation via Prediction-Powered Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05308)
*Abhishek Divekar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PRECISE 是一种结合人工与 LLM 的无偏性能评估框架，通过降低计算复杂度与偏差校正技术，显著提升了排序指标评估的效率与准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在排序系统中，人类评估成本高昂，而直接使用 LLM 进行评估存在偏差且不具备统计保证，因此需要一种方法能结合两者，实现既准确又高效的评价指标估计。

Method: 基于预测驱动推理（PPI），通过数学变换将分层指标的计算复杂度由 O(2^|C|) 降低至 O(2^K)；利用少量高质量人工标注对大规模 LLM 评测结果进行偏差校正。

Result: 在 ESCI 基准测试中，将 Precision@4 的标准误降低了 21%；在生产环境中，仅使用少量人工标注即正确识别了最优模型，并通过 A/B 测试实现了每日销售额 407 个基点的提升。

Conclusion: PRECISE 框架通过结合少量人类标注和大规模 LLM 评测，实现了对排序评价指标（如 Precision@K）的高效且无偏的校准，显著降低了估计的标准差，并在实际生产环境中验证了其指导模型迭代和提升业务指标的有效性。

Abstract: With PRECISE, we extended Prediction-Powered Inference to produce bias-corrected estimates of ranking evaluation metrics by combining a small human-labeled set with a large LLM-judged set. PPI is provably unbiased regardless of the LLM judge's error profile. We make it applicable to hierarchical metrics like Precision@K, where annotations are per-document but the metric is per-query, by reducing the output-space computation from O(2^|C|) to O(2^K). On the ESCI benchmark, augmenting 30 human annotations with Claude 3 Sonnet judgments reduces the standard error of Precision@4 estimates from 4.45 to 3.50 (a 21% relative reduction). In a production system, our framework correctly identified the best of three system variants from 100 human labels and 2 hours of domain-expert annotation; A/B testing confirmed this ranking with +407 bps in daily sales.

</details>


### [217] [Policy-Conditioned Counterfactual Credit for Verifiable Reinforcement Learning of Long-Horizon Language Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05263)
*Renwei Meng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CVT-RL通过引入反事实因果推理和可验证约束，解决了语言智能体在长程任务中因过度拟合过程奖励而产生的逻辑错误和欺骗性行为问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的过程奖励（Process Rewards）多仅限于相关性监督，无法判断步骤是否真实对任务成功有因果贡献，导致长程任务中智能体易出现错误推理链、信念漂移及为满足终端检查而采取的“走捷径”式作弊行为。

Method: 提出CVT-RL算法：包含干预有效性门控机制和策略调节的反事实贡献(PCCC)估计器；利用删除、语义/证据替换和工具输出扰动进行受控干预；采用增强拉格朗日乘子法施加不可篡改、安全调用等约束。

Result: 在Long-context QA、ALFWorld、ScienceWorld及网页工具任务中，CVT-RL将任务成功率提升至78.9%（优于对比方法），证据F1从78.9%提升至82.8%，并将“作弊”发生率从7.2%-8.1%显著降低至3.9%-4.6%。

Conclusion: 引入基于反事实因果推理的CVT-RL框架，通过对语言智能体长程任务中的每一步骤进行因果贡献评估和可验证性约束，可有效抑制错误证据链、信念漂移及“作弊”行为，显著提升智能体的推理可靠性与安全性。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards improves reasoning and tool use, yet long-horizon language agents still learn unsupported evidence chains, belief drift, and shortcut actions that satisfy terminal checks. Existing process rewards are mostly correlational: they reward retrieval-, reflection-, or verification-like steps without estimating whether the step contributes to final verified success under a specified intervention. We propose CVT-RL, a constrained policy-gradient algorithm with dense verifiable rewards, intervention-validity gating, and a policy-conditioned counterfactual contribution (PCCC) estimator. Deletion, semantic substitution, evidence substitution, and tool-output perturbation define separate controlled interventions; continuations are sampled from a frozen reference policy, and a selection-adjusted doubly robust estimator augments the advantage. Belief control uses only prefix-observable labels, while an augmented Lagrangian constrains unsupported claims, skipped verification, tool tampering, and unsafe calls. On long-context QA, ALFWorld, ScienceWorld, and web/tool tasks, CVT-RL improves average task success from 71.8% for compute-matched non-causal RL and 75.4% for an information-matched counterfactual-process baseline to 78.9%, improves evidence F1 from 78.9 to 82.8 over the information-matched baseline, and reduces measured hacking from 7.2% to 3.9%. Independent human audit estimates 4.6% hacking for CVT-RL versus 8.1% for the information-matched baseline, and adaptive detector-evasion attacks raise hacking only to 7.1%. Stratified bootstrap and mixed-effects tests give p<0.01 after Holm correction for all primary metrics. Carefully scoped counterfactual credit, paired with validity gating, diagnostics, and verifiable constraints, provides a reproducible route toward more reliable long-horizon RL for language agents.

</details>


### [218] [MolE-RAG: Molecular Structure-Enhanced Retrieval-Augmented Generation for Chemistry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05693)
*Joey Chan,Wonbin Kweon,Ashley Shin,Niharika Bhattacharjee,Pengcheng Jiang,Yue Guo,Jiawei Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MolE-RAG 框架通过在推理时引入化学文献、分子属性元数据及结构相似分子，有效弥补了大语言模型在分子性质预测中对化学结构理解的不足，无需微调即可显著提升预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在处理分子结构（如 SMILES）时存在理解鸿沟，主要原因是其训练数据主要是自然语言，缺乏对化学结构的深入推理能力。

Method: 提出了一种无需训练的框架 MolE-RAG，在推理阶段为大语言模型补充三种上下文信息：1) 检索到的相关化学文献；2) 分子特异性元数据（如同义词、功能基团、理化描述符）；3) 从训练集中检索到的结构相似分子。

Result: 在九项分子性质预测任务中，MolE-RAG 显著提升了性能。与仅使用 SMILES 的基线相比，通用大语言模型的分类任务 ROC-AUC 最高提升 28 个百分点，回归任务的 RMSE 最高降低 67%。研究还发现不同模型和任务对不同上下文源的依赖程度存在差异。

Conclusion: MolE-RAG 证明了在不进行模型微调的情况下，通过推理时增强化学上下文，可以显著提高大语言模型在分子性质预测任务中的表现，并为整合异构化学知识提供了一种灵活、有效的框架。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have shown promise for molecular property prediction, but their ability to reason over chemical structures remains limited, as molecular representations such as SMILES differ substantially from the natural language on which LLMs are primarily trained. To bridge this semantic and chemical knowledge gap, we propose MolE-RAG, a training-free, molecule-centric retrieval-augmented generation framework for LLM-based molecular property prediction. MolE-RAG augments each prediction with three complementary sources of inference-time context: retrieved chemistry literature, molecule-specific information including compound synonyms, identifiers, functional group annotations, and physicochemical descriptors, and structurally similar molecules retrieved from the training set. We evaluate MolE-RAG across nine molecular property prediction tasks using proprietary, chemistry-specialized, and open-source LLMs. Across general-purpose LLMs, MolE-RAG improves ROC-AUC by up to 28 percentage points on classification tasks and reduces regression RMSE by up to 67% relative to a SMILES-only baseline. We further find that the utility of each context source varies across models and tasks, with different models benefiting most from textual retrieval, molecular context, or structural retrieval. These results suggest that molecule-centric retrieval can improve LLM-based molecular property prediction without model fine-tuning while providing a flexible framework for integrating heterogeneous chemical knowledge at inference time.

</details>


### [219] [REGEN: Reference-Guided Synthetic Multivariate Time Series Generation for Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05264)
*Moulik Gupta,Dhruv Kumar,Murari Mandal,Saurabh Deshpande*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对小样本时间序列预测任务，ReGeN框架通过对参考序列进行组件解耦与结构化重构，实现了在保持领域特定结构的同时进行高质量数据增强，优于现有的生成对抗或先验建模技术。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列生成方法在小样本场景下存在局限：基于先验的方法缺乏领域针对性，而基于数据驱动的方法（如TimeGAN）缺乏对周期性、局部波动及跨变量动态的明确控制，导致无法有效支撑鲁棒模型的训练。

Method: 提出一种参考导向的生成框架（ReGeN）：将参考序列分解为三个可解释组件——1) 利用相位对齐的周期性骨架捕获形态；2) 利用深度核高斯过程建模变量残差；3) 利用结构因果模型引入滞后感知跨变量依赖。通过对这些组件进行可控温度采样来生成合成数据。

Result: 实验显示，ReGeN生成的合成数据可作为真实数据的有效替代，在交通流等周期性领域表现优异。基于ReGeN预训练的模型显著优于基于传统合成方法预训练的模型。

Conclusion: 在小样本环境下，通过对参考序列进行结构化解耦与可控合成（ReGeN），能够生成比现有数据驱动或基于先验的方法更具鲁棒性的数据，且在特定周期性领域中甚至能超越真实数据对预训练模型的提升效果。

Abstract: Training robust multivariate time series forecasting models requires large, diverse corpora, yet many real-world domains provide only a handful of observed sequences. Existing generators fail to resolve this mismatch: prior-based approaches (e.g., CauKer, TimePFN) produce domain-agnostic samples, while data-driven methods (e.g., TimeGAN) treat references as black-box supervision, forfeiting explicit control over periodic structure, local variability, and cross-variable dynamics.
  We propose ReGeN, a reference-guided generative pipeline that treats observed sequences not as examples to imitate, but as structural scaffolds for controllable synthesis. ReGeN decomposes each reference into three interpretable components: a phase-aligned periodic backbone capturing dominant domain morphology; per-variable stochastic residuals modeled with a deep-kernel Gaussian process; and lag-aware cross-variable dependencies injected through a structural causal model with fitted coupling coefficients. Sampling these components at controllable temperature broadens distributional coverage while preserving domain-grounded structure.
  We show that ReGeN-generated data consistently substitutes for real sibling data with minimal forecasting degradation, and in strongly periodic domains such as traffic, can outperform the real source itself. We further show that a foundation model pretrained on ReGeN corpora outperforms those pretrained on prior-based and data-driven synthetic alternatives. This suggests that in low-data regimes, how reference data is structurally exploited can matter as much as how much data is available.

</details>


### [220] [Data-efficient flood depth prediction through domain-aware coreset selection and tabular foundation models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05265)
*Lipai Huang,Adithi Srinath,Manas Singh,Junwei Ma,Ali Mostafavi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种结合表格基础模型与“领域感知核心集”的方法，极大地降低了洪水预测模型的训练数据需求，实现了无需针对性重训练的高精度跨流域洪水深度预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的监督学习 surrogate 模型存在两大痛点：一是每个流域需要海量训练数据，二是无法在原始网格之外进行泛化，这限制了其在近实时洪水预测中的应用。

Method: 提出了一种领域感知的核心集（Domain-aware coreset）构建管线，通过按重现期和受影响流域对风暴进行分层，并利用目标感知的空间选择器对六边形区域进行采样，从而在推理时引导（condition）表格基础模型。

Result: 仅使用单个流域训练数据池的 0.7% 作为核心集，模型在休斯顿九个流域中达到了 0.663 的平均 R²，达到了完全监督基准线（R²=0.673）的 98.5%；并具备良好的跨流域迁移能力及对分布外（OOD）数据的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 领域感知的核心集构建流程使表格基础模型能够实现数据高效、跨流域迁移的洪水预测，且无需针对每个流域进行微调或重训练。

Abstract: Near-real-time flood depth prediction demands surrogate models that are accurate, fast, and transferable across watersheds. Supervised surrogates can match physics-based simulators in accuracy but need millions of training rows per watershed and cannot extrapolate beyond their original mesh. We propose a domain-aware coreset construction pipeline that conditions a tabular foundation model at inference time. The pipeline stratifies storms by return period and most-affected watershed, then samples hexagons with a target-aware spatial selector. With 0.7% of the per-watershed training pool, the model attains a mean $R^2$ of 0.663 across nine Houston-area watersheds, within 98.5% of the supervised reference ($R^2$ = 0.673). It transfers to held-out watersheds without task-specific retraining, staying ahead of a coreset-trained supervised baseline. On real storms it exceeds the supervised reference on a far out-of-distribution case and trails it on a mostly in-distribution one. Domain-aware coreset construction lets tabular foundation models deliver data-efficient, watershed-transferable flood predictions without per-watershed training.

</details>


### [221] [Learning Manifold and Itô Dynamics with Branched Neural Rough Differential Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05272)
*Luke Thompson,Dai Shi,Lequan Lin,Junbin Gao,Andi Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对 NRDEs 在 Itô 动力学和流形计算中的局限性，本文提出了 B-NRDEs 框架，通过引入 Hopf 代数实现通用的几何数值积分，显著提升了模型对复杂随机动力学的建模能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 Neural Rough Differential Equations (NRDEs) 基于 shuffle 代数（对应 Stratonovich 计算），无法处理 Itô 动力学中的二次变分项，也难以直接应用于流形上的协变导数动力学。

Method: 引入分支神经粗糙微分方程 (B-NRDEs)；利用 Hopf 代数重构 log-ODE 步骤，实现几何数值积分；根据适用场景匹配相应的驱动代数（Grossman-Larson 树、Munthe-Kaas-Wright 树或 shuffle 代数）；引入分支签名核函数提升对 Itô 动力学中二次变分项的捕捉能力。

Result: B-NRDEs 能够精确保持流形约束，并在粗糙 Bergomi 波动率模型、SO(3) 动力学预测及 SPD 协方差动力学任务中展现出比传统方法更优的性能和更广泛的适用性。

Conclusion: B-NRDEs 框架成功将神经粗糙微分方程推广至非欧几里得空间和 Itô 计算范式，实现了对随机过程和流形约束动力学的高效、精确建模，在多个复杂动力学任务中表现优异。

Abstract: Neural rough differential equations (NRDEs) stay accurate under irregular sampling while taking far fewer integration steps than standard neural differential equations, summarising a finely sampled driver by its log-signature and advancing the hidden state over coarse intervals using the log-ODE method. This efficiency rests on the shuffle algebra, the algebraic counterpart of Stratonovich calculus. This reliance means NRDEs cannot expose the quadratic-variation terms Itô dynamics require, nor the ordered covariant derivatives that govern Itô flows on connection-equipped manifolds. Ameliorating this, we introduce Branched Neural Rough Differential Equations (B-NRDEs), a Hopf-algebraic framework that recasts the NRDE log-ODE step as geometric numerical integration on the state-space manifold, matching the driving algebra to the governing calculus: Grossman--Larson rooted trees for Euclidean Itô dynamics, Munthe-Kaas--Wright planar rooted trees for ordered covariant derivatives on manifolds, and the shuffle algebra in the classical Stratonovich case. This yields intrinsic coarse-step dynamics that exactly preserve manifold constraints. Finally, we introduce a branched signature-kernel objective to enable Itô-consistent law matching by making quadratic-variation terms visible during training. On rough Bergomi volatility, sim-to-real $\mathrm{SO}(3)$ dynamics forecasting, and SPD covariance dynamics, B-NRDEs offer a unified, effective approach to stochastic and manifold-valued dynamics beyond the Euclidean--Stratonovich setting.

</details>


### [222] [Anomaly Detection for Electro-Hydrostatic Actuators using LSTM Autoencoder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05274)
*Nehal Afifi,Abdelmonem Elhendawi,Felix Leitenberger,Nadine Piat,Sven Matthiesen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对EHA系统传感器数据特征，提出了一种基于LSTM自编码器的离线异常检测框架，有效解决了传统方法无法捕捉时序依赖的问题，实验证明其在多工况下具有极高的检测效能与稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统统计学与经典机器学习方法难以捕捉EHA（电动静液压执行器）传感器数据中的时序依赖性，导致异常检测准确率低、误报率高，且目前针对不同工况下EHA系统的数据驱动检测评估研究较为匮乏。

Method: 提出了一种基于LSTM（长短期记忆网络）自编码器的重构型异常检测框架，利用传感器信号的重构误差分布进行异常识别与评估。

Result: 该模型在多个故障注入场景中表现优异，平均准确率达99.0%，最高查准率（Precision）达100%，召回率（Recall）在90.2%至99.6%之间，F1-score在93.1%至99.8%之间。

Conclusion: 基于LSTM自编码器的离线异常检测框架在EHA系统传感器数据中表现出高灵敏度和超低误报率，证明了其在工业和航空航天领域应用的可行性，未来将进一步研究其在线实时部署。

Abstract: Electro-Hydrostatic Actuators (EHAs) are widely used in aerospace and industrial systems, where timely detection of sensor anomalies is essential to ensure safe and reliable operation. However, the large volume and high sampling frequency of EHA sensor data pose challenges for accurate and efficient anomaly detection. Conventional statistical and classical machine-learning methods such as Z-score, Interquartile Range (IQR), Median Absolute Deviation (MAD), Isolation Forest, Gaussian Mixture, and k-means often fail to capture the temporal dependencies inherent in EHA signals, resulting in limited detection accuracy and elevated false-alarm rates. Furthermore, systematic evaluations of data-driven anomaly detection approaches for EHA systems remain scarce, particularly under varying operational conditions. This study presents an offline anomaly-detection framework for univariate EHA sensor signals, focusing on temperature and pressure data collected from a controlled test bench. The method employs a reconstruction-based Long Short-Term Memory (LSTM) autoencoder, calibrated and evaluated using validation-set reconstruction-error distributions. Performance is assessed across multiple fault-injection scenarios using accuracy, precision, recall, and F1-score, complemented by sensitivity analyses under varying operating conditions. The LSTM autoencoder achieved an average accuracy of 99.0\%, precision up to 100\%, recall between 90.2\% and 99.6\%, and F1-scores from 93.1\% to 99.8\%, demonstrating high detection sensitivity and a very low false-alarm rate across all evaluated sensors. These results highlight the feasibility of data-driven offline anomaly detection for EHAs. Future work will focus on adapting the developed framework for an online (real-time) environment.

</details>


### [223] [Agentic Monte Carlo: Simulating Reinforcement Learning for Black-Box Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05296)
*Dae Yon Hwang,Raunaq Suri,Valentin Villecroze,Anthony L. Caterini,Jesse C. Cresswell,Noël Vouitsis,Brendan Leigh Ross*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了 Agentic Monte Carlo (AMC)，一种针对黑盒 LLM 智能体的推理时优化框架。通过将强化学习视为贝叶斯推断，AMC 无需修改模型参数，利用序贯蒙特卡洛采样和价值函数引导，在 AgentGym 测试中实现了优于传统微调方法（如 GRPO）的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的强化学习方法通常需要修改模型权重（Open-weight），而对于 API 调用式的黑盒模型（Black-box），由于无法进行参数优化，传统的 RL 方法不可行。因此需要一种在推理阶段优化黑盒智能体的方法。

Method: 利用强化学习与贝叶斯推断之间的等价性，将黑盒 LLM 视为先验模型，通过序贯蒙特卡洛（Sequential Monte Carlo）方法对轨迹后验进行采样。该方法通过学习一个价值函数来引导黑盒模型的生成行为，而无需对模型进行参数微调。

Result: 在 AgentGym 基准测试的三个不同环境中进行了验证，结果显示 AMC 显著优于传统的提示词工程（prompting）基线，并且随着推理时计算量的增加，其表现甚至超过了基于权重的 GRPO 算法。

Conclusion: 本文提出的 Agentic Monte Carlo (AMC) 证明了在不改变黑盒模型参数的情况下，通过推理时计算（test-time compute）对大模型智能体进行原则性的 RL 风格优化是切实可行的。

Abstract: LLM agents operate in two distinct regimes: open-weight agents amenable to reinforcement learning (RL) and black-box agents whose behaviour must be controlled purely at test time. Although black-box agents are often backed by state-of-the-art proprietary LLMs, API-only access precludes parameter-level optimization, rendering most RL methods inapplicable. To address this limitation, we turn to a known equivalence between RL and Bayesian inference. We propose Agentic Monte Carlo (AMC) to directly sample from the optimal policy of a black-box agent rather than training it through RL. The optimal policy is a posterior over trajectories whose prior we define as the fixed black-box LLM agent. We employ Sequential Monte Carlo to sample from this posterior by learning a value function to steer the agent while leaving the underlying black-box model unchanged. We validate AMC on three diverse environments from the AgentGym benchmark, demonstrating significant improvements over prompting baselines and even outperforming Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) as we scale the test-time compute of our method. AMC demonstrates the feasibility of performing principled RL-style optimization of black-box LLM agents. Code is available at https://github.com/layer6ai-labs/Agentic-Monte-Carlo

</details>


### [224] [Multimarginal flow matching with optimal transport potentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05327)
*Raghav Kansal,David Crair,Nghia Nguyen,Scott Pope,Bradley Parry*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了OTP-FM，这是一种将中间观测边缘分布约束整合到流匹配框架中的新型高效算法，通过动态最优传输势能引导流向，有效提升了复杂时空动态系统的建模能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的流匹配（FM）主要关注两个端点分布之间的映射，未能充分利用动态系统中常见的中间观测边际分布，而这些中间信息对于精确建模科学领域中的时间演化过程至关重要。

Method: 利用流匹配（FM）与动态最优传输（OT）之间的联系，通过在动态OT动作中引入势能项（potential terms），将中间边际分布的约束软性地融入到条件流匹配的学习目标中，从而开发出一种无需模拟（simulation-free）的算法。

Result: OTP-FM在单细胞RNA测序、海洋学和气象学等多样化数据集上展现了最先进（SOTA）的性能和显著的训练效率。

Conclusion: 提出了OTP-FM算法，通过在流匹配目标中引入动态最优传输势能项，实现了高效、灵活且具备时空感知能力的多元边际分布建模，并在多个科学应用领域取得了SOTA性能。

Abstract: Flow matching (FM) has emerged as a powerful framework for learning dynamic transport maps between two empirical distributions. However, less explored is the setting with intermediate observed marginals that can help constrain the flows between the endpoints. This "multimarginal" regime is central to modeling temporal evolution in dynamical systems in many scientific domains that can sample sequential distributions. We tackle this problem with a novel approach that leverages the connection between FM and dynamic optimal transport (OT), softly steering the flow towards the intermediate marginals through potential terms in the dynamic OT action. By extending the conditional FM learning target to incorporate these potentials, we derive an efficient, simulation-free algorithm for multimarginal FM that offers considerable flexibility in the spatiotemporal dynamics of the learned flows. We demonstrate state-of-the-art performance and training efficiency of OT-potential FM (OTP-FM) on diverse single-cell RNA sequencing, oceanographic, and meteorological datasets. Our code is available at https://github.com/Bexorg-Inc/OTP-FM.

</details>


### [225] [A prism hierarchy of learning regimes in large linear autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05335)
*Eugene Golikov,Yaroslav Gusev,Dmitry Yarotsky*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过几何建模（三角形棱柱），系统揭示了权重绑定线性自编码器在不同参数尺度下的五种极端学习动态，并对其中四种推导出了精确的性能演化解析解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器学习理论多关注单一的限制性机制，缺乏对特定模型中所有定性不同的极端学习状态的系统性认识，特别是在权重非线性导致梯度流无一般解析解的情况下。

Method: 通过形式化损失展开（formal loss-expansion hierarchy）分析，将模型参数（输入维度、潜在维度、初始化幅度和训练集大小）映射到一个几何结构（三角形棱柱），并针对五个极端区域推导了梯度流下的训练损失和总体损失的显式演化表达式。

Result: 提出了五个基本极端学习区域：大规模数据、小规模数据、平均场、窄潜在空间和自由状态。除自由状态外，其余四个区域所得的理论预测与实验结果高度吻合。

Conclusion: 该研究通过将权重绑定线性自编码器的训练动态映射到一个三角形棱柱的各个面，成功将各种极端学习行为系统化，为理解非线性模型在不同参数限制下的学习动态提供了统一的理论框架。

Abstract: Theoretical studies of machine learning models commonly consider different limiting regimes in which the learning dynamics of gradient descent becomes theoretically tractable. It is, however, desirable to have a systematically obtained picture of all qualitatively different extreme learning regimes for a particular type of models. In this paper we propose such a picture for large weight-tied linear autoencoders characterized by input and latent dimensions, initialization magnitude, and training set size. This model is nonlinear in the weights and its gradient flow does not have a general theoretical solution. We show that at the level of the formal loss-expansion hierarchy, its extreme regimes are naturally associated with faces of a triangular prism. In particular, there are five basic extreme regimes associated with the 2-faces of the prism: (1) large-data, (2) small-data, (3) mean-field, (4) narrow-latent, and (5) free. For regimes (1,2,3,4), we derive explicit expressions for both train and population limiting loss evolutions under gradient flow, obtaining very good agreement with experimental results.

</details>


### [226] [Mamba-Assisted Non-Markovian Closure for Reduced-Order Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05371)
*Zhi-Feng Wei,Saad Qadeer,Panos Stinis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 作者提出了基于Mamba架构的MAC框架，通过处理动力系统中的非马尔可夫记忆效应来提升降阶模型的预测精度，兼顾了高效的并行训练与低成本的递归推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维动力系统降阶模拟中，未解析变量对解析变量的影响通常表现为非马尔可夫闭合项，传统建模难以有效处理此类记忆效应。

Method: 提出了Mamba-Assisted Closure (MAC)框架，将闭合建模视为序列建模问题。利用Mamba架构处理非马尔可夫记忆效应，结合状态空间模型的卷积训练模式（支持并行计算）和循环推理模式（具备恒定的单步推理成本），并通过数值积分器与降阶方程耦合。

Result: 在粘性Burgers方程和混沌Lorenz '96系统中，MAC模型在预测准确性和长时演化稳定性方面均优于马尔可夫降阶模型、GRU序列模型及Wilks方法。

Conclusion: MAC框架通过利用状态空间模型的双重表示，有效解决了高维动力系统降阶模拟中非马尔可夫闭合项建模的难题，在预测精度和长时稳定性上显著优于传统方法。

Abstract: Reduced-order modeling of high-dimensional dynamical systems is often hindered by the non-Markovian closure term that represents the effect of unresolved variables on the resolved dynamics. Inspired by the Mori--Zwanzig formalism, in which the closure takes the form of a memory functional of the resolved trajectory, we recast closure modeling as a sequence modeling problem and propose the Mamba-Assisted Closure (MAC) framework: a Mamba-based sequence model, trained to predict the closure from the resolved trajectory, is coupled with the reduced-order governing equations through a numerical integrator to advance the resolved variables in time. A key feature of the framework is its exploitation of the dual representation of state-space models -- the model is trained in a sequence-to-sequence fashion via the convolutional form, and deployed for step-by-step autoregressive rollout via the recurrent form, yielding both efficient long-trajectory training and constant per-step inference cost. On the viscous Burgers' equation and the chaotic two-scale Lorenz '96 system, the MAC model substantially outperforms the Markovian reduced-order model, the GRU-based sequence model, and the Wilks method in predictive accuracy and long-time rollout stability.

</details>


### [227] [PJ-RoPE: A Fourier-Jet-Affine Position Space for Relative Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05345)
*Yaobo Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PJ-RoPE 将 RoPE、Jordan-RoPE 和 ALiBi 统一为同一个可学习的相对位置空间，并利用差分方程根的理论框架，揭示了不同位置编码在特定任务中的选择机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的相对位置编码方法（如 RoPE 的傅里叶相位、Jordan-RoPE 的有限喷流、ALiBi 的仿射近因性）在结构上存在差异，缺乏统一的数学视角来解析它们在不同任务中的表现及内在联系。

Method: 提出了 PJ-RoPE（Fourier-Jet-Affine）框架，将位置编码抽象为有限常系数差分模的根。通过引入标量 PJ-偏差核与精确旋转特征变换的分离、自适应扇区诊断工具以及 LC/rapidity 坐标系，实现了对位置信息的建模与高阶项的稳定化。

Result: 通过受控探针验证了不同任务对位置空间区域的选择性；语言建模实验揭示了仿射/近因性边界；音乐流处理展示了高阶项对位置信息的测量贡献；LC 诊断显示了尺度稳定性与相位分辨率之间的取舍。

Conclusion: PJ-RoPE 成功将现有的位置编码方法（RoPE, Jordan-RoPE, ALiBi）统一为一个可学习的相对位置空间。实验表明，不同任务对该空间中的不同区域（Fourier/Jet/Affine）有明显的选择偏好，该框架不仅具备理论上的统一性，且在实际任务中展现了良好的可解释性与性能稳定性。

Abstract: We unify RoPE's Fourier phase, Jordan-RoPE's finite jets, and ALiBi's affine recency into a single learnable relative-position space, and study which regions of this space are selected by different tasks. PJ-RoPE is a Fourier-Jet-Affine formulation for relative attention, with an optional Poincare-type reading as the affine completion of a homogeneous Fourier-jet positional representation. Algebraically, the same primitives form a finite constant-coefficient difference module: simple roots of the lag-shift operator give Fourier/RoPE characters, repeated nonzero roots give Jordan/Fourier jets, and the repeated unit root gives ALiBi-like affine recency.
  The framework separates scalar PJ-bias kernels from exact PJ-rotary feature transforms, introduces adaptive sector diagnostics, and uses LC/rapidity coordinates to stabilize high-order jets. Controlled probes verify sector containment and selection; small language runs expose an affine/recency boundary; music-token streams provide the clearest case where LC/affine variants remain strong while carrying measurable high-order corrections; and LC diagnostics show a scale-stability gain coupled to phase-resolution loss.

</details>


### [228] [GOTabPFN: From Feature Ordering to Compact Tokenization for Tabular Foundation Models on High-Dimensional Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05441)
*Al Zadid Sultan Bin Habib,Md Younus Ahamed,Prashnna Kumar Gyawali,Gianfranco Doretto,Donald A. Adjeroh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了GOTabPFN，通过GO-LR特征排序和NSC元特征压缩技术，使小规模表格基础模型能高效处理高维小样本预测任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决小规模表格基础模型（如TabPFN）在面对高维小样本（HDLSS）数据时，受限于Token预算及特征维度大导致的性能与稳定性挑战。

Method: 引入图引导排序与局部细化（GO-LR）方法，将其等价转化为加权最小线性排列问题；利用TSP路径代理求解器及神经启发式子单元压缩（NSC）技术，将相邻特征聚合为元特征。

Result: 在多个表格基准测试中，GOTabPFN在严格的Token预算约束下，显著提升了预测的稳定性和准确度。

Conclusion: GOTabPFN通过结合特征排序策略与神经启发式压缩，在不重训大模型的前提下，有效解决了小规模表格基础模型在“高维小样本”（HDLSS）场景下的性能瓶颈。

Abstract: We investigate how to make small tabular foundation models effective for High-Dimensional, Low-Sample Size (HDLSS) tabular prediction without retraining large backbones. We introduce Graph-guided Ordering with Local Refinement (GO-LR), show its equivalence to weighted Minimum Linear Arrangement, and interpret the practical solver as a TSP-path-style surrogate. We propose GOTabPFN,which builds on GO-LR, and a Neuro-Inspired Subunit Compression (NSC) unit to pool locally adjacent ordered features into meta-features, yielding a compact representation that makes TabPFN-style prediction practical in HDLSS regimes. Across tabular benchmarks, GOTabPFN improves stability and accuracy under tight token budgets.

</details>


### [229] [Mitigating the Curse of Dimensionality in Uniform Convergence of Deep Neural Networks via Smooth Activations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05599)
*Yizhe Ding,Runze Li,Jia Liu,Lingzhou Xue*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出ReLU网络在一致收敛方面存在维度灾难，并提出平滑激活函数DNN作为替代方案，通过理论推导证明其能有效克服维度灾难，提供更可靠的统计学习保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准ReLU网络在$L^2(P)$范数下表现良好，但在一致收敛行为上存在维度灾难（curse of dimensionality）；为满足下游任务中对最坏情况可靠性的需求，需要寻找具有强一致性保证的替代架构。

Method: 建立了平滑激活函数DNN的理论框架；推导了伪维度边界（pseudo-dimension bounds）、非渐近逼近保证及Hölder范数边界；在Huber、最小二乘、分位数和逻辑回归等多种统计背景下，推导了非渐近一致收敛速率。

Result: 证明了平滑DNN通过自适应地利用目标函数的低维层次结构，克服了一致收敛中的维度灾难；实验与应用研究验证了其理论分析的有效性。

Conclusion: 平滑激活函数的深度神经网络（Smooth DNNs）在处理需要一致性保证（uniform guarantees）的统计学习任务中，是比ReLU网络更具理论基础且实际可行的替代方案，能够有效克服维度灾难。

Abstract: This paper establishes a theoretical framework for the uniform convergence of smoothly activated deep neural network (DNN) estimators. While standard ReLU networks achieve minimax-optimal rates in the $L^2(P)$ norm for various nonparametric regression tasks, we establish a theoretical lower bound demonstrating that least-squares ReLU estimators can suffer from the curse of dimensionality in their uniform convergence behavior. Motivated by the need for reliable uniform guarantees in downstream tasks requiring worst-case reliability, we address this limitation by analyzing smoothly activated DNNs (smooth DNNs), encompassing both feedforward and residual structures. We establish novel pseudo-dimension bounds, non-asymptotic approximation guarantees, and Hölder-norm bounds for the approximators of these models. Leveraging these results, we derive non-asymptotic uniform convergence rates for smooth DNN estimators across multiple statistical contexts, including Huber, least-squares, quantile, and logistic regression. We prove that smooth DNNs can mitigate the {curse of dimensionality} in uniform convergence by adaptively exploiting the low-dimensional hierarchical composition structure of the target function. Supported by both simulation studies and a real-world application, our results position smooth DNNs as a theoretically grounded and practically viable alternative to ReLU networks for statistical learning tasks requiring uniform guarantees.

</details>


### [230] [Evidence-Guided Neural Architecture Selection under Uncertainty for Subject-Specific Blood Glucose Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05373)
*Md Azharul Islam,Dwyer Deighan,Tarunraj Singha,Danial Faghihi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 为解决复杂异构时间序列数据中的架构选择难题，开发了 EVIDENT 框架，利用贝叶斯推断和模型容量控制，实现了更小巧、更可靠且泛化性更强的神经网络模型发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在数据受限、高噪声和异构的时间序列预测任务中，传统的启发式架构设计和验证方法难以保证预测的准确性和泛化能力，导致模型容易出现欠拟合或过拟合。

Method: 提出 EVIDENT 框架，集成了贝叶斯训练、基于证据的排名和面向任务的不确定性验证。该方法在候选架构池中搜索，通过评估模型容量与验证标准的契合度，筛选出性能最佳且参数适中的模型，并支持基于似然加权的集成预测。

Result: 在 1 型糖尿病患者血糖预测任务中，EVIDENT 能有效排除过度参数化或欠参数化的模型，其筛选出的架构在未见过的患者数据上表现出更强的一致性和更高的预测精度，且优于随机搜索基准。

Conclusion: EVIDENT 是一种有效的神经架构选择策略，能在数据有限、噪声大且异构的场景下实现更可靠的预测，特别适用于高风险的时间序列预测任务。

Abstract: Reliable neural architecture selection is an open challenge in time-series forecasting under limited, noisy, and heterogeneous data, where standard heuristic architecture design and validation approaches fail to ensure accurate and reliable prediction and generalization. We propose EVIDENT (EVidence-based IDEntification of Neural archiTectures), a framework for architecture selection that integrates Bayesian training, evidence-based ranking, and task-specific validation under uncertainty. The framework explores the candidate architecture pool and identifies the lowest-capacity model that satisfies a prescribed validation criterion. We demonstrate this method using temporal convolutional networks (TCNs) for individualized blood glucose forecasting in type 1 diabetes patients. The results show that EVIDENT systematically rejects both under- and over-parameterized TCN architectures on population-level diabetes data, while identifying models that generalize reliably to unseen patients. When multiple architectures are competitive, the framework further supports plausibility-weighted ensemble predictions that enhance predictive performance. Compared with a random-search baseline, EVIDENT identified smaller architectures with more consistent forecasting performance on unseen patients. These findings establish EVIDENT as a strategy to neural architecture discovery, enabling reliable model selection for high-consequence forecasting in data-limited and heterogeneous settings.

</details>


### [231] [Zero-Copy Semantic Contagion: An In-Memory Streaming Architecture for Evolving Attention Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05733)
*Kabir Murjani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本论文提出了一种基于实时新闻流的动态跨公司注意力模型。通过 Rust 优化的流式架构处理数据，并利用神经霍克斯过程建模公司间的传播效应，有效解决了单资产模型忽视市场连锁反应的缺陷，显著提升了金融趋势预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有金融预测模型多为单资产模型，无法捕捉跨公司间的联动效应（如供应链冲击引起的连锁反应），导致对重大市场突发事件的预判滞后。

Method: 构建了一个异构的 Rust-Python 流式架构。Ingestion 端采用零拷贝 Rust 边缘解析器，Inference 端采用基于多变量神经霍克斯过程（Neural Hawkes Process）的模型，结合持续时间 LSTM 状态和双线性潜在投影，并通过自适应剪枝规则控制计算成本。

Result: 系统在单台普通 CPU 上实现了约 13 ms 的端到端处理延迟。在 FNSPID 数据集测试中，其预测精度较随机基准提升 1.70 倍，较同行业基准提升 3.36 倍。若移除图拓扑结构，预测精度则降为零。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了跨公司图拓扑结构是捕捉金融市场连锁反应的决定性因素。引入基于实时新闻流的动态图注意力机制，相比传统的单资产预测模型，能显著提升市场波动的预测精度。

Abstract: Per-ticker forecasting models dominate financial time-series work yet remain blind to cross-company propagation: a foundry disruption in Taiwan does not register in a single-asset model until Apple's own price has already moved. To address this limitation, we introduce a heterogeneous Rust-Python streaming architecture that maps cross-company attention as a continuous-time graph driven directly from text. We show that on the ingestion side, a zero-copy Rust edge parses news records in $\sim$100 ns and scans the target equity universe in $\sim$1.2 $μ$s. On the inference end, a multivariate Neural Hawkes Process featuring per-node continuous-time LSTM states and a bilinear latent projection propagates directed excitation, while an adaptive pruning rule bounds the computational cost of dynamic neighborhood updates. Combining these stages, we demonstrate an end-to-end processing latency of $\sim$13 ms per incoming news record on a single commodity CPU. Evaluated on a one-month temporal holdout of the FNSPID corpus (638 articles across 47 tickers), the system delivers a $1.70\times$ precision lift over random at the 90th-percentile next-day return threshold, and $3.36\times$ over a same-sector baseline. Crucially, removing the graph topology collapses precision to zero, confirming that the dynamic attention network is the sole driver of cross-company signal in this architecture.

</details>


### [232] [SHALA-LLM: Smartly Handling Ambiguous Labels in Aligning LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05376)
*Jingyao Wu,Ashley Wang,Keane Ong,Paul Pu Liang,Rosalind Picard*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SHALA-LLM 是一种新的强化学习对齐算法，它将标注样本中的不确定性和分歧视为关键信息而非噪声，通过直接学习人类标注分布，显著提升了 LLM 在处理复杂、歧义性强任务时的准确度与模型鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 对齐方法通常假设单一“正确”标签，忽略了自然语言推理 (NLI) 和情绪识别 (ER) 等任务中客观存在的人类标注分歧。这种做法丢弃了重要的不确定性信息，限制了模型在复杂真实场景下的表现。

Method: 提出了一种名为 SHALA-LLM (SHALA-LLM: Smartly Handling Ambiguous Labels in Aligning LLMs) 的强化学习框架。该方法直接学习标注者的分布，并引入动态加权机制，在优化过程中优先处理高度模糊的样本，从而保留并利用标注分歧。

Result: 在 ChaosNLI、GoEmotions 和 MSP-Podcast 等基准测试中，SHALA-LLM 表现优异。在 ChaosNLI 上，该方法将 Jensen-Shannon 距离降低了高达 62.1%，同时 F1 分数提升了高达 16.7%。

Conclusion: 通过将标注的不确定性视为有价值的信息而非噪声，SHALA-LLM 框架不仅能更精准地捕捉人类判断的全部分布，还能显著提升模型在处理模糊任务时的分类性能（F1 分数），证明了建模标注者分歧对于提升 LLM 对齐效果的重要性。

Abstract: Many human-centered tasks, including natural language inference (NLI) and emotion recognition (ER), have multiple plausible interpretations, leading to label ambiguity and challenging disagreements across human annotators. As LLMs are increasingly deployed in real-world settings, faithfully modeling such ambiguity is essential to identify contested inputs, preserve variability in ambiguous cases, and capture the full distribution of human judgments. Yet, existing LLM alignment approaches have predominantly assumed a single correct label, excluding annotator disagreement during optimization. Instead of treating this ambiguity as noise, we show how to treat it as information that improves model behavior through a new algorithm called SMARTLY HANDLING AMBIGUOUS LABELS IN ALIGNING LLMS (SHALA-LLM). This reinforcement learning framework provides a new way for LLMs to learn directly from annotator distributions while dynamically prioritizing highly ambiguous samples during optimization. Experiments on ambiguity-sensitive NLI and ER benchmarks, including ChaosNLI, GoEmotions, and MSP-Podcast, demonstrate that SHALA-LLM improves agreement with annotator label distributions, e.g. on ChaosNLI, it reduces Jensen-Shannon Distance by up to 62.1%. At the same time, SHALA-LLM improves F1 by up to 16.7%, showing that modeling annotator disagreement can also strengthen classification performance.

</details>


### [233] [Causal Longitudinal Prior-Fitted Networks for Counterfactual Outcome Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05797)
*Amirhossein Zare,Amirhessam Zare,Herlock Rahimi,Reza Salarikia,Mohammad Kashkooli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CausalLongPFN 是一种因果预训练框架，通过在合成的通用因果序列上进行预训练，实现了无需领域微调即可处理复杂纵向观测数据的因果预测，显著提升了模型在数据受限或训练资源受限场景下的通用性和实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纵向治疗决策面临包含时变混杂、患者动态异质性以及特定领域数据匮乏等挑战。传统的纵向因果估计器通常需要针对每个队列或模拟器重新训练模型，计算成本高且在数据稀缺场景下表现受限。

Method: 提出 CausalLongPFN（因果纵向先验拟合网络）。该模型在由时序结构因果模型（SCM）生成的合成数据上进行全量预训练，涵盖了处理混杂因素、异质性、非线性演化及延迟处理效应的机制。在测试阶段，模型保持冻结状态，通过上下文学习（In-context learning）接收支撑轨迹、查询历史及未来处理序列，直接输出预测分布，且无需梯度更新或倾向性得分建模。

Result: 在癌症、HIV 及华法林等带有反事实标签的因果基准测试中，CausalLongPFN 与领域训练的纵向基线模型具有竞争力。在仅包含事实标签的 MIMIC-III ICU 滚动预测任务中也表现出色。

Conclusion: CausalLongPFN 证明了基于广泛合成因果先验的预训练模型在处理纵向因果推断任务时，无需针对特定领域进行训练即可达到或超过现有基线方法的性能，为高昂或不可行的领域特定训练提供了一种高效的替代方案。

Abstract: Longitudinal treatment decisions require predicting potential outcomes under future treatment sequences in the presence of time-varying confounding, heterogeneous patient dynamics, and limited domain-specific data. Existing longitudinal causal estimators typically train a new model for each cohort or simulator. We introduce Causal Longitudinal Prior-Fitted Networks (CausalLongPFN), a prior-fitted in-context predictor for longitudinal causal prediction. The model is pretrained entirely on synthetic episodes sampled from a broad prior over temporal structural causal models, exposing it to treatment-confounder feedback, latent heterogeneity, nonlinear state evolution, delayed effects, and cumulative treatment responses. At test time, CausalLongPFN is frozen: it conditions on support trajectories, a query history, and a proposed future treatment sequence, and returns a predictive distribution over future outcomes without gradient updates or propensity-model fitting. Multi-step predictions are obtained by recursively applying the one-step predictor under the specified treatment sequence. We evaluate on branchable cancer, HIV, and warfarin benchmarks with ground-truth counterfactual labels, and on factual-only rolling-origin prediction in MIMIC-III ICU trajectories. CausalLongPFN is competitive with domain-trained longitudinal baselines on counterfactual benchmarks and performs strongly on factual MIMIC-III prediction, suggesting that broad synthetic causal pretraining can provide a useful frozen alternative when repeated domain-specific training is costly or impractical.

</details>


### [234] [Pattern Selectivity is Not Task-Causal Structure: A Cross-Architecture Mechanistic Study of Composed-Task Circuits in 1B-Class Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05378)
*Yongzhong Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究通过对比不同模型对相同任务的实现方式，发现尽管模型表现出相似的行为，但其内部的注意力电路机制截然不同，挑战了可解释性结论在模型间的通用性假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究常假设模型之间的机制可以类比或移植，但作者质疑这种假设。其动机是检验单一机械可解释性分析流程是否能在不同训练流水线的模型中产生一致的机制结论，并量化不同模型在实现相同任务时的机制差异。

Method: 1. 构建统一的“筛选-消融（screen-and-ablate）”协议：先通过任务模式选择性识别注意力头电路，再通过匹配随机对照（matched-random null）进行因果消融验证。
2. 跨模型对比：使用 Pythia 1B、OLMo 1B 和 OLMoE 1B-7B 三种不同架构和训练数据的模型，测试四项复合任务（IOI、Greater-than、Successor sequences、Variable binding）。
3. 提出五类分类法（Screen-outcome taxonomy）：将筛选结果定量分类为：主要原因、次要原因、相关物、干扰项、空值。

Result: 1. 本应一致的机制在不同模型中呈现出高度异构性：12个任务-模型组合中不存在两个具有相同主要因果电路的案例。
2. 实验涵盖了五类分类法中的所有结果，证明电路实现缺乏统一性。
3. 发现混合专家模型（MoE）倾向于利用“前一个Token”作为基础性的位置基质（positional substrate）来构建复杂任务电路。

Conclusion: 机械可解释性研究中，即使面对相同的任务和相似的模型规模，不同训练流水线的模型也会采用根本不同的电路（Attention-head circuits）来实现同一功能。这证明了模型内部表征的“不可移植性”，强调了对每个模型进行独立的因果验证（Causal Verification）是必要的。

Abstract: We test whether a single screen-and-ablate recipe -- identify attention-head circuits by task-pattern selectivity, then verify by causal ablation against a matched-random null -- produces consistent mechanistic claims across model families. The recipe ports across pipelines; the specific circuit it identifies does not. Across four composed tasks (indirect-object identification, greater-than, successor sequences, variable binding) and three 1B-class language models from distinct training pipelines (Pythia 1B / Pile / dense; OLMo 1B / DCLM / dense; OLMoE 1B-7B / DCLM / mixture-of-experts), we run a unified protocol with the matched-random null sampled across ten seeds per cell. The resulting 12 (task, model) cells contain no two that share the same primary causal screen at comparable effect size: the same task, with the same behavioral capability, is implemented through different attention-pattern types across models.
  We introduce a five-category screen-outcome taxonomy -- primary cause, secondary cause, correlate, interferer, null -- with quantitative thresholds, and show that all five outcomes appear in the panel. We propose a falsifiable hypothesis: the MoE model in our panel builds composed-task circuits on top of a foundational previous-token positional substrate (the prev-token-circuit ablation is the strongest causal screen on 3 of 4 tasks for OLMoE 1B-7B), with the IOI exception consistent with IOI being a final-position name-copying task whose structure directly probes a different pattern. The hypothesis comes with explicit predictions for other MoE language models.
  We frame the methodology honestly: the spectral participation-ratio signal from the companion methodology paper is a general indicator of specialized computation; what makes a finding task-specific is the task-pattern screen plus a per-model causal verification.

</details>


### [235] [Dead Directions: Geometric Singular Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05957)
*Tejas Pradeep Shirodkar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文统一了奇异学习理论与信息几何，通过引入“退化方向”概念，实现了在原始参数空间中直接计算奇异模型的关键几何属性（如RLCT），并据此优化了深度学习优化算法（DDCAdam）及模型分析方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 奇异学习理论（SLT）与信息几何在处理过度参数化模型时存在脱节：前者依赖复杂的分辨率坐标，后者依赖在奇异点处失效的非退化假设。本文旨在统一两者，实现在原始坐标系下、无需消解奇异点即可计算奇异模型核心属性（如RLCT）的目标。

Method: 1. 引入“退化方向（dead direction）”作为桥梁，连接奇异学习理论与信息几何；2. 利用Fisher曲率在奇异点附近的衰减率，在原始坐标下推导出KL阶数；3. 结合纤维选择规则，将该速率映射为实对数正则阈值（RLCT）的贡献；4. 将该理论应用于深度网络，通过K-FAC因子分解分析各层的激活与梯度贡献；5. 构建满足规范不变性的DDCAdam优化器。

Result: 1. 证明了KL阶数可由方向性Fisher曲率的衰减率直接计算；2. 实现了从单个检查点（checkpoint）的前向和后向传递直接获取渡边三元组（λ, m, ν）的预测；3. 推导出了适用于残差连接、层归一化和注意力机制等现代网络结构的闭式分析公式；4. 提出了DDCAdam，一种能保持梯度流中G-不变性的新型预处理算法。

Conclusion: 本文通过建立奇点学习理论与信息几何之间的桥梁，证明了奇异模型的KL阶数（即渡边三元组信息）可以直接通过原始参数空间中的方向性Fisher曲率衰减率来恢复，无需进行复杂的Hironaka分辨率解析。该框架不仅支持对现代深度网络架构进行闭式预测，还通过构建DDCAdam优化器实现了对梯度流中规范对称性的保持，为理解深度学习模型的奇异几何结构提供了高效的分析工具。

Abstract: Singular learning theory and information geometry have studied the same parameter spaces in mostly separate vocabularies: the former computes Bayesian invariants in resolved coordinates, the latter works in original coordinates under a non-degeneracy assumption that overparameterised models routinely violate. We bridge them through one primitive, the dead direction: a unit vector along which the Fisher metric degenerates, equivalently a tangent to the analytic singular set with a definite KL order, set by how fast the KL divergence vanishes. The two readings name the same vector; our central move shows its KL order is recoverable as the decay rate of the directional Fisher curvature approaching the singularity, in original parameter coordinates and without a Hironaka resolution. A selection rule on smooth fibres translates this rate into Watanabe's single-direction contribution to the real log canonical threshold, and we extend the recovery to multi-component crossings, multiplicity $m$, the singular fluctuation $ν$ (universal in the KL order for 1D directions), prior-RLCT shifts, and tempered posteriors. We then lift this rate to a deep network: a multi-layer K-FAC factorisation writes each Fisher block as a product of activation- and gradient-side rates with a duality between them, instantiated at modern-network primitives (residual streams, layer normalisation, attention). A quotient theorem carries the rate to the gauge quotient $Θ/G$ under gradient flow on a $G$-invariant metric; SGD qualifies, standard Adam does not, and we construct a $G$-equivariant Adam-family preconditioner (DDCAdam) that does. The bridge yields a parameter-coordinate handle on singular geometry, closed-form per-architecture predictions, and a trajectory-rate readout of Watanabe's triple $(λ, m, ν)$ from one checkpoint's forward and backward passes, without posterior sampling.

</details>


### [236] [Generalized TV--$\ell_p$ Structured Priors for Bayesian $T_1$ Mapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05381)
*Disi Lin,Martin Berggren,Tommy Löfstedt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合TV函数与$\ell_p$范数的贝叶斯空间先验，有效提升了$T_1$映射的空间一致性和不确定性量化精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对现有$T_1$映射方法在空间一致性保持及不确定性量化方面的不足，寻求一种更稳健的先验分布框架。

Method: 构建包含全变分（TV）函数与$\ell_p$范数的扩展结构化空间先验族，嵌入贝叶斯回归框架，并利用No-U-Turn Sampler (NUTS)进行后验推理。

Result: 在合成脑/心脏数据及真实乳腺$T_1$映射实验中表现优于最大似然估计及其他传统贝叶斯先验（如均匀、Gamma、有界TV先验），表现为更高的后验密度集中度、更低的偏差及方差。

Conclusion: 引入基于TV-$\ell_p$范数的结构化空间先验，能有效提高$T_1$映射的精度与不确定性量化能力，在保持空间一致性的同时降低了估计偏差与方差。

Abstract: We propose an extended family of structured spatial priors that incorporates the total variation (TV) function with $\ell_p$ norms. The prior is proven to be proper and incorporated into a Bayesian regression framework to enable uncertainty quantification in $T_1$ mapping, with posterior inference performed using the No-U-Turn Sampler (NUTS). This TV--$\ell_p$ construction is proven to constitute a well-defined family of prior distributions, and it naturally enforces spatial consistency and smooth variations in the estimated parameter maps. The method was evaluated in comparison to maximum-likelihood estimation and several Bayesian alternative priors based on the uniform, Gamma, and bounded TV priors. The evaluation includes experiments on synthetic brain and cardiac $T_1$ mapping datasets, as well as a real in-vivo breast $T_1$ mapping dataset. The results show that the TV--$\ell_p$ prior yields more concentrated posterior densities, indicating reduced uncertainty. It also consistently achieves lower variance and smaller (negative) bias, leading to more reliable estimates. Overall, embedding a TV-based structured penalty along with $\ell_p$ norms in a prior in a Bayesian model improves spatial coherence in $T_1$ maps and enhances uncertainty quantification, offering a robust approach for $T_1$ mapping with uncertainties.

</details>


### [237] [Adaptive state-action abstractions via rate-distortion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06123)
*Fernando E. Rosas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了强化学习中动态调整抽象粒度的一种通用原则，即当学习误差与抽象误差相当时进行精细化，通过贝尔曼残差与双模拟度量的证书机制实现，有效解决了复杂任务学习中的效率问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习缺乏通用的原则来指导智能体如何在学习过程中动态调整任务抽象的粒度（类似于婴儿学习行走时先掌握粗略方案再逐步精细化）。

Method: 提出了一种衡量价值误差的分解原则：将价值误差通过贝尔曼残差（学习误差界）和双模拟度量（抽象误差界）进行分解。基于此，采用由率失真理论构建的软状态-动作抽象，并根据分解后的误差动态调整抽象的分辨率。

Result: 在多个表格型环境测试中，该方法证明了在对状态和动作信息进行大幅度有损压缩的情况下，智能体依然能够维持近乎最优的学习表现。

Conclusion: 本研究提出了一种基于性能证书的动态抽象细化原则，验证了在学习误差与抽象误差相当时进行细化可实现高效学习，且该方法在存在状态与动作信息有损压缩的情况下仍能达到近优性能。

Abstract: When learning to walk, infants seem to address a coarse version of the problem first - stay upright, reach the caregiver - and refine it only when further practice at that resolution stops paying off. Reinforcement learning offers multiple techniques for building simple versions of complex tasks, but lacks general principles for how to dynamically adjust the granularity of these abstractions during learning. This paper proposes one such principle: refine the abstraction as soon as the learning error within it becomes comparable to the error induced by the abstraction itself. Here, we investigate one way of formalising this principle via a performance certificate that decomposes value error into two terms: a learning error bound captured by a Bellman residual, and an abstraction error bound given by a bisimulation metric. The resulting switching strategy is implemented by soft state-action abstractions built from rate-distortion principles, whose resolution along state and action axes can be continuously adjusted. We validate this construction in a range of tabular settings, showing that near-optimal performance can be achieved under substantial lossy compression of state and action information.

</details>


### [238] [Trust, but Don't Verify: Epistemic Blind Spots in LLM Source Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05403)
*Rohan N. Pradhan,Steve Goley*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究发现大语言模型在综合多源信息时，会忽视统计数据的真实性，转而过度依赖文本看起来是否“专业”的表层特征，这种“认识论对齐”偏见是模型训练与部署策略导致的缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨大语言模型在作为认知代理（epistemic proxies）进行多源信息综合时，到底是真正评估了证据的质量，还是仅仅基于表层特征（如文本风格、范式）来聚合信息。

Method: 1. 跨模型实验：测试Claude、Qwen、OLMo三个模型族系在五个模型版本中的表现。
2. 任务设计：对比单一统计数据识别与多来源信息合成任务，评估模型对虚假统计数据的处理差异。
3. 机械可解释性分析：利用因果追踪（Causal Tracing）、线性探针（Linear Probes）及组件级归因分析（Component-level Attribution），识别模型内部对“方法论描述”与“数值有效性”的处理路径。
4. 干预测试：对比提示工程策略（Prompting）与模型后训练管线对该评估偏差的影响。

Result: 1. 模型能够识别孤立的虚假统计数据（准确率0.76-1.00），但在多源合成任务中会直接无视数值有效性。
2. 模型内部存在“方法论-注册门控（methodology-register gate）”，导致模型更看重文本是否看起来“像分析报告”，而非数据逻辑是否成立。
3. 提示工程无法实现选择性辨别，反而会导致“全盘怀疑”。
4. 现有的后训练流程进一步加固了这种基于样式的捷径，而非培养数值验证能力。

Conclusion: 大语言模型存在“认识论对齐”偏差：模型虽然具备检测统计真实性的底层能力，但在多源信息综合任务中会忽略指标有效性，转而过度依赖文本的“分析范式（methodology-register）”表征。这一失效并非能力缺失，而是模型在对齐训练中形成的风格化捷径与部署策略所致。

Abstract: Language models increasingly act as epistemic proxies, synthesizing evidence from multiple sources to inform decisions. Whether they evaluate the quality of that evidence, or merely aggregate it based on surface presentation, remains poorly understood. We show that models possess the capability to detect fabricated statistics (correct identification rates of 0.76-1.00 for methodology in isolation) but do not recruit this capability during multi-source synthesis, producing similar numeric estimates whether the statistics are fabricated or valid. Specifically, source influence is governed by a methodology-register gate that responds to the distributional register of analytical text but not to numeric validity: for example, statistically impossible confidence intervals receive the same weight as valid ones. The behavioral dissociation replicates across five models from three families (Claude, Qwen, OLMo) and three professional domains. Mechanistic analyses, including causal tracing, linear probes, and component-level attribution, converge on the same account: the model encodes and causally uses a methodology-register representation that transfers across domains (probe AUC 0.83-0.92), while numeric-validity signals, decodable in isolation, are suppressed to chance during multi-source synthesis. Prompting-based mitigations, even an oracle checklist naming the exact statistical checks, produce blanket skepticism rather than selective discernment, and the post-training pipelines we examine reinforce the stylistic shortcut without building numeric verification. Unlike sycophancy, which tracks user preference, this failure tracks whether a source presents as analytically credible, not whether its claims are internally consistent. We term this epistemic alignment: like preference and safety alignment, the question is not capability but deployment.

</details>


### [239] [PAC-Bayesian Adversarially Robust Generalization for Message Passing Graph Neural Networks: A Sensitivity Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06293)
*Ziling Liang,Xinping Yi,Qingsong Wen,Shi Jin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将敏感度感知 PAC-Bayesian 框架引入 MPGNN，通过分析网络输出对权重的雅可比矩阵特性，利用各向异性高斯后验，导出了一个不再受隐藏层宽度限制、且大幅提升了理论紧凑性的鲁棒泛化边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于 PAC-Bayesian 的 GNN 鲁棒泛化分析通常假设各向同性高斯后验，忽略了参数的异质敏感性，且依赖于结构参数（如隐藏层宽度），导致所得泛化界不够紧凑。

Method: 1. 将敏感度感知 PAC-Bayesian 框架从深度神经网络扩展至消息传递 GNN (MPGNN)；
2. 计算网络输出相对于权重参数的雅可比矩阵 (Jacobian)，并利用其秩最多为 $K$ 的特性进行量化；
3. 构建由雅可比矩阵对齐的敏感度矩阵；
4. 采用各向异性高斯后验分布 (anisotropic Gaussian posteriors) 并优化协方差，以紧致地界定 KL 散度。

Result: 导出了针对对抗环境下的 MPGNN 鲁棒泛化界；证明了该界通过将复杂的隐藏宽度依赖项降低为类别数 $K$，显著提高了理论界的紧凑性，同时优化了权重谱范数的依赖关系。

Conclusion: 通过将敏感度感知 PAC-Bayesian 框架扩展至 MPGNN，并引入雅可比矩阵对齐的技术，研究成功导出了更紧凑的鲁棒泛化界，证明了该界不依赖于隐藏层宽度而是由类别数 $K$ 主导，为增强 GNN 的对抗鲁棒性提供了理论指导。

Abstract: Whilst the vulnerability of graph neural networks (GNNs) to adversarial attacks poses a critical threat to graph representation learning, the understanding of the robust generalization behavior remains a fundamental challenge in the adversarial setting. Recently, PAC-Bayesian margin-based generalization analysis substantially advances this line of research by providing a flexible and data-dependent analytical framework. However, existing robust analyses often rely on isotropic Gaussian posteriors and control weight perturbations in the full parameter space, which limits the ability to capture heterogeneous parameter sensitivity yet hinges on hidden-width-dependent complexity terms, resulting in not-tight-enough generalization bounds. In this paper, we extend a recently proposed sensitivity-aware PAC-Bayesian framework from deep neural networks to message passing GNNs (MPGNNs) and derive a tighter robust generalization bound in the adversarial setting. Specifically, we first quantify how sensitive the perturbations across different parameter blocks are to the network outputs by deriving the output Jacobians with respect to the weight parameters. Exploiting the fact that these Jacobian matrices have rank at most $K$ in $K$-class graph classification, we then construct Jacobian-aligned sensitivity matrices and use anisotropic Gaussian posteriors with optimized covariances to upper bound the KL divergence in a tight way. Notably, by refining the spectral-norm dependence on the learned weights and reducing the leading dimension factor from hidden-width-dependent terms to the number of classes $K$, our analysis yields much tighter robust generalization guarantees for MPGNNs, thereby guiding their designs to enhance adversarial robustness.

</details>


### [240] [CausalPOI: Spatio-Temporal Graph-Based Causal Modeling for Cold-Start POI Check-in Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05413)
*Zhaoqi Zhang,Miao Xie,Yi Li,Linyou Cai,Siqiang Luo,Gao Cong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了CausalPOI框架，通过时空功能交互图与因果表示学习，解决了新引入POI（冷启动）的签到预测难题，并实现了对城市干预影响的可解释性分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 1. 现有模型过度依赖邻近关系与相关性建模，忽视了POI的功能依赖性；2. 无法有效处理“冷启动”POI（新引入POI）的未来签到预测问题；3. 缺乏捕捉城市干预带来的因果效应的能力。

Method: 1. 构建“时空功能交互图”（STFIG）来建模POI间的语义与空间关系；2. 通过构建结构对齐的“处理组”与“对照组”图，利用因果表示学习模拟真实场景与反事实场景，以捕捉城市干预的因果效应。

Result: 在SafeGraph真实数据集上的实验表明，CausalPOI在时空预测、语义交互建模和因果效应估计任务中均显著优于现有主流基准模型。

Conclusion: CausalPOI通过引入因果推断框架，有效提升了冷启动POI签到预测的准确性和可解释性，并为城市干预分析提供了可靠的决策依据。

Abstract: As urban environments continue to evolve rapidly, accurately modeling the dynamic behaviour of Points of Interest is essential for supporting data-driven urban planning and commercial decision-making. While recent advancements in spatio-temporal graph learning have improved POI forecasting, most methods rely on proximity-based graphs and correlation-driven modeling, which overlook the functional dependencies between POIs and fail to capture the causal effects of urban interventions. In this paper, we introduce a novel research problem -- cold-start POI check-in forecasting, which aims to predict the future check-in pattern of a newly introduced POI, by modeling its temporal evolution and functional interactions with nearby POIs in a structured urban spatial context. To address these challenges, we propose CausalPOI, a spatio-temporal graph-based causal representation learning framework. CausalPOI leverages Spatio-Temporal Functional Interaction Graph to model semantic and spatial relationships between POIs, and constructs structurally aligned treatment and control graphs to simulate factual and counterfactual scenarios. Extensive experiments on real-world SafeGraph datasets demonstrate that CausalPOI significantly outperforms state-of-the-art baselines across the board, validating its effectiveness in spatio-temporal forecasting, semantic interaction modeling, and causal effect estimation, providing a more interpretable and actionable foundation for urban intervention analysis.
