To tackle this, we focus on optimising corner kick routines, and formulate a decision-making problem in which a central policy makes adjustments to attacking player positions and velocities to maximise first contact shot probability. Unlike classic optimisation that solves for isolated setups, we contribute a reinforcement learning architecture operating on graph-structured data that yields a general policy for adjusting arbitrary starting player positions. Evaluated on over 3,000 Premier League corners, our approach strongly outperforms baseline optimisation techniques under matched inference budgets. Our results suggest that graph reinforcement learning can shift set-piece analysis from historical evaluation and imitation towards reward-driven tactical discovery.

</details>


### [316] [Learned Response-Field Inertia Operator for HEC-RAS 2D Water-Surface Elevation Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06385)
*Edward Holmberg,Elias Ioup,Md Meftahul Ferdaus,Mahdi Abdelguerfi,Julian Simeonov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了LRFIO模型，这是一种能够在非结构化网格上直接进行水面高程快速预测的代理框架；通过按需调整模型复杂度，在保持HEC-RAS 2D求解器一致性的同时，实现了数量级的计算加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统水面高程（WSE）预测模型常受限于栅格重采样带来的误差以及对输入信息的混淆，且计算效率低下。研究旨在通过在HEC-RAS 2D的原生计算单元上实现求解器一致性的快速代理预测，以在保证精度的同时大幅提升计算效率。

Method: 提出了“学习响应域惯性算子（LRFIO）”。该方法在原始非结构化二维计算网格上直接运行，通过增量学习而非强制施加物理约束，构建了一个包含基准持久性、全局惯性和分段响应域惯性的层次化响应模型，并采用选择器机制仅在必要时引入神经化或空间残差纠正等复杂项。

Result: LRFIO在四个多样化的HEC-RAS 2D基准测试中表现出良好的泛化性与适应性，最大验证后悔值仅为4.30%。在大坝溃决等场景的实际部署中，实现了高达2.75×10⁴倍的加速比，且测试耗时极短（最低达0.003秒）。

Conclusion: 通过在HEC-RAS 2D计算网格上直接构建代理模型，证明了“原生单元（native-cell）”增量预测法的有效性。LRFIO框架强调了根据经验需求动态调整模型复杂度的必要性，在保证预测精度的前提下，实现了相较于传统求解器数万倍的加速比。

Abstract: This article presents a cross-dataset evaluation of learned native-cell surrogate models for solver-consistent water-surface elevation (WSE) prediction in HEC-RAS 2D. To avoid raster remapping error and information-access confounding, surrogates are evaluated directly on the original nonuniform computational cells under an explicit policy that separates static project inputs, current hydraulic state, project-input forcing, calibration-derived quantities, and future solver-output targets. We introduce the Learned Response-Field Inertia Operator (LRFIO), a no-forcing, increment-based learned surrogate that calibrates an inertial response operator from solved HEC-RAS trajectories and deploys the retained operator through closed-form native-cell rollout. LRFIO evaluates a base-case-first response hierarchy consisting of persistence, global calibrated inertia, and segmented response-field inertia. Segmentation, residual correction, and neuralized inertia are treated as learnable modeling choices, with added complexity retained only when validation evidence justifies its cost. Evaluated across four diverse HEC-RAS 2D benchmarks, LRFIO retains different response structures for different domains, demonstrating adaptive learned complexity. The selector audit shows controlled complexity with a maximum validation regret of 4.30%. During deployment, retained rollout times range from 0.003 s to 0.242 s, and the Beaver Bayou measured-solve comparison gives an estimated 2.75 x 10^4 horizon-normalized speedup over HEC-RAS. These results indicate that the current native-cell increment is a strong solver-conditioned predictive scaffold and that added response-field, neural, or spatial complexity should be retained only when empirically justified.

</details>


### [317] [Proper Scoring Rules for Right-Censored Survival Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06393)
*Jef Jonkers,Glenn Van Wallendael,Luc Duchateau,Sofie Van Hoecke*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对右删失生存预报的严谨评分框架，通过映射预测分布至观测数据空间，解决了经典评分规则无法直接应用的问题，并提升了生存分析模型的评估与训练性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的严谨评分规则在右删失（Right-censoring）生存数据环境下失效，需要一种能在部分观测数据下保持统计有效性的评估和训练框架。

Method: 提出了一种映射框架：将预测分布通过删失机制进行映射，进而在诱导的观测数据分布上应用经典且严谨的评分规则（Proper Scoring Rules）。该框架涵盖了从固定删失到随机/部分观测删失的多种场景，并导出了CRPS、分位数损失、Brier得分等的右删失版本。

Result: 在实验中，新方法能正确对预报模型进行排序，避免了现有插件式加权方法（Plug-in weighted scores）中常见的排序倒置问题。所提出的“删失内回归”（Censored engression）在多变量生存建模中的表现显著优于传统的朴素训练方法。

Conclusion: 提供了一种通用且严谨的、针对右删失数据的概率预报评分框架，证明了该方法在特定条件下的严谨合规性，并为多变量生存分析提供了更优的训练目标。

Abstract: Proper scoring rules provide a rigorous theoretical basis for the training and evaluation of probabilistic forecasts. However, in the presence of right censoring, the event time is only partially observed, rendering conventional scoring rules inapplicable in their standard form. We propose a framework for proper scoring of right-censored survival outcomes based on a simple idea: first, map the predictive distribution through the censoring mechanism, then apply the underlying proper score on the induced observed-data law. This yields localized scores for fixed censoring times and marginalized scores when the censoring time is random or only partially observed. The resulting construction recovers familiar right-censored likelihood and IPCW-type criteria within a coherent framework, while also yielding right-censored versions of the CRPS, pinball loss, Brier score, and energy score. We show that the marginalized score is proper under conditional independent censoring and strictly proper on the identifiable region. The same principle also leads to censored engression, a sample-based learning objective for multivariate right-censored survival modeling. In experiments, our scores correctly rank the oracle forecast across several censoring regimes, whereas forecast-dependent plug-in weighted scores can exhibit ranking reversals. Censored engression likewise substantially improves over naive training on censored outcomes.

</details>


### [318] [The Post-GCN Decade Revisited: Curvature-Stratified Evaluation of Relational Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06397)
*Shuo Wang,Xiangyu Wang,Quanxin Wang,Bailin Wu,Bokui Wang,Shunyang Huang,Boyan Deng,Haonan Liu,Ruiyi Fang,Zhenxiang Xu,Boyu Wang,Zhao Kang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文揭示了关系学习中模型性能与数据集几何属性（曲率）的强相关性，批评了传统的聚合评价指标，并推出了CurvBench基准，强调跨几何结构的差异化评估对模型选择的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关系学习的评估方式过度依赖平铺（flat）排行榜并对数据集进行平均，这由于忽视了底层几何结构的异质性，导致严重的系统性偏差，掩盖了模型在不同几何属性下的真实泛化能力。

Method: 提出了一个“曲率分层评估框架”（Curvature-stratified evaluation framework），将数据集按几何属性（正、负、零曲率）进行分区，以揭示不同几何下的性能权衡。并在14个数据集上对18个代表性模型进行了基准测试。

Result: 实验表明，模型排名在特定曲率区内非常稳定，但跨区时发生显著变化，且发现图基础模型（GFMs）在特定几何条件下相较于几何对齐的GNNs表现出收益递减现象。

Conclusion: 模型性能具有本质上的几何依赖性，而非普适通用。因此，应摒弃传统的聚合式评价，转而采用考虑几何特性的评估协议，以获得更可靠和可解释的结果。

Abstract: Current evaluation practices in relational learning rely heavily on flat leaderboards that average performance across heterogeneous datasets, implicitly assuming a uniform underlying structure. We show that this assumption introduces systematic bias: it obscures geometry-dependent performance variations and can lead to misleading conclusions about model generalization. In this work, we identify intrinsic geometry as a key latent factor governing model effectiveness. We demonstrate that conventional aggregated metrics mask critical performance trade-offs that only become visible when datasets are stratified by their geometric properties. To address this issue, we introduce a curvature-stratified evaluation framework that partitions datasets into positive, negative, and near-zero curvature regimes. Our benchmark evaluates 18 representative models including Graph Convolutional Networks (GCNs), Graph Foundation Models (GFMs), and tabular learning methods across 14 datasets. We find that model rankings are highly stable within each curvature regime but shift significantly across regimes, indicating that performance is fundamentally geometry-dependent rather than universally transferable. Notably, we identify regimes where GFMs offer diminishing returns compared to geometry-aligned GNNs. Based on these findings, we propose a geometry-aware evaluation protocol that yields more reliable and interpretable comparisons than standard aggregated benchmarks. We release all code, curvature-stratified dataset splits, and evaluation tools to support reproducible and rigorous assessment of future relational learning methods. Code and datasets are provided in our project homepage: https://sirbabbage.github.io/CurvBench_HOME/.

</details>


### [319] [Double Preconditioning (DoPr): Optimization for Test-Time Performance, not Validation Loss](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06418)
*Thomas T. Zhang,Alok Shah,Yifei Zhang,Vincent Zhang,Nikolai Matni,Max Simchowitz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为DoPr的双预处理优化范式，通过结合梯度级与激活级预处理，有效缓解了 autoregressive 等模型中因误差累积导致的“测试时反馈”问题，并揭示了传统验证集损失与下游性能脱节的现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习模型通常采用一步预测损失训练，但在部署时却采取自回归或展开式预测，导致“测试时反馈”（TTF）现象，即训练指标与下游任务表现（如生成质量、任务成功率）之间存在不一致，且误差随序列长度增加而累积。现有研究多关注数据或架构改进，缺乏从优化角度的干预。

Method: 引入双预处理（Double-preconditioning, DoPr）机制，将梯度级预处理（如Adam、Muon）与激活级预处理（如KFAC）相结合，专门针对误差累积问题优化网络训练过程。

Result: 在多种TTF任务中，应用DoPr方法能够显著提升模型的下游任务性能。值得注意的是，这种性能提升并不总是伴随着验证集损失的下降。

Conclusion: 本文提出了双预处理（DoPr）方法，证明了优化策略是解决测试时反馈（TTF）影响的关键维度。此外，实验结果表明，在TTF场景下，验证集损失函数并不总是作为下游任务性能的可靠指标，强调了评估范式优化的必要性。

Abstract: Many modern applications of deep learning involve training a neural network via a one-step prediction loss (e.g., $L^2$ regression, cross-entropy), but deploy the network by rolling out along its own predictions. Key examples include autoregressive language modeling, flow-based generative modeling, and robot policy learning. It is well-documented that these settings induce a phenomenon we call test-time feedback (TTF): the mismatch between the training/validation loss and downstream metrics of interest, such as task success rate and generation quality, which grows with task length. While data curation, architecture, and objective design have been proposed to combat train-test shift in TTF settings, this paper proposes optimization as a new design axis to mitigate error accumulation. Specifically, we introduce a new optimization paradigm called double-preconditioning (DoPr) uniquely tailored to the challenges of TTF. DoPr combines gradient-wise preconditioning, as in Adam and Muon, with activation-wise preconditioning (AP), such as in KFAC. We show that the addition of AP yields a drop-in intervention for increasing downstream model performance across a range of TTF settings. Interestingly, these gains in test-time performance do not consistently accompany improvements in validation loss, opening new questions about how to properly evaluate models trained with one-step supervised objectives.

</details>


### [320] [In-Context Multiple Instance Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06458)
*Alexander Möllers,Marvin Sextro,Julius Hense,Gabriel Dernbach,Klaus-Robert Müller*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种针对多示例学习（MIL）的预训练上下文学习方法，通过Perceiver架构在合成数据上进行训练，实现了在极少量标注样本下无需微调即可完成任务，提升了MIL在低数据环境下的鲁棒性与泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多示例学习（MIL）算法在数据标注稀缺（低标签）场景下表现不佳：复杂模型容易过拟合，而简单模型则难以适应多样化的任务需求。

Method: 提出一种基于Perceiver架构的上下文学习（In-context Learning）方法。通过在大规模合成的包结构数据（Bag-structured data）上进行预训练，使模型具备在新任务中仅通过极少量标注包即可直接进行推理的能力，无需额外的梯度更新。

Result: 在十二个多示例学习基准测试中，该模型表现出最优的平均性能，证明了跨任务的泛化能力和在低资源环境下的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过在多种合成数据生成器上进行预训练，Perceiver架构的上下文学习器能够获得强大的归纳偏置，在少样本MIL任务中实现无需微调的高效推理，且性能优于需要特定任务训练的基线模型。

Abstract: Multiple Instance Learning (MIL) addresses problems where supervision is available at the level of bags of instances and has been successfully applied in fields ranging from computational pathology to satellite imagery. Nevertheless, existing algorithms struggle in the low-label regime that characterizes many real-world applications. Flexible models overfit and rigid ones fail to adapt to the task at hand. We show that pretraining an in-context learner with a Perceiver-style architecture on synthetic data yields a model that can solve new tasks from a handful of labeled bags. At inference time, classification happens in a single forward pass and requires no gradient updates. We propose and investigate different synthetic data generators for bag-structured data and find that they capture complementary inductive biases. A model pretrained on a mixture of these generators inherits their per-task strengths and achieves the best average performance across twelve MIL benchmarks, outperforming supervised baselines that require task-specific training.

</details>


### [321] [Event Detection for Parameter-to-KPI Dependency Learning for AI-RAN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06459)
*Christie Djidjev,Nicholas Kaminski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于显著性检测的方法，通过将AI-RAN中复杂的连续遥测数据转化为二进制事件，有效识别并恢复参数与网络性能间的隐含依赖关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 下一代无线网络（如AI-RAN/O-RAN）中多个AI控制功能并发导致交互干扰，缺乏一种可靠且可解释的依赖结构来明确参数与KPI之间的影响关系，难以从原始噪声数据中有效检测出真实的控制互动。

Method: 1. 构建了一个包含预植入隐式依赖关系的合成闭环流量生成器；2. 将“将连续遥测数据转化为二进制事件指标”的问题建模为显著性检测问题；3. 开发了一套基于机器学习的依赖关系恢复流水线，并研究了阈值校准对检测质量的影响。

Result: 实验表明，该流水线能够从噪声连续轨迹中成功恢复出隐含的依赖结构，证明了其在处理复杂AI-RAN控制系统交互方面的可行性，同时强调了阈值选取对事件检测准确性的决定性作用。

Conclusion: 本文提出了一种在AI-RAN/O-RAN架构中检测控制参数与网络KPI之间依赖关系的方法。通过将连续数据转化为二进制事件指标，实验证明在信号与噪声分离度高的情况下，该方法能可靠恢复隐含依赖结构，并指出阈值校准是系统性能的核心因素。

Abstract: Next-generation wireless networks are expected to rely on multiple concurrent AI-driven control functions that optimize different network objectives simultaneously, particularly in AI-integrated and open radio access network architectures such as AI Radio Access Network (AI-RAN) and Open Radio Access Network (O-RAN). When these functions interact, they can interfere with one another in ways that are difficult to detect from raw network data alone. A key missing piece for managing such interactions is a reliable, interpretable dependency structure that captures which control parameters are actively influencing which network performance outcomes at any given time. This paper focuses on the event-detection step needed to support such dependency learning by converting noisy continuous telemetry into binary indicators of parameter activity and KPI response. The central difficulty is that not every fluctuation in the data reflects a genuine control interaction, so the method must distinguish real parameter-outcome relationships from background variation. Because real AI-RAN traffic traces with known parameter-KPI ground truth are difficult to obtain, we introduce a synthetic closed-loop traffic generator with planted latent dependencies. We use this controlled telemetry to evaluate a machine-learning-based dependency recovery pipeline that formulates the conversion of continuous traces into binary event indicators as a significance-detection problem. Experimental evaluation shows that the proposed pipeline reliably recovers the latent dependency structure from noisy continuous traces when the signal is sufficiently separated from background variation, while highlighting threshold calibration as the key factor controlling event-detection quality. These results constitute a foundational step toward interpretable dependency learning for adaptive AI-RAN control systems.

</details>


### [322] [PC Layer: Polynomial Weight Preconditioning for Improving LLM Pre-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06470)
*Senmiao Wang,Tiantian Fang,Haoran Zhang,Yushun Zhang,Kunxiang Zhao,Alex Schwing,Ruoyu Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种“PC层”多项式预条件技术，通过重塑权重奇异值谱提升LLM训练稳定性，且推理阶段零开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决大语言模型（LLM）训练过程中权重矩阵条件数不佳、训练不稳定的问题。

Method: 提出一种预条件（PC）层，即通过低阶多项式对权重矩阵进行参数化，以重塑其奇异值谱。在训练结束后，该参数化结构可合并回原权重矩阵。

Result: 在Llama-1B预训练任务中，验证了该方法在使用AdamW和Muon优化器时均优于标准Transformer，并从理论上证明了限制奇异值范围可保证深度线性网络梯度下降的几何收敛性。

Conclusion: 通过多项式预条件处理能够有效控制权重矩阵的奇异值分布，从而提升训练稳定性和收敛性能，且预处理层在推理阶段可无损拆解，不增加额外计算开销。

Abstract: We propose a preconditioning (PC) layer, a weight parameterization via polynomial preconditioner that ensures stable weight conditioning throughout LLM training. The PC module reshapes the singular-value spectrum of weight matrices via low-degree polynomial preconditioning. After training, the preconditioned weights can be merged back into the original architecture, incurring no inference overhead. We demonstrate the advantage of the proposed PC layer over standard transformers in Llama-1B pre-training, for both the AdamW and Muon optimizers. Theoretically, we justify this spectrum-control principle by proving that uniformly bounding each layer's singular values ensures geometric convergence of gradient descent to global minima, for certain deep linear networks. Our code is available at https://github.com/Empath-aln/PC-layer.

</details>


### [323] [RREDCoT: Segment-Level Reward Redistribution for Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06475)
*Mykyta Ielanskyi,Kajetan Schweighofer,Lukas Aichberger,Sepp Hochreiter*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RREDCoT 通过模型自我估值实现高效的思维链（CoT）奖励重分配，降低了强化学习中的训练方差，解决了长上下文推理中的信用分配难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的推理模型强化学习（如 GRPO）通常面临延迟奖励带来的高方差问题，且传统的蒙特卡洛（MC）信用分配方法计算开销过大，难以在长上下文语境中实现高粒度的训练时信用分配。

Method: 提出 RREDCoT (Reward REDistribution for Chain of Thoughts) 方法，利用模型自身来近似最优的奖励重分配，无需额外的采样生成，同时探讨了 CoT 轨迹分段与状态价值估计等关键技术细节。

Result: 研究表明 RREDCoT 在处理奖励重分配方面优于传统的 MC 采样法及多种属性归因方法，能够有效优化 CoT 轨迹的学习过程。

Conclusion: RREDCoT 通过利用模型自身能力进行高效的奖励重分配，有效地解决了推理大模型在 CoT 任务中面临的延迟奖励及高方差问题，为在大规模训练中进行精细化信用分配提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Recent advancements in reasoning language models have been driven by Reinforcement Learning (RL) fine-tuning. Most often, these rely on the Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) algorithm or modifications thereof to steer the models to produce Chain-of-Thought (CoT) traces. The final answer can only be verified, and the reward assigned, after the CoT trace is complete, making it a delayed reward problem. GRPO and its modifications correspond to Monte Carlo methods in standard RL, which are known to suffer from high variance. A possible solution to this problem is the redistribution of rewards through credit assignment, where segments of the CoT trace that are important for arriving at the desirable solution are emphasized by assigning a higher reward. While Monte Carlo sampling can be used to provide an unbiased estimate of intermediate state values, its computational overhead makes it unsuitable for train-time credit assignment in long contexts at high granularity. We introduce RREDCoT (Reward REDistribution for Chain of Thoughts), which utilizes the model itself to approximate the optimal reward redistribution without additional generation. We investigate the advantages of our method compared to MC sampling and several attribution methods. We further analyze several aspects relevant to the construction of the redistribution such as segmentation of CoT traces and state value estimation.

</details>


### [324] [Pretraining Recurrent Networks without Recurrence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06479)
*Akarsh Kumar,Phillip Isola*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SMT方法，将RNN训练解构为监督学习任务，通过Transformer获取记忆标签，不仅解决了RNN训练无法并行的问题，还突破了BPTT在捕获长距离依赖上的限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统通过时间反向传播（BPTT）训练RNN存在梯度消失/爆炸、难以捕获长距离依赖，且由于时序依赖导致无法并行化的问题。

Method: 提出监督记忆训练（SMT）方法，将RNN训练简化为一步记忆转移的监督学习任务。利用Transformer编码器通过预测状态目标（Predictive State Objective）获取记忆标签，通过固定长度（O(1)）梯度路径实现并行化训练。

Result: 在语言建模和像素序列建模任务中，SMT在预训练RNN架构时表现优于BPTT，并实现了时间并行化和稳定的恒定长度梯度路径。

Conclusion: SMT通过解耦记忆更新与记忆内容，成功绕过了BPTT的时序依赖，实现了RNN的时间并行训练。在语言建模和像素序列建模任务中，SMT比BPTT更能有效捕获长距离依赖，且具备更好的扩展性。

Abstract: Training recurrent neural networks (RNNs) requires assigning credit across long sequences of computations. Standard backpropagation through time (BPTT) addresses this problem poorly: it is sequential in time, limiting parallelism, and suffers from vanishing or exploding gradients, making long-range associations difficult to learn. We propose Supervised Memory Training (SMT), a method for training nonlinear RNNs that sidesteps recurrent credit propagation entirely by reducing RNN training to supervised learning on one-step memory transition labels $(m_t, x_{t+1}) \rightarrow m_{t+1}$. SMT acquires these memory labels by training a Transformer-based encoder on a predictive state objective--retaining only information from the past necessary to predict the future. By decoupling what to remember from how to update memory, SMT enables time-parallel RNN training with a stable $O(1)$ length gradient path between any two tokens--without ever unrolling the RNN. We find that SMT outperforms BPTT when pretraining various RNN architectures on tasks like language modeling and pixel sequence modeling. SMT enables nonlinear RNNs to better capture long-range dependencies and train in parallel, potentially unlocking the scaling of models that build temporal abstractions of past experience.

</details>


### [325] [Regret Minimization with Adaptive Opponents in Repeated Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06486)
*Mingyang Liu,Asuman Ozdaglar,Tiancheng Yu,Kaiqing Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对传统遗憾度量无法应对自适应对手的问题，本文提出了 RP-Regret，并给出了相应的理论条件与三类优化算法，证明其能有效学习博弈均衡并提升在线学习中的合作水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准的外部遗憾（External Regret）无法有效衡量博弈中能够根据历史博弈进行反应的“自适应对手”，因此需要一种新的博弈论衡量指标，以应对对手的对策性反应。

Method: 1. 定义了 RP-Regret（重复策略遗憾）作为衡量指标；2. 分析了实现次线性 RP-Regret 所需的必要条件（针对策略变分和记忆限制）；3. 提出了三种针对 RP-Regret（非凸）的算法：基于优化预言机的算法、基于线性化凸代理的算法、以及针对慢变对手的算法。

Result: 证明了在特定条件下可以实现次线性 RP-Regret，并展示了这些算法在 Stag-Hunt 等博弈中能促成更具合作性的解。

Conclusion: RP-Regret 能够有效应对自适应对手，其最小化过程有助于学习博弈中的子博弈完美均衡（SPE），并在协同博弈中实现更高的效用。

Abstract: In this paper, we study regret minimization in repeated games with \emph{adaptive} opponents who can respond based on histories of play. The standard metric of \emph{external regret} in online learning is known to fail to capture such adaptivity. To account for players' counterfactual reasoning, we introduce {\tt Repeated Policy Regret (RP-Regret)}, a game-theoretic metric that measures the difference between the \emph{realized} and the \emph{best-in-hindsight} accumulated utility when all players can \emph{respond} to the history of play. Compared to existing regret notions in this setting, ours is native to repeated game playing, enabling stronger comparators and opponents with fewer constraints, while maintaining the possibility of finding better equilibria when all players minimize it. We first identify necessary conditions for obtaining {\tt RP-Regret} sublinear in time, on the variation of the player's comparator strategies in the regret definition and on the memories of both the comparator and opponents' strategies. We then study additional conditions and provable algorithms to minimize {\tt RP-Regret}, which is by definition \emph{non-convex} in the strategy space. To address this challenge, we propose three algorithms: (i) one based on an optimization oracle, as assumed in some prior work in online non-convex learning; (ii) one that minimizes a convex and \emph{linearized} surrogate of {\tt RP-Regret} at each iteration; (iii) one that directly minimizes {\tt RP-Regret} when opponents change strategies slowly. Furthermore, when all players can run algorithms to minimize the {\tt RP-Regret} (or its linearized variant), certain subgame perfect equilibria of the repeated game can be learned. We also provide experiments showing that minimizing our regret notions can lead to more cooperative solutions with higher utility in games such as Stag-Hunt.

</details>


### [326] [TailLoR: Protecting Principal Components in Parameter-Efficient Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06494)
*Marius Dragoi,Ioana Pintilie,Alexandra Dragomir,Antonio Barbalau,Florin Brad*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TailLoR是一种基于谱分解的持续学习微调方法，通过固定奇异向量基并利用软谱惩罚引导模型在长尾谱坐标上进行细粒度更新，从而减轻任务间的干扰。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了解决持续学习（Continual Learning）中参数高效微调（PEFT）面临的性能权衡问题，特别是如何在维持预训练知识的同时实现高效的知识更新，克服传统低秩更新导致的干扰。

Method: 提出TailLoR方法，将预训练权重的奇异向量矩阵U和V作为固定参考系，仅对奇异值矩阵进行低秩更新；同时设计“软谱惩罚”（soft spectral penalty）机制，通过惩罚主奇异方向的更新，将模型调整导向长尾（高频/细粒度）谱坐标。

Result: TailLoR方法能够有效降低任务间的干扰，在保持模型参数高效性的同时，提升了模型在持续学习任务上的适应能力。

Conclusion: TailLoR通过在奇异值矩阵上应用低秩更新，并引入软谱惩罚机制，有效缓解了持续学习中的灾难性遗忘，为参数高效微调提供了新的视角，即利用长尾谱坐标进行细粒度适配。

Abstract: Parameter-efficient finetuning methods based on spectral decomposition have enabled progress in Continual Learning. In this paper we introduce TailLoR, which utilizes the singular bases U and V of the pre-trained weights as a fixed reference frame to learn a low-rank update applied to the singular value matrix. A soft spectral penalty discourages updates aligned with dominant singular directions, reducing interference while routing fine-grained adaptation into the highly flexible, long-tail spectral coordinates.

</details>


<div id='cs.DB'></div>

# cs.DB [[Back]](#toc)

### [327] [Data Flow Control: Data Safety Policies for AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05679)
*Charlie Summers,Eugene Wu*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出了“数据流控制（DFC）”框架，通过Passant查询改写层，在无需物化数据来源的情况下，高效地在数据库底层强制执行数据安全合规策略，解决了AI代理生成SQL带来的私密与合规风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前的AI代理生成的SQL在语义上可能是正确的，但在隐私、合规和业务逻辑上可能是违规的。现有的安全机制往往处于应用层，缺乏底层数据库对数据流的直接控制。

Method: 提出了DFC框架，通过声明式语言定义元组级别的数据流策略；设计了Passant查询改写层，利用聚合谓词在来源单项式（provenance monomials）上进行计算，避免了繁重的物化操作。

Result: 在DuckDB、Umbra、PostgreSQL、DataFusion和SQLServer五种主流数据库引擎上测试，Passant实现了接近0%的性能开销，且效率比现有替代方案高出几个数量级。

Conclusion: 本文提出的“数据流控制（DFC）”是将数据安全从提示词工程和事后检查层面提升至数据基础设施层面的关键一步，证明了策略执行无需牺牲数据库性能。

Abstract: Agents increasingly generate SQL, orchestrate pipelines, and automate data analysis on behalf of users. While recent work improves query correctness, correctness is not safety. A query may be semantically valid yet violate regulatory, privacy, or business constraints that govern how data may be combined and released. We argue that enforcing such constraints is fundamentally a data infrastructure problem.
  This paper introduces Data Flow Control (DFC), a framework to declaratively specify and guarantee policy enforcement over tuple-level data flows within a DBMS query. A key challenge is defining a policy language that is optimizer-invariant yet efficient to enforce at scale. We formalize data safety as aggregate predicates over provenance monomials and present Passant, a portable query rewriting layer that enforces DFC policies without materializing provenance. Across five DBMS engines -- DuckDB, Umbra, PostgreSQL, DataFusion, and SQLServer -- Passant achieves ~0% overhead and outperforms alternatives by orders of magnitude. As a result, Data Flow Control is the first step towards moving data safety from prompts and post-hoc checks into the data infrastructure. Data Flow Control is available open source at https://github.com/dataflowcontrol/data-flow-control.

</details>


### [328] [Validation of graph databases against PG-Schema](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06127)
*Jacek Ciszewski,Jakub Kłos,Maxime Jakubowski,Dominik Tomaszuk,Filip Murlak*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文研究了无完整性约束下评估PG-Schema图型的复杂度：一般情况下为NP完全，通过限制特定语法结构可降至多项式时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了明确在缺乏完整性约束的条件下，图数据库实例遵循PG-Schema图型定义进行验证的计算难度，并探究降低该判定问题复杂度的关键结构因素。

Method: 通过计算复杂性分析（Computational Complexity Analysis），对验证问题的联合复杂度（Combined Complexity）和数据复杂度（Data Complexity）进行定性评估，并探讨了限制结构对复杂度的影响。

Result: 验证问题在一般情况下的联合复杂度为NP完全，但在限制了类型组合与并集的交替后，可以实现多项式时间求解。

Conclusion: 非完整性约束下的图数据库实例与PG-Schema图型验证问题，其联合复杂度为NP完全，数据复杂度为多项式时间（PTIME）。若适当限制类型组合与并集之间的交替，其联合复杂度可降至PTIME。

Abstract: The problem of validating a given graph database instance against a given PG-Schema graph type without integrity constraints is NP- complete in terms of combined complexity and in PTIME in terms of data complexity. The combined complexity drops to PTIME when the alternation between type combinations and unions is suitably restricted

</details>


### [329] [TOKI: A Bitemporal Operator Algebra for Contradiction Resolution in LLM-Agent Persistent Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06240)
*Ziming Wang*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 针对 LLM 代理长期记忆中冲突解决缺乏规范的问题，TOKI 提出了一种双时态写时并发控制模型，通过类型化算子和审计行架构，在保持 LLM 判决能力的同时，消除了现存系统常见的重放不一致和数据漂移等异常。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 代理的持久化存储系统（写密集型）在处理信念更新和冲突时，缺乏明确的写时并发控制契约，导致在处理冲突时容易出现各种写时异常且不可审计。

Method: 1. 将冲突解决视为“写时并发控制”，并建立 Bitemporal 操作符族。
2. 提出双行模式架构，利用审计行（audit row）保留冲突数据，保证可追溯性。
3. 证明了四项可靠性定理，涵盖了隔离度、模式和来源。
4. 引入“紧致性伴随证明”（tightness companion）指出键控日志记录的必要性。
5. 通过 verdict 矩阵对比现有系统，评估写时异常（重放不一致、信念漂移偏差、审计擦除）。

Result: 1. 表明现有的基准系统（Baseline）在保留 LLM 裁判时均存在写时异常。
2. TOKI 是唯一能同时避免上述三种写时异常且保持 LLM 裁判的系统。
3. 在 LoCoMo 工作负载实验中，TOKI 的审计行防御机制提升了性能，且消融实验证明其类型化存储层对保持准确性至关重要。

Conclusion: TOKI 通过引入双时态算子、明确隔离前提和来源标注，形式化了 LLM 代理在持久化存储中的冲突解决契约，证明了该方法在处理写时并发异常时的优越性，为生产系统提供了更严格的正确性标准。

Abstract: Persistent memory for an LLM agent is a write-heavy substrate: every belief update is a versioned write, and a new claim may contradict a stored one. Production systems use four resolution heuristics (last-writer-wins, evidence-weighted merge, await-confirmation, per-rule policy), yet none declares the isolation level it assumes or the write-time anomalies it admits. We show that contradiction resolution is write-time concurrency control and make the missing contract explicit. TOKI types the four heuristics as one family of bitemporal operators over a dual-row schema, each with an isolation precondition and a provenance annotation that preserves the losing fact in an audit row. Four soundness theorems close the contract across isolation, schema, and provenance, lift the guarantees to operator pipelines, and extend the fold operators to n-ary conflict sets. A tightness companion proves that, within the relational schedule model, keyed logging of the adjudicating judge is necessary for replay consistency, which every audited baseline omits. A verdict matrix over eight systems localizes the gap: every baseline that keeps a language-model judge on the write path admits at least one of three write-time anomalies (replay inconsistency, belief-drift skew, audit erasure); a content-addressed engine-layer comparator avoids them only by removing the judge, and TOKI alone excludes all three while keeping it. On its one natural-workload slice the audit-row defence moves LoCoMo by 0.86, and ablating the typed memory layer removes 0.49 accuracy on 1,444 answerable LoCoMo questions; the cross-system comparison stays underpowered and claims no superiority. The contribution is the contract: a write-time correctness specification, proved sound across isolation, schema, and provenance, pinning the guarantee every production heuristic assumes but no deployed system makes explicit.

</details>


<div id='cs.DS'></div>

# cs.DS [[Back]](#toc)

### [330] [Exponential Quantum Space Advantage for Approximating Max-$k$SAT in the Streaming Setting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05366)
*Haoyu Wang,Guangxu Yang*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文提出了Max-kSAT和Max-2OR的量子流式算法，在polylog(n)空间下取得了超过经典限制的近似比，证明了量子计算在流式CSP问题下的指数级空间优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有经典算法在Max-kSAT问题上存在空间复杂度的下界限制（如需优于0.7071的近似比，经典流式算法需要Ω(sqrt(n))空间），研究旨在探索在流式设置下，量子计算是否能提供指数级的空间优势。

Method: 设计了两种单遍量子流式算法（One-pass quantum streaming algorithms）：一种用于Max-kSAT（达到0.7172近似），另一种用于Max-2OR（达到0.7425近似），均仅需polylog(n)空间。

Result: 在Max-kSAT上实现了0.7172的近似比（优于经典的0.7071阈值），且空间复杂度仅为polylog(n)，成功证实了量子指数优势。同时给出了Max-2OR的高效量子流式算法，并完成了该问题类别的完整分类。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了量子流式算法在Max-kSAT及Max-2OR问题上相比经典算法具有指数级的空间优势，并完成了对所有布尔Max-2CSPs量子空间优势的分类。

Abstract: In this paper, we give a one-pass quantum streaming algorithm for Max-$k$SAT that uses $\operatorname{polylog}(n)$ space and achieves a $0.7172$-approximation on instances with $n$ variables. In contrast, prior work by Chou, Golovnev, and Velusamy (FOCS 2020) implies that achieving an approximation ratio better than $\sqrt{2}/2 \approx 0.7071$ for Max-$k$SAT requires $Ω(\sqrt{n})$ space for any classical streaming algorithm. Therefore, it yields an exponential quantum space advantage for Max-$k$SAT in the streaming setting.
  We further give a one-pass quantum streaming algorithm for Max-2OR that uses $\operatorname{polylog}(n)$ space and achieves a $0.7425$-approximation on instances with $n$ variables. Combining with the known results, it gives a complete classification of quantum space advantages for all Boolean Max-2CSPs.

</details>


### [331] [Learning-Augmented Online Minimization with Dual Predictions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05380)
*Christian Coester,Alexa Tudose,Alexander Turoczy*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用对偶线性规划预测的在线最小化算法，通过利用对偶解的高稳定性，实现了优于传统算法的性能，成功应用于度量任务系统和层级集合覆盖问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的学习增强在线算法多依赖于原始解的预测，但原始解在实例扰动下极不稳定，难以学习。研究旨在探索利用更稳定的对偶问题解（Dual Solutions）来提升在线最小化算法的理论性能和鲁棒性。

Method: 提出了一种基于双重线性规划（Dual Linear Program）对偶解的预测学习增强算法框架。该方法利用对偶解相较于原始解在实例扰动下更稳定的特性，将学习到的预测信息整合进度量任务系统（Metrical Task Systems）和层级集合覆盖（Laminar Set Cover）问题的在线决策中。

Result: 算法在理论分析上为两类在线最小化问题提供了改进的性能保证；实验结果验证了该方法在 k-server 问题和停车许可问题中的有效性。

Conclusion: 该研究首次证明了利用对偶预测能有效提升在线最小化问题的算法性能，通过对偶解的稳定性解决了学习增强算法中预测值不稳定的挑战，为处理更广泛的在线优化问题提供了新范式。

Abstract: We present learning-augmented algorithms for two general classes of online minimization problems: metrical task systems and laminar set cover. Both algorithms achieve improved theoretical guarantees using machine-learned predictions of an optimal solution to the dual linear program. Unlike optimal primal solutions, which can change drastically under tiny instance perturbations, these dual solutions are much more stable, which ensures the existence of good (and learnable) predictions for families of similar instances. While previous work has used dual predictions in offline settings and for online maximization problems, our algorithms are, to the best of our knowledge, the first demonstration that such dual predictions can be effective for online minimization. Our theoretical results are complemented by experiments on the $k$-server problem and the parking permit problem.

</details>


### [332] [Multi-Objective Submodular Maximization with Differential Privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05596)
*Ting Hou,Yanhao Wang,Yiping Wang,Cen Chen,Minghao Zhao,Fan Dang*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文首次研究了满足差分隐私（DP）约束的多目标次模最大化（MOSM）问题，提出了两种结合DP机制的近似算法，并通过实验证明了其在实际应用中的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的次模最大化研究主要集中在单目标场景，尽管多目标次模最大化（MOSM）和差分隐私（DP）单目标次模最大化已有研究，但尚无针对受基数约束且满足差分隐私的多目标次模最大化问题的研究。

Method: 提出了两种新颖算法：一是基于经典贪婪算法进行扩展的隐私算法；二是利用截断技术（truncation technique）并结合DP机制的算法。

Result: 通过数值实验（最大覆盖和设施选址问题）验证了所提算法在多目标设置下的有效性和效率，并给出了相应的近似保证。

Conclusion: 研究证明了所提出的算法在满足差分隐私（DP）约束的同时，能够有效解决多目标次模最大化（MOSM）问题，在准确性和隐私保护之间取得了良好的平衡。

Abstract: In this paper, we study multi-objective submodular maximization (MOSM) subject to a cardinality constraint under differential privacy (DP). Specifically, we aim to select a set of at most $k \in \mathbb{Z}_{+}$ elements to maximize the minimum of $d > 1$ monotone submodular functions while satisfying $\varepsilon$-DP. Although extensive studies have been conducted on both differentially private single-objective submodular maximization on sensitive data and non-private MOSM, to the best of our knowledge, there has not yet been any prior work on MOSM with DP. We propose two novel algorithms: the first extends the classic greedy algorithm and the second employs a truncation technique, both of which are integrated with DP mechanisms for privacy protection and achieve approximation guarantees for MOSM. Finally, we conduct numerical experiments on two submodular maximization applications, namely maximum coverage and facility location, in multi-objective settings to validate the efficacy and efficiency of our proposed algorithms.

</details>


### [333] [Detecting Large Quasi-cliques on Dynamic Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05809)
*Luciano Gualà,Simone Pellegrini,Luca Pepè Sciarria,Alessandro Straziota*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文将Pang等人（2024）的准团检测算法扩展至动态网络，通过高效维护顶点评价指标，实现了增量场景下$O(\log \Delta)$的更新复杂度，并在保持高质量结果的同时，大幅优化了全动态网络中的计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检测大型准团在真实网络中具有重要意义，但该问题存在计算复杂性。尽管Pang等人（WWW 2024）提出了基于相似度的静态算法，但如何将其高效扩展至动态网络（支持边插入与删除）仍是一个未解决的挑战。

Method: 1. 基准算法：基于Pang等人框架，支持全动态更新（插入/删除），复杂度为$\widetilde{O}(\Delta)$。
2. 增量式优化：针对仅插入场景，引入动态维护顶点$\gamma$-度（$\gamma$-degree）的新技术，将更新时间优化至$O(\log \Delta)$。
3. 全动态优化：扩展上述技术以支持删除，在准团规模和密度略有损失的情况下，换取显著的性能提升。

Result: 1. 增量式算法相比基准算法实现了最高207倍的速度提升。
2. 全动态算法在保持解质量的前提下，实现了最高21倍的速度提升。
3. 理论分析验证了算法的有效性，实验证明了在真实数据集上的卓越性能。

Conclusion: 该研究成功地将静态准团（quasi-clique）检测算法扩展至动态图场景，通过引入动态维护顶点$\gamma$-度的技术，实现了高效的增量式更新（$O(\log \Delta)$时间复杂度）和全动态更新，在保证解质量的前提下大幅提升了计算速度。

Abstract: Motivated by the problem of detecting large and cohesive groups of vertices in real networks, the task of finding large \emph{quasi-cliques} has attracted considerable attention across different research areas. From a computational complexity perspective, strong inapproximability results are known for this problem, yet several heuristics have been proposed to identify large quasi-cliques in real-world networks. Recently, [Pang \emph{et al.}, (WWW 2024)] introduced a similarity-based approach that represents the current state of the art. In this work, we extend that approach to \emph{dynamic} networks, thereby addressing an open problem posed by [Pang \emph{et al.}, (WWW 2024)].
  We first present a Baseline fully dynamic algorithm where edges of the network can be both inserted and deleted. The algorithm exactly maintains the same quasi-clique returned by the algorithm by Pang et al. on the current graph, with update time $\widetilde{O}(Δ)$, where $Δ$ is the maximum degree. We then focus on the practically relevant incremental case, where only edge insertions are allowed, and design an algorithm with $O(\log Δ)$ update time. This method leverages a novel technique for dynamically maintaining accurate estimates of vertex $γ$-degrees, a core component of framework by Pang et al., and achieves up to $207\times$ speed-up over the Baseline while preserving comparable solution quality. Finally, we extend the approach to the fully dynamic setting, supporting both insertions and deletions, obtaining up to $21\times$ speed-up with limited and acceptable loss in quasi-clique size and density. We provide a formal analysis of our algorithms and validate them through an extensive set of experiments on real-world datasets.

</details>


### [334] [Workload-Aware Autotuning of Block Size in Square-Root Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06145)
*Ruize Zhao*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 研究表明，针对算法工程中的分块大小选择问题，使用机器学习模型进行自动调优可以显著优于传统的固定 $\sqrt{n}$ 启发式方法，从而实现更好的性能表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的平方根分解分块大小（$B=\sqrt{n}$）虽然在渐进分析上合理，但在实际应用中并非实现性能最优的最快选择，且缺乏对不同工作负载和硬件平台的自适应能力。

Method: 提出了一种基于机器学习（特别是KNN-9模型）的块大小自动调优方法。通过重复分组交叉验证学习工作负载模型，并引入置信度门限（confidence gate）以减少预测导致的性能回退风险。同时评估了不同特征提取方式对模型性能的影响。

Result: KNN-9模型将平均遗憾（mean regret）从 1.2882 降低至 1.0646，实现了 1.151 倍的几何平均加速比；带置信度门限的模型在保持性能增益的同时有效减小了极端性能回退；实验证明模型学习到的是工作负载统计规律而非简单的标签记忆。

Conclusion: 固定分块大小（$\sqrt{n}$）并非最优选择，基于负载特征的自动调优技术能显著提升性能。通过机器学习模型预测最优块大小可以超越传统启发式方法，且该方法具有跨工作负载的泛化能力。

Abstract: The textbook choice B=sqrt(n) for square-root decomposition is asymptotically natural, but it is not always the fastest implementation choice. We study block-size autotuning as a reproducible algorithm-engineering problem and show that a learned workload model can improve over fixed sqrt(n) on the tested implementation. Under repeated grouped cross-validation, the best policy is a full-feature KNN-9 model that reduces mean regret from 1.2882 to 1.0646 and yields a paired geometric-mean speedup of 1.151x. A confidence gate retains most of that gain while reducing slowdowns. A family-free full-observation follow-up remains better than fixed blocking, which suggests that the model is learning from workload statistics rather than memorizing labels. In contrast, short-prefix variants do not produce a successful low-overhead online tuner in the current prototype. External validation is selective but supportive: Zipf-Hotspot is the strongest out-of-distribution case, and a six-window Baleen follow-up still improves over fixed blocking. Overall, block-size choice is workload aware and platform aware, and the fixed sqrt(n) rule leaves substantial performance on the table.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [335] [SET: Stream-Event-Triggered Scheduling for Efficient CUDA Graph Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05495)
*Zhengxiong Li,Tsung-Wei Huang,Umit Ogras*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对任务并行流水线的CUDA运行时框架，通过优化任务调度和流水线管理，显著降低了同步开销并提升了GPU硬件利用率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的CUDA程序受限于宿主到设备的同步延迟、内核调度开销以及硬件资源（计算单元与复制引擎）利用率不足。

Method: 提出了一个基于任务并行流水线的CUDA运行时框架：(1)采用基于事件链(event-chaining)与工作窃取(work-stealing)的多流编程模型；(2)引入基于图的执行流与每流缓冲区(per-stream buffers)，确保内存安全性与任务并发。

Result: 在典型工作负载下，相较于现有的CUDA Graph基准，实现了1.15-1.44倍的性能加速，并降低了18%-54%的调度开销。

Conclusion: 该框架通过多流任务并行流水线模型和图执行流，有效解决了CUDA中存在的通信与调度瓶颈，提升了GPU的硬件利用率与系统吞吐量。

Abstract: Achieving peak GPU performance remains a significant challenge as the system throughput is constrained by host-device synchronization delays and kernel scheduling overheads, even with aggressive kernel optimizations and batch processing. Furthermore, existing approaches often underutilize hardware resources such as compute cores and copy engines due to scheduling overheads. To address these problems, we propose a CUDA runtime framework for task-parallel pipelines to minimize the synchronization overheads and the gap between kernel executions. The proposed solution combines two innovations: (1) a multi-stream task-parallel pipeline programming model that leverages event-chaining and work-stealing mechanisms to fully utilize available hardware resources; (2) a graph-based execution flow with per-stream buffers to ensure memory safety for multiple in-flight jobs running concurrently. Extensive evaluations on representative real-world workloads show 1.15--1.44X speedup and reduce scheduling overheads by 18--54% compared to state-of-the-art CUDA graph baselines.

</details>


### [336] [PoCQ: Proof of Contribution Quality as a Lightweight Blockchain Consensus for Secure Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05642)
*Sudad Abed,Nasser Sabar,Abdun Mahmood,Mohammad Jabed Morshed Chowdhury*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种去中心化联邦学习的共识框架 PoCQ，利用声誉机制和轻量级验证技术，在保证模型抗攻击鲁棒性的前提下，显著降低了验证开销并提升了准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 去中心化联邦学习（FL）脱离了中心化协调节点，通过区块链实现去中心化，但仍面临模型中毒攻击、验证结果不可靠以及验证开销过大的挑战。

Method: 提出了一种基于区块链的共识框架 PoCQ，包含：1. 基于加密承诺和轻量级范数（norm-based）的验证机制；2. 基于历史贡献质量的声誉驱动共识算法；3. 仅存储紧凑审计元数据的机制以保障可扩展性。

Result: 在中毒场景的实验中，PoCQ 在挑战性的医疗数据集（非 IID 环境下）准确率提升 34.1%，全局平均准确率提升 11%，且平均验证时间显著降低 21.27%。

Conclusion: PoCQ 通过声誉感知验证与聚合机制，有效解决了去中心化联邦学习中安全性不足与验证开销高的问题，在保障鲁棒性的同时提升了计算效率，是实现安全高效的去中心化联邦学习的可行方案。

Abstract: Decentralized Federated Learning (FL) removes reliance on centralized coordinators but remains vulnerable to model poisoning, unreliable validation, and high validation overhead. This paper introduces Proof of Contribution Quality (PoCQ), a blockchain-based consensus framework designed to secure decentralized FL through reputation-aware validation and aggregation. PoCQ evaluates client updates using cryptographic commitments and lightweight norm-based validation, enabling efficient detection of malicious contributions while limiting validation cost. A reputation-driven consensus mechanism dynamically adjusts the influence of participants based on their historical contribution quality, while the blockchain stores only compact audit metadata to preserve scalability. Extensive experiments under poisoning scenarios across three benchmark datasets demonstrate that PoCQ outperforms the strongest state-of-the-art methods, achieving accuracy gains of 34.1% on challenging medical datasets in highly non-iid settings and an 11% improvement in global average accuracy. In addition, PoCQ reduces validation time by 21.27% on average per round, highlighting its effectiveness in jointly enhancing robustness and efficiency for fully decentralized federated learning.

</details>


### [337] [IN2P3 Computing Center 2024 Workload Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05914)
*Guillaume Cochard,Bertrand Simon*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文发布了2024年IN2P3计算中心的超大规模真实作业数据集（44M条目），包含详细的执行指标与内存使用数据，为算法调度研究提供了高保真的季节性与周期性行为研究基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了解决现有计算资源调度研究中数据集普遍存在的时间跨度较短、缺乏内存使用数据或仅依赖合成数据的问题，旨在提供一个更具多样性、时效性和高保真的真实工作负载基准。

Method: 收集并整理了2024年全年IN2P3计算中心的作业特性及执行数据，涵盖约4400万个作业、1000名用户，并在包含内存使用率及多维度时空特征的基础上进行了数据集标准化处理。

Result: 构建了一个包含4400万作业记录的超大规模数据集，覆盖了312台机器、4.6万个并发线程及105TB内存资源的集群环境，通过扩展时间维度实现了对用户复杂行为模式的有效覆盖。

Conclusion: 本数据集提供了一个涵盖全年度的长周期真实工作负载，极大地提升了调度算法模拟与评估的可靠性与现实相关性，特别是在捕捉用户行为的季节性与周期性模式方面具有显著优势。

Abstract: This paper provides and analyzes a dataset detailing the characteristics and execution data of all jobs submitted to the IN2P3 Computing Center (Villeurbanne, France), a national research and support unit of the CNRS, in 2024. The main additional value of this contribution compared to previously available datasets consists in the combination of an extended time interval considered, the inclusion of memory usage data and its recency, on top on improving the diversity of datasets provenance. This allows researchers to simulate and evaluate scheduling algorithms on a real workload over a large time window. Thus, specificities due to seasonal, monthly, and weekly user behaviors can be taken into account, which is not possible with smaller or synthetic datasets. It is composed of 44M jobs submitted by 1k users running on a cluster of a maximum of 312 machines supporting 46k concurrent threads and providing 105To of RAM.

</details>


### [338] [Beyond Greedy Chunking: SLO-Aware Sliding-Window Scheduling for LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05933)
*Yuansheng Chen,Yue Zhang,Xuan Mo,Weigang Wu,Jialun Li*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: SlidingServe 是一种针对 LLM 在线推理的 SLO 感知调度系统，通过滑动窗口机制和动态规划策略，在显著提高系统吞吐量的同时，有效保障了请求的延迟服务等级目标（SLO）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 推理调度系统因输出约束粒度较粗，难以应对多请求资源竞争，导致资源利用率低且难以实现细粒度的服务质量（QoS）差异化管理。

Method: 提出滑动窗口驱动的调度系统 SlidingServe，包含四个核心组件：1) 轻量级批处理延迟预测器；2) SlidingChunker（动态分块技术）；3) 多级优先级排序器（平衡公平与效率）；4) BatchConstructor（基于动态规划的选批以规避 SLO 违例）。

Result: 在多种负载条件下，SlidingServe 将服务容量提升了高达 30%，并在重负载模式下将 SLO 违例率降低了 16%-53%。

Conclusion: SlidingServe 通过结合滑动窗口机制、轻量级延迟预测、多级优先级排序及动态规划选批策略，在满足 LLM 推理严格 SLO 要求的同时，显著提升了系统吞吐量及服务能力。

Abstract: With the rapid growth of interactive applications in large language model (LLM) online services, maintaining high system throughput while ensuring user-perceived latency has become a key issue in inference scheduling. Existing LLM service systems rely on coarse-grained output constraints, making it difficult to effectively handle resource contention among multiple requests, resulting in low resource utilization efficiency and limited support for fine-grained quality of service (QoS) differentiation. We present SlidingServe, a sliding-window-driven SLO-Aware scheduling system for online LLM inference. SlidingServe designed a lightweight batch latency predictor to estimate the execution time of a batch. Based on this, SlidingServe uses SlidingChunker to combine information from the current iteration and the next iteration to achieve dynamic chunking and improve the overall system throughput while maintaining strict QoS guarantees. SlidingServe introduces Multi-Level Priority Sorter to sort candidate requests in order to balance fairness and efficiency. Additionally, when multiple requests within the same batch are at risk of SLO violating,SlidingServe introduces BatchConstructor, which uses dynamic programming to select the set of requests to execute in the current round, mitigating the SLO violation risk of critical requests.Our evaluation demonstrates that SlidingServe can improve service capacity by up to 30% compared to advanced scheduling systems under various load conditions, and further reduces the rate of SLO violation by 16%-53% under heavy-load inference mode.

</details>


### [339] [Demystifying NVSHMEM: A System-Level Analysis on Symmetric Memory and Device-Initiated Operations in GPU Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05951)
*Yijun Ma,Siyuan Shen,Tiancheng Chen,Akhil Langer,Jiri Kraus,Benjamin Glick,Craig Belusar,Jeff Hammond,Torsten Hoefler*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文全面综述了NVIDIA NVSHMEM通信库的编程模型与实现机制，分析了其通过对称内存实现高性能单边通信的能力，并通过案例验证了其在复杂深度学习负载中的系统级价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管NVSHMEM在GPU集群中应用日益广泛，但目前缺乏对其系统级设计、行为机制及底层权衡的统一综述，导致开发者和研究人员难以全面理解其工作原理。

Method: 对NVSHMEM的编程模型、底层实现及性能特征进行了系统性梳理；重点分析了对称内存、单边操作及设备端集合通信；以DeepEP在稀疏深度学习负载中的应用为例进行了案例研究。

Result: 明确了NVSHMEM作为高性能通信基石的定位，揭示了其在细粒度、GPU驱动通信方面的优势，并指出了当前设计中的权衡关键点及潜在改进空间。

Conclusion: NVSHMEM是构建大规模高性能GPU集群系统的关键组件，其基于对称内存的细粒度GPU驱动通信模型对于逼近硬件通信性能极限至关重要，同时也为未来通信运行时的优化指明了方向。

Abstract: NVSHMEM is NVIDIA's OpenSHMEM-based PGAS communication library for GPU clusters, enabling GPU-initiated, one-sided communication through symmetric memory. Despite its growing adoption, a system-level understanding of its design and behavior remains scattered across documentation, source code, and application experience. This paper presents a concise study of NVSHMEM's programming model, implementation, and performance characteristics, focusing on symmetric memory, one-sided operations, and device-side collectives. We also examine DeepEP as a case study of NVSHMEM in performance-critical sparse deep learning workloads. Our analysis shows that NVSHMEM pioneered a device-side symmetric-memory programming model that enables fine-grained GPU-driven communication and is important for approaching the hardware performance limit. Overall, this work defines NVSHMEM's role as a systems building block, highlights its design tradeoffs, and identifies opportunities for improving GPU communication runtimes.

</details>


### [340] [LLM-Based Porting of Optimized C++ to CUDA Through Deoptimization and Reoptimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06063)
*Daichi Mukunoki,Ryo Mikasa,Shunichiro Hayashi,Tetsuya Hoshino,Takahiro Katagiri*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种先简化再重新优化（Deopt-Reopt）的流程，以纠正 CPU 代码特性对 LLM 翻译 CUDA 代码的干扰。实验表明，该方法虽能提升复杂核函数的翻译质量，但其效果随任务和模型而异，并非万能方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将高性能计算（HPC）代码从 CPU 移植到 GPU 时，原有的 CPU 导向优化会阻碍大模型（LLM）准确生成高质量的 CUDA 代码，导致翻译效率与性能下降。

Method: 设计并评估了 Deopt-Reopt 工作流：首先简化（Deopt）原始C++代码，随后将其翻译并重优化（Reopt）为CUDA代码。通过对比“直接翻译”（Direct）策略，在 12 个 HPC 核函数上使用两种大模型（O120 和 Q235）进行“单次”（Single-shot）和“迭代”（Iterative）实验。

Result: 1. 单次实验中，Deopt-Reopt 在部分案例（如 conv2d）中表现优异，但在其他案例中并不占优；即使在控制 LLM 调用次数后，优势也不明显。2. 迭代实验中，重复生成和修复缩小了不同方法间的差距，但 Q235 模型在使用 Deopt-Reopt 时在核心核函数（conv2d, ddgemm, bgemm）上仍具显著优势。3. 对代码可行性（编译成功率）影响混合：在某些难编译场景下大幅提高，而在其他场景下则有所下降。

Conclusion: “去优化-重优化”（Deopt-Reopt）工作流是辅助大模型进行GPU迁移的有效手段，但其收益并非普适，而是高度依赖于具体核函数、所选大模型、搜索预算以及成功率。该方法并非端到端的保证方案，而是条件性的技术提升。

Abstract: When porting high-performance computing (HPC) code from CPU to GPU, CPU-oriented optimizations may obstruct LLM-based CUDA translation. We design and evaluate a Deopt-Reopt workflow that first simplifies the input C++ code and then retranslates and reoptimizes it for CUDA, comparing it against direct translation (Direct) on twelve HPC kernels with two LLMs (gpt-oss-120b (O120) and qwen-3-235b-a22b-instruct-2507 (Q235)) in Single-shot (one pass) and Iterative (repeated refinement) settings. In Single-shot, among 18 testable cases Deopt-Reopt was significantly faster among successful trials (after BH-FDR correction) in five - most clearly for conv2d, where CPU- and GPU-oriented designs diverge - but Direct was faster in three, so removing CPU-specific optimizations is not universally beneficial. An exploratory Direct-3 control that equalizes the LLM-call count left Deopt-Reopt ahead in only four of nineteen testable cases, with Direct-3 ahead in four others. In Iterative, repeated generation and repair narrow the mode gap - markedly so for O120 - while Q235 retains large Deopt-Reopt advantages on conv2d, ddgemm, and bgemm. Deopt-Reopt's effect on feasibility is also mixed - sharply higher for some kernels Direct rarely compiles, lower for others. Because performance is conditioned on successful trials, the benefit is conditional rather than a guaranteed end-to-end gain. Overall, Deopt-Reopt is an effective but non-universal technique for LLM-based GPU porting, with gains that depend on the kernel, the model, the search budget, and the success rate.

</details>


### [341] [Discrete Incremental Voting: New Bounds for General Graphs and Expanders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06381)
*Petra Berenbrink,Colin Cooper,Thorsten Götte,Lukas Hintze,Tomasz Radzik*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本研究分析了离散增量投票过程（DIV）的收敛速度，给出了基于图电导率和度分布的通用时间界限，并证明了其在正则图上的收敛性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了深入理解Cooper等人引入的离散增量投票过程（DIV）的演化特性、收敛速度及其对网络拓扑结构的依赖关系。

Method: 利用概率分析、柯西-施瓦茨不等式及随机过程理论，推导了DIV过程的收敛时间上界，并结合谱图理论分析了正则图上的收敛行为。

Result: 1. 给出了DIV过程在一般图上的期望收敛时间上界为 $O\left(\frac{n(K\log(Kn)+\gamma(G)n)}{\Phi(G)^2}\right)$；2. 证明了在特定的正则图条件下，DIV过程以高概率（w.h.p.）收敛至初始意见的加权平均值。

Conclusion: 该论文提供了离散增量投票过程（DIV）收敛时间的严谨界限，表明收敛时间与图的电导率、度分布及初始意见差异紧密相关，并证明了其在特定条件下收敛至初始平均意见。

Abstract: We analyze the discrete incremental voting process (DIV) introduced by Cooper, Radzik, and Shiraga [OPODIS '23]. In this process, we consider a set $V$ of $n$ nodes connected in an undirected graph $G = (V, E)$ where each node has an integer opinion. In one step a randomly selected node interacts with its randomly selected neighbor and changes its opinion by $1$ in the direction of the neighbour's opinion. The process converges to a unique opinion that, in expectation, is the degree-weighted average of the initial opinions.
  We show that if the graph has conductance $Φ(G)$, the ratio of the average to smallest degree is $γ(G)$, and the maximal difference between initial opinions is $K$, then the expected convergence time is ${O}\left({n\left(K\log (Kn)+γ(G) n \right)}/{Φ(G)^2}\right)$. This bound is essentially optimal for a large class of graphs of bounded expansion. We also show that for regular graphs, if the second largest eigenvalue is $o(1/\log^2 n)$ and $K$ is $o\left({n}/{\log^2 n}\right)$, then w.h.p.\ DIV converges to the initial average opinion (rounded up or down).

</details>


### [342] [On GPU Implementation for Multi-Precision Integer Division](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06386)
*Martin B. Marchioro,Aske N. Raahauge,Marc I. Løvenskjold,Cosmin E. Oancea,Stephen M. Watt*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在通用GPU上实现大整数快速除法的方法，通过牛顿迭代法改进了$2^{15}$至$2^{18}$位精度的计算，实现了接近最优的性能，弥补了现有大整数计算库的功能不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的GPU大整数运算库（如CGBN）不支持某些特定范围内（$2^{15}$至$2^{18}$位）的大整数除法，且在通用GPU上实现高效的大整数除法算法存在实现复杂度和效率挑战。

Method: 1. 基于移位逆运算（shifted inverse）的牛顿迭代法，全程保持在整数域运算；
2. 利用数据并行算子（data-parallel operators）；
3. 构建基于乘法次数的成本模型，并结合前缀和技术及多精度乘法优化算法；
4. 在CUDA平台上进行高效实现。

Result: 成功实现了针对$2^{15}$至$2^{18}$位大整数的高效GPU/CUDA除法算法，实验数据表明其性能表现非常接近所提出的成本模型下的理论最优值。

Conclusion: 在特定的GPU架构下，针对$2^{15}$至$2^{18}$位的大整数除法，可以通过基于牛顿迭代法的数据并行实现达到接近理论最优的性能，填补了现有库（如CGBN）在特定位宽支持上的空白。

Abstract: This paper presents the issues arising in implementing a fast integer division algorithm on general purpose GPUs. The algorithm uses a Newton iteration based on the shifted inverse operation, keeping all arithmetic in the integer domain and relying on data-parallel operators. The principal contribution is an efficient GPU/CUDA implementation for integer precisions from $2^{15}$ to $2^{18}$ -- sizes not supported by \cgbn{} division. We propose algorithmic refinements, define a cost model in terms of multiplications, build on prefix sums and previous work on multi-precision multiplication, and present an evaluation showing near-optimal performance relative to the model for the target precision.

</details>


### [343] [CarbonSim: A Lifecycle-Aware Framework for Evaluating Carbon Tradeoffs in Hardware Upgrade Decisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06438)
*Kartik Hans,Kaiwen Zhao,Stephen Lee*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了 CarbonSim 全生命周期仿真框架，通过综合考量硬件生产制造排碳（隐含碳）与运行能效，揭示了盲目进行硬件升级并非总是环保的最优解，并强调了在硬件全生命周期视角下进行碳优化的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然硬件升级能提升能效，但其生产过程中的隐含碳（Embodied Carbon）往往会被忽视。在轻负载工作负载或低碳电力环境下，过早更换硬件可能导致总碳足迹增加，而非减少。

Method: 开发了名为 CarbonSim 的全生命周期仿真框架，集成工作负载特征、机器功率模型、隐含碳清单、调度策略及随时间变化的电网碳强度数据，支持多种隐含碳核算策略（如均匀摊销和前期分配）。

Result: 通过异构CPU平台的校准实验证明，新机器并未在所有场景下实现最低总碳排放。CarbonSim 能够量化不同更新策略下的碳排放权衡，证明了延长硬件使用寿命在特定条件下具有明显的环保优势。

Conclusion: 硬件升级决策不应仅基于能效，而必须采取“工作负载感知”、“位置感知”和“全生命周期感知”的策略；在低负载或碳排放电力较清洁的地区，延长现有硬件寿命往往比更换新硬件更能减少碳排放。

Abstract: As the demand for information and communication technologies (ICT) continues to rise, the environmental impact of computing systems is becoming an increasingly critical concern. Although newer hardware often improves performance and energy efficiency, these gains do not always offset the carbon cost of premature replacement, particularly under low-utilization workloads or low-carbon electricity grids. We present CarbonSim, a lifecycle-aware simulation framework for evaluating carbon tradeoffs in hardware upgrade decisions. CarbonSim combines workload execution profiles, machine-level power characteristics, embodied carbon inventories, scheduling policies, and time-varying grid carbon intensity to estimate total emissions under alternative deployment scenarios. The framework supports multiple embodied-carbon accounting strategies, including uniform amortization and front-loaded lifecycle attribution, enabling analysis under different hardware lifespan assumptions. Using heterogeneous CPU generations as calibration platforms, we demonstrate that newer machines do not always minimize total emissions: under lightly loaded workloads or cleaner electricity mixes, extending the useful life of existing hardware can reduce lifecycle carbon despite lower operational efficiency. These results highlight that hardware refresh decisions should be workload-aware, location-aware, and lifecycle-aware.

</details>
